<section class="category">
<h1>Intro</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-vande-ham-shri-guroh-shri-yuta-pada-kamalam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vande ’haṁ śrī-guroḥ śrī-yuta-pada-kamalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gurūn vaiṣṇavāṁś cha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpaṁ sāgrajātaṁ saha-gaṇa-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;raghunāthānvitaṁ taṁ sa-jīvam</strong><br>
<strong>sādvaitaṁ sāvadhūtaṁ parijana-sahitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-devaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-pādān saha-gaṇa-lalitā-śrī-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśākhānvitāṁś cha</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">vande</i> — bow; <i class="wbw__term">haṁ</i> — I; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — revered; <i class="wbw__term">guroḥ</i> — teacher's; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — glory; <i class="wbw__term">yuta</i> — adorned; <i class="wbw__term">pada</i> — feet; <i class="wbw__term">kamalaṁ</i> — lotuses; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — revered; <i class="wbw__term">gurūn</i> — teachers; <i class="wbw__term">vaiṣṇavāṁś</i> — Vaiṣṇavas; <i class="wbw__term">cha</i> — and; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — Śrī; <i class="wbw__term">rūpaṁ</i> — Rūpa (Goswāmī); <i class="wbw__term">sāgrajātaṁ</i> — with elder brother (Sanātana Goswāmī); <i class="wbw__term">saha</i> — together with; <i class="wbw__term">gaṇa</i> — associates; <i class="wbw__term">raghunāthānvitaṁ</i> — with Raghunātha (Goswāmī); <i class="wbw__term">taṁ</i> — him; <i class="wbw__term">sa</i> — with; <i class="wbw__term">jīvam</i> — Jīva (Goswāmī); <i class="wbw__term">sādvaitaṁ</i> — with Advaita (Āchārya); <i class="wbw__term">sāvadhūtaṁ</i> — with Avadhūta (Nityānanda); <i class="wbw__term">parijana</i> — associates; <i class="wbw__term">sahitaṁ</i> — accompanying; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇa</i> — Kṛṣṇa; <i class="wbw__term">chaitanya</i> — Chaitanya (Mahāprabhu); <i class="wbw__term">devaṁ</i> — Lord; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — Śrī; <i class="wbw__term">rādhā</i> — Rādhā's; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇa</i> — Kṛṣṇa's; <i class="wbw__term">pādān</i> — feet; <i class="wbw__term">saha</i> — together with; <i class="wbw__term">gaṇa</i> — associates; <i class="wbw__term">lalitā</i> — Lalitā; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — (with) Śrī; <i class="wbw__term">viśākhānvitāṁś</i> — Viśākhā; <i class="wbw__term">cha</i> — also.</p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Krishnadās Kavirāj prays, “First I offer my respects unto the lotus feet of my initiating spiritual masters and the recruiting spiritual masters and advanced Vaiṣṇavas. Next I offer my obeisances to my śāstra gurus, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, his elder brother, Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, and Śrīla Jīva Goswāmī. On a higher platform I offer my respects unto Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, who came with His paraphernalia and associates, along with those great personalities Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu and Śrī Advaita Āchārya. And in the highest stage, I offer my obeisances unto the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Govinda, and all of the gopīs, headed by Lalitā devī and Viśākhā devī.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā</strong><br>
<strong>chakṣur unmilitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">oṁ</i> — (oṁ); <i class="wbw__term">ajñāna</i> — ignorance; <i class="wbw__term">timirāndhasya</i> — darkness-blinded; <i class="wbw__term">jñānāñjana</i> — knowledge eye-salve; <i class="wbw__term">śalākayā</i> — (applied with) applicator; <i class="wbw__term">chakṣur</i> — eye; <i class="wbw__term">unmilitaṁ</i> — opened (became); <i class="wbw__term">yena</i> — by whom; <i class="wbw__term">tasmai</i> — to him; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — revered; <i class="wbw__term">gurave</i> — teacher; <i class="wbw__term">namaḥ</i> — I bow.</p>
<p class="translation">Applying the soothing salve of sambandha-jñāna, a proper acquaintance with the environment, my spiritual master has opened my inner eye and thereby rescued me from the darkness of ignorance, fulfilling my life’s aspirations. I offer my respects unto Śrī Gurudeva.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-acharya-varyam-gaura-dhama-nishtham" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Rañjan Madhusūdana Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>āchārya-varyaṃ gaura-dhāma-niṣṭhaṃ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣaka-śrīdhara-deva-preṣṭham</strong><br>
<strong>govindānuraktaṃ śrī-madhusūdanaṃ</strong><br>
<strong>namāmi nityaṃ śrī-bhakti-rañjanam</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">āchārya-varyaṃ</i>–the best of preceptors; <i class="wbw__term">gaura-dhāma-niṣṭhaṃ</i>–one who has firm faith in the abode of Śrī Gaura; <i class="wbw__term">śrī-bhakti-rakṣaka-śrīdhara-deva</i>–Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣaka Śrīdhara Deva Goswāmī’s; <i class="wbw__term">preṣṭham</i>–dear one; <i class="wbw__term">govindānuraktaṃ</i>–one who has <i>anurāga</i>, intense loving attachment, for Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Deva Goswāmī’s or Lord Śrī Govinda; <i class="wbw__term">namāmi nityaṃ śrī-madhusūdanaṃ śrī-bhakti-rañjanam</i>–I eternally bow down to Śrī Bhakti Rañjan Madhusūdana Mahārāj.</p>
<p class="translation">With firm faith in Śrī Gaura Dhāma (the abode of Śrī Gaura) he is very dear to Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣaka Śrīdhara Deva Goswāmī Mahārāj, and is the best of preceptors. He is full of intense loving attachment to the service of Govinda (Lord Śrī Govinda and Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Deva Goswāmī Mahārāj … Govinda, Govinda!). I eternally and repeatedly bow down to Śrīla Bhakti Rañjan Madhusūdana Mahārāj.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vande-vaikuntha-vritti-stham" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Bimal Avadhūt Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vande vaikuṇṭha-vṛtti-stham</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-karuṇāspadam</strong><br>
<strong>vimalam avadhūtākhyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govindāṅghari-niṣevayā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I offer my obeisances to Śrīla Bhakti Bimal Avadhūt Mahārāja, the embodiment of Nitāi-Gaura’s compassion. His vast intelligence dwells in endless ways (<i>vaikuṇṭha vṛtti</i>) to serve and distribute the highest mercy. An unconventional devotee (<i>bhaktāvadhūta</i>) he is pure (<i>vimal</i>) as he possesses exclusive dedication to the service of the holy name lotus feet of Govinda (Krishna) and his Guru Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-goswaminam-sudhirakhyam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Sudhīr Goswāmī Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>goswāminaṁ sudhīrākhyam</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda-preṣṭham āśraye</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-gāthā-rataṁ nityaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrīdhara-pāda-sevanam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I take shelter in Śrīla Bhakti Sudhīr Goswāmī Mahārāja, Govinda Krishna’s favourite and Śrīla Govinda Mahārāja’s favourite, as he is addicted to always singing the glories of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu, having taken eternal shelter in service to the lotus feet of Śrīla Śrīdhar Mahārāja (Śrī Rādhā’s other self).”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-pujya-sri-guru-varga-vandita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrila Bhakti Nirmal Āchārya Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūjya śrī-guru-varga-vandita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mahā-bhāvānvitāyāḥ sadā</strong><br>
<strong>paurvā paryya paramparā prachalita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prājya pramūrttā kṛteḥ</strong><br>
<strong>bhakte nirmala-nirjharasya nibhṛtaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁrakṣakaṁ sādaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande śrī-gurudevam ānata-śirā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchāryya-varyyaṁ nijam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">"I bow my head in eternal obeisance to my Gurudev, the best of Acharyas, Shrila Bhakti Nirmal Acharya Maharaj. He is the ever vigilant, stalwart guardian of the current of pure devotion whose highest form flows from our most worshippable Shri Rupanuga Guru-varga in their exclusive dedication to Mahabhava, Shrimati Radharani."</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>prerakaṁ prāchya-pāścātya-śiṣyāṇāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-vartmani</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-nirmalam-ācārya-svāminaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praṇamāmy aham</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances to Swami Bhakti Nirmal Acharya, who is energetically inspiring all of the Eastern and Western disciples on the path of pure devotion.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gurvabhishta-supurakam-guru-ganair" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurvābhīṣṭa-supūrakaṁ guru-gaṇair</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśīṣa-saṁbhūṣitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>chintyāchintya-samasta-veda-nipuṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-rūpa-panthānugam</strong><br>
<strong>govindābhidham ujjvalaṁ vara-tanuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakty anvitaṁ sundaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande viśva-guruñ cha divya-bhagavat-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premṇo hi bīja-pradam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“His Guru’s most cherished wishes, he’s truly fulfilling,<br />Adorned with his guru-varga’s affectionate blessing.<br />In knowledge both conceivable and inconceivable,<br />His expertise illumines the ontological whole.<br />He’s the foremost follower of Śrī Rūpa’s divine aim,<br />Śrī Bhakti Sundar Govinda is his celebrated name.<br />His beautiful figure stands out, clean, clear and effulgent,<br />Devotionally endowed with transcendent sentiment.<br />I worship him, the world-wide guru from high above,<br />The empowered bestower of the seed of Divine Love.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-devam-divya-tanum-suchanda-vadanam-balarka-chelanchitam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>devaṁ divya-tanuṁ suchanda-vadanaṁ-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bālārka-chelāñchitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sāndrānanda-puraṁ sad-eka-varanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vairāgya-vidyāmbudhim</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-siddhānta-nidhiṁ subhakti-lasitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sārasvatānām varaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande taṁ śubhadaṁ mad-eka-śaraṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nyāsīśvaraṁ śrīdharam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I fall at the feet of Śrīla Śrīdhar-Deva,<br />who with charming expression sings his songs of sweet nectar.<br />Dressed with fine garments like sun newly-risen,<br />he’s the true saint the devotees have chosen.<br />His detachment and knowledge are just like an ocean,<br />the treasure-house of complete, perfect conclusion.<br />Radiant in Pastimes of loving devotion,<br />the abode of pure ecstasy, bestower of good fortune;<br />foremost true follower of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta,<br />foremost great general of the whole renounced order,<br />my lord and my master, my only shelter,<br />I worship his feet, Śrīla Śrīdhar-Deva.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-siddhanta-sarasvatiti-vidito" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Bhagavān Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>

<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yo gaura-saṅkīrtanaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvāda-timiṅgilodara-gatān</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uddhṛtya jīvanimān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-prema-sudhābdhi-gāhana-sukhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prādāt prabhuṁ taṁ bhaje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“In the great Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava teachers’ line,<br />as Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī he’s renowned.<br />Like the radiant sun in the morning sky,<br />he appeared to rescue all souls swallowed by<br />the all-devouring impersonal philosophy.<br />By spreading the teachings of Lord Gaurāṅga<br />to sing the Holy Name of Lord Śrī Krishna,<br />he gave all the chance to dive in the ocean <br />of love of Śrī Krishna, the Supreme Person.<br />Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta, my lord, divine master —<br />at his feet do I pray to serve him forever.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namo-gaurakishoraya-bhaktavadhuta-murtaye" data-translated="1">

<section class="song" id="song-vande-bhaktivinodam-shri-gaura-shiakti-svarupakam" data-translated="1">

<section class="song" id="song-gaura-vrajashritashieshair-vaishnavair-vandya-vigraham" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Jagannāth Dās Bābājī Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>

<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇavair vandya-vigraham</strong><br>
<strong>jagannātha-prabhuṁ vande</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premābdhiṁ vṛddha-vaiṣṇavam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“The great venerable Grandsire of pure devotion,<br />loved by all Vaiṣṇavas of both Nabadwīp and Vṛndāvan —<br />I worship the feet of that great Vaiṣṇava Guru,<br />the ocean of love, Śrīla Jagannāth Prabhu.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vancha-kalpatarubhyas-cha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>vāñchā-kalpatarubhyas’ cha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vāñchā-kalpa-tarubhyas’ cha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā-sindhubhya eva cha</strong><br>
<strong>patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">vāñchā</i> — wish; <i class="wbw__term">kalpa</i> — fulfilling; <i class="wbw__term">tarubhyas</i> — trees; <i class="wbw__term">cha</i> — and; <i class="wbw__term">kṛpā</i> — mercy; <i class="wbw__term">sindhubhya</i> — oceans; <i class="wbw__term">eva</i> — indeed; <i class="wbw__term">cha</i> — and; <i class="wbw__term">patitānāṁ</i> — fallen; <i class="wbw__term">pāvanebhyo</i> — deliverers; <i class="wbw__term">vaiṣṇavebhyo</i> — Vaiṣṇavas; <i class="wbw__term">namo</i> — obeisance; <i class="wbw__term">namaḥ</i> — obeisance.</p>
<p class="translation">I bow down unto the holy feet of the pure Vaiṣṇavas, who are like desire trees able to fulfil all the devotees’ aspirations. The Lord’s devotees are comparable to a vast ocean of causeless mercy, as they deliver the fallen souls from material existence. I offer all respects unto them.<br>
<i>(Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>pañcha-tattvātmakaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-rūpa-svarūpakam</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktāvatāraṁ bhaktākhyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;namāmi bhakta-śaktikam</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">pañcha</i> — five; <i class="wbw__term">tattvātmakaṁ</i> — truth-natured; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇaṁ</i> — Kṛṣṇa (Śrī Gaurāṅgadeva); <i class="wbw__term">bhakta</i> — devotee; <i class="wbw__term">rūpa</i> — form; <i class="wbw__term">svarūpakam</i> — own form (Śrī Nityānanda); <i class="wbw__term">bhaktāvatāraṁ</i> — devotee-incarnation (Śrī Advaita Prabhu); <i class="wbw__term">bhaktākhyaṁ</i> — known as devotee (Śrīvāsa Paṇḍita); <i class="wbw__term">namāmi</i> — I bow; <i class="wbw__term">bhakta</i> — devotee; <i class="wbw__term">śaktikam</i> — potency (Śrī Gadādhara).</p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisances unto Lord Krishna in His fivefold aspect known as the pañcha-tattva: I offer my respects unto Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who appears as a devotee of the Lord relishing His own internal mellows. Furthermore I bow down before Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, who is the self-same form and elder brother of Śrī Gaurāṅga. Next, I pay homage unto that great personality Śrī Advaita Prabhu, who is an incarnation of the Lord’s devotee, and unto the Lord’s eternal associates like Śrīvās Paṇḍit, who serve the Lord in loving devotional relationships. Finally, I bow down at the feet of those devotees who hold the actual potency of unalloyed dedication in their hands: Śrī Rāmānanda Rāya, Śrī Gadādhara Paṇḍit, and Śrī Swarūp Dāmodar.<br>
<i>(Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>namo mahā-vadānyāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-prema-pradāya te</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇāya kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāmne gaura-tviṣe namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">namo</i> — obeisance (to); <i class="wbw__term">mahā</i> — great; <i class="wbw__term">vadānyāya</i> — magnanimous; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇa</i> — Kṛṣṇa; <i class="wbw__term">prema</i> — love; <i class="wbw__term">pradāya</i> — granting; <i class="wbw__term">te</i> — to You; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇāya</i> — to Kṛṣṇa; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇa</i> — (who is) Kṛṣṇa; <i class="wbw__term">chaitanya</i> — Chaitanya; <i class="wbw__term">nāmne</i> — by name; <i class="wbw__term">gaura</i> — (with) golden; <i class="wbw__term">tviṣe</i> — radiance; <i class="wbw__term">namaḥ</i> — obeisance.</p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisances unto Lord Gaurāṅga, whose divine complexion is a radiant golden hue and who possesses the qualities of the most benevolent incarnation. His spiritual Pastimes freely grant pure love for Lord Krishna to all. He is none other than Lord Krishna known in the age of Kali as Śrī Krishna Chaitanya.<br>
<i>(Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayatam-suratau-pangor" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī-Śrī-Guru-Gaurāṅga-Rādhā-Madana-Mohanjīu <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Kolkata, Sree Chaitanya Sāraswata Krishnanushilana Sangha</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayatāṁ suratau paṅgor</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mama manda-mater gatī</strong><br>
<strong>mat-sarvasva-padāmbhojau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-madana-mohanau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohan, I am a helpless cripple and my meagre intelligence is absorbed in the vile material sense objects. Kindly let deep devotional attachment arise for the exclusive wealth of my life which is Your lotus feet. May Your Lordships, who are so compassionate and affectionate, be forever victorious!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-divyad-vrindaranya-kalpa-drumadhah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī-Śrī-Guru-Gaurāṅga-Gāndharvā-Govindasundarjīu <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Nabadwīp, Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīvyad-vṛndāraṇya-kalpa-drumādhaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrīmad-ratnāgāra-siṁhāsana-sthau</strong><br>
<strong>śrī gāndharvā-śrīla-govinda-devau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preṣṭhālībhiḥ sevyamānau smarāmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I constantly meditate upon that Divine Couple Śrī Śrī Gandharvā-Govinda, who are seated upon a wonderful throne highly decorated with brilliant jewels. They are sitting within the illustrious forest of Vraja, beneath a mind-attracting desire tree, accompanied by Their dedicated servitors such as Śrī Lalitā Devī, Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī and other intimate servants like the <i>priyanarma sakhīs</i>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shriman-rasa-rasarambhi" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī-Śrī-Guru-Gaurāṅga-Rādhā-Gopīnāthjīu <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Hāpāniyā, Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Ashram</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmān rāsa-rasārambhī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-vaṭa-taṭa-sthitaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>karṣan veṇu-svanair gopīr</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopīnāthaḥ śriye ’stu naḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gopīnāth, who instigates the exchange of devotional mellows, is standing upon the banks of the Yamunā River at the special place known as Vaṁśīvaṭa. There, the sweet tune of His flute is naturally attracting the hearts of the cowherd maidens of Vraja Dhām. May that charming Lord bring us within His auspicious embrace.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vrindayai-tulasi-devyai" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>vṛndāyai tulasī-devyai <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāyai tulasī-devyai</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;priyāyai keśavasya cha</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-bhakti-prade devi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;satyavatyai namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I eternally offer my respects unto Śrī Vṛndādevī, Śrīmatī Tulasī Mahārāṇī, who is the dearmost object of Lord Keśava’s affection. I bow down before Satyavatī who can bestow pure dedication unto Lord Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>atha natvā mantra-gurūn</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gurūn bhāgavatārthadān</strong><br>
<strong>vyāsān jagat-gurūn natvā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tato jayam udīrayet</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then, let me bow down before all my spiritual masters: the guru who gave me the divine mantra, as well as all those who have taught me the meaning of Śrīmad Bhāgavatam. Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the spiritual master of the entire universe and the giver of the all-conquering conclusions of Śrīmad Bhāgavatam.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>jayaḥ sa-parikara śrī-śrī-guru-gaurāṅga-gāndharvā-govindasundar-pādapadmānāṁ jayastu!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Let the holy lotus feet of Śrī Gurudeva, Śrīman Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Śrī Gāndharvā-Govindasundar, along with Their eternal associates, be victorious over all!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sakala-vaishnava-pade-mora-namaskara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Prayers to the Vaiṣṇavas <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala vaiṣṇava-pade mora namaskāra</strong><br>
<strong>ithe kichu aparādha nahuka āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>hoiyachen hoiben prabhur jato bhakta jan</strong><br>
<strong>vandanā kori’ āmi sabāra charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I respectfully bow to the lotus feet of all the Vaiṣṇavas, praying that there is no offence in my attempt to please them. To all Vaiṣṇavas who have been, and all Vaiṣṇavas who shall be, I offer my obeisances to their lotus feet.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namah-om-vishnupadaya-krishna" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Praṇām Mantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>to Śrīla A.C Bhaktivedānta Swāmī Mahārāj Prabhupād</h3>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>namaḥ om viṣṇupādaya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale</strong><br>
<strong>swāmī śrī bhaktivedānta prabhupādāya te namaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>gurvajñaṁ śīrasi-dhṛtvā śaktyāveśa sva-rūpine</strong><br>
<strong>hare-kṛṣṇeti mantreṇa pāśchatya-prāchya-tāriṇe</strong><br>
<strong>viśvāchārya prabaryāya divya kāruṇya mūrtaye</strong><br>
<strong>śrī bhāgavata-mādhurya-gīta-jñāna pradāyine</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-śrī-rūpa-siddhānta-sarasvatī niṣevine</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-padāmbhoja-bhṛṅgāya gurave namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my humble obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedānta Swāmī Prabhupāda, who is Krishna’s beloved associate and who came down to this plane from Goloka. Taking the order of his guru on his head, he is the <i>śaktyāveśa</i> (empowered) <i>avatār</i> of Nityānanda Prabhu personified. He distributed the Hare Krishna mantra all over the Eastern and Western world, delivering and uplifting all fallen souls. He is the best of millions of <i>jagat-gurus</i>, because he is the personification of divine mercy. He has distributed the sweet nectar of <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i> and the transcendental knowledge of <i>Bhagavad-gītā</i> all over the world. He is constantly engaged in exclusive devotional service to Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākura, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, and Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu. I offer my humble obeisances unto Śrīla Prabhupāda, who is like a bumblebee always tasting the nectar of the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Govinda.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shrimach-chaitanya-sarasvata-mathavara-udgita-kirtir" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>The Glory of the Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla B.R. Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj while residing in a hut on the banks of the Ganges in 1941.</h4>
<h4>Rendered into English by Śrīpād B.A. Sāgar Mahārāj from the Bengali translation by Śrīla B.S. Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj.</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmach-chaitanya-sārasvata-maṭhavara-udgīta-kīrtir jaya-śrīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bibhrat sambhāti gaṅgā-taṭa-nikaṭa-navadvīpa-kolādri-rāje</strong><br>
<strong>yatra śrī-gaura-sārasvata-mata-niratā-gaura-gāthā gṛṇanti</strong><br>
<strong>nityaṁ rūpānuga śrī-kṛtamati-guru-gaurāṅga-rādhā-jitāśā</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="translation">In the Holy Abode of transcendental touchstone,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Śrī Nabadwīp Dham—the selfsame Śrī Vṛndāvan,<br />in the land of Koladwīp, whose inner identity<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;is the King of mountains Śrī Govardhan,<br />near the charming shores of the holy River Bhāgīrathī<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;who delivers all the fallen souls,<br />stands that grand and glorious King of all Temples —<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="translation">In that beautiful retreat, the surrendered devotees<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;whose lives are the message of Śrī Gaura,<br />are eternally absorbed in the loving service<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of the Divine Master, the Golden Lord Gaurāṅga,<br />and the Loving Lord Śrī Govindasundar<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with His consort Śrīmatī Gāndharvā,<br />accompanied by all Their beloved associates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="translation">With hearts always filled with good hope<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to attain the grace of the Lord,<br />following faithfully in the line<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of Śrī Śrī Rūpa and Raghunāth —<br />the storekeepers of the unlimited treasure<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of pure, sweet love divine —<br />those surrendered souls ever sing the unending glories<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of the transcendental name and qualities<br />of the most magnanimous, original Supreme Lord,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Śrī Śrī Gaurāṅgasundar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="translation">Indeed the hearts of all beings, moving and still,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;marvel in the glory of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh<br />as they take shelter in the soothing, affectionate shade<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of the victory flag that flies aloft<br />and sings wholeheartedly to the world the ever-expanding<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;renown of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh;<br />bearing that banner of all transcendental benedictions,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh ever shines resplendent<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in all its divine magnificence.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-sa-parikara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Jaya Dhvani <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>“Kī Jaya!” Chants</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya sa-parikara śrī-śrī-guru-gaurāṅga-rādhā-govindasundar giridhārījīu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda paramahaṁsa-parivrājakāchārya-varya aṣṭottara-śata-śrī śrīmad bhakti sundar govinda dev-gosvāmī mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda paramahaṁsa-parivrājakāchārya-varya aṣṭottara-śata-śrī śrīmad bhakti rakṣak śrīdhar dev-gosvāmī mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda paramahaṁsa-parivrājakācārya-varya aṣṭottara-śata-śrī śrīmad bhakti siddhānta sarasvatī ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda śrīla gaura-kiśora dās bābājī mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda śrīla sach-chid-ānanda bhaktivinoda ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya oṁ viṣṇupāda śrīla jagannāth dās bābājī mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī rūpānuga guru-varga kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>namāchārya śrīla haridās ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ananta-koṭī vaiṣṇava-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī baladeva vidyābhūṣaṇa kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrīla viśvanāth chakravartī prabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī narottama-śyāmānanda-śrīnivāsa-āchārya-prabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrīla vṛndāvan dās ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrīla kṛṣṇadās kavirāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>saparṣada śrī nityānanda prabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>saparṣada śrīman mahāprabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>jaya śrī-śrī rādhā-kṛṣṇa gopa-gopī-gopīnāth śyāma-kuṇḍa rādhā-kuṇḍa giri-govardhan kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī lakṣmī-varāhadeva kī jaya! (In our Nabadwip Maṭh)</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śuddha bhakti vighna vināśāya śrī nṛsiṁhadeva kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-pravara prahlād mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>tulasī mahārāṇī kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>gaṅgā-yamunājī kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>grantharāja śrīmad-bhāgavatam chaitanya-charitāmṛta kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>viśva-vaiṣṇava rāja sabhā kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ākara maṭha-rāja śrī chaitanya maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>tadīya śākha-maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī chaitanya sāraswat maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>viśva-byāpī tadīya śākha-maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrīdhām māyāpur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrīdhām nabadwīp kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvan dhām kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>puruṣottam dhām kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>baladeva-subhadrā-jagannāthjī kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī govinda kuṇḍa kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śrī sadāśiva gaṅgadharajīu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>samaveta vaiṣṇava-maṇḍala kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>samaveta bhakta-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>harināma-saṅkīrtana kī jaya!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>nitāi-gaura-premānande haribol!</strong></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-nityananda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Pañcha-tattva mantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are ten offences in the chanting of the Hare Krishna <i>mahāmantra</i>, but these are not considered in the chanting of the Pañcha-tattva <i>mantra</i>. Śri Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is the most magnanimous incarnation, for He does not consider the offences of the fallen souls. Thus to derive the full benefit of the chanting of the <i>mahāmantra</i>, we must first take shelter of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu and his associates, the Pañcha-tattva, who are the bestowers of the Hare Krishna <i>mahāmantra</i>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hare-krishna-hare-krishna-krishna-krishna-hare-hare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Hare Krishna mahāmantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Illumination by Śrīla Śrīdhar Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the Name of Rāma within the Hare Krishna mantra, the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas will find Rādhā-ramaṇa Rāma. That means, “Krishna, who gives pleasure (<i>ramaṇ</i>) to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.” In our conception, the Hare Krishna mantra is wholesale Krishna consciousness, not Rāma  (Lord  Rāmachandra)  consciousness.  Śrī  Chaitanya’s highest conception of things is always <i>svayaṁ bhagavān</i>, Krishna-<i>līlā</i>, Rādhā-Govinda-<i>līlā</i>. That is the real purpose of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu’s advent and teachings.<br>
In that consideration, the Hare Krishna mantra does not mention the Rāma-<i>līlā</i> of Āyodhya at all. There is no connection with that in the highest conception of the Hare Krishna mantra.  The inner conception of the mantra is responsible for our spiritual attainment. The inner conception of the devotee will guide them to their destination.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-nama-mahamantra-sarva-mantra-sara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Daśa-vidha Nāmāparādha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Ten Offences to the Holy Name</h3>
<h4>Bengali poem composed by</h4>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāma mahāmantra sarva-mantra-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>jādera karuṇābale jagate prachāra</strong><br>
<strong>sei nāma-parāyaṇ sādhu, mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>tāhādera nindā nā koriho kadāchana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hare Krishna Mahāmantra — of all mantras the best,<br />Throughout the world is preached by saints’ potent grace behest;<br />Such saints to the Name devoted, such pure souls great:<br />Never dare offend them — never show them hate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendranandana kṛṣṇa sarveśvareśvara</strong><br>
<strong>maheśvara ādi tāra sevana-tatpara</strong><br>
<strong>nāma chintāmaṇi kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-svarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>bheda-jñāna nā koribe līlā-guṇa-rūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Krishna, son of Nanda, of all lords the Leader —<br />The great Shiva and all the gods serve His feet forever;<br />The touchstone of the Name is Krishna incarnate —<br />His Pastimes, nature, form, also think not separate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘‘guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hon śāstrera pramāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā kore bhāgyavāne’’</strong><br>
<strong>se gurute martya-buddhi avajñādi tyaji</strong><br>
<strong>iṣṭa-lābha kara, nirantara nāma bhaji</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Guru’s a form of Krishna — the Scriptures corroborate;<br />In the form of Guru, Krishna blesses the fortunate.”<br />Never offend that Guru by thinking him mere mortal;<br />Gain your highest objective — serve the Name for time eternal.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti, śruti-mātā-saha sātvata-purāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nāma-charaṇa-padma kore nīrājana</strong><br>
<strong>sei śruti śāstra jebā karaye nindana</strong><br>
<strong>se aparadhīra saṅga koribe varjana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Vedas with Mother Gāyatrī and Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam<br />Illuminate the lotus feet of Śrī Hari’s Name;<br />Whoever vilifies those Holy Vedic Scriptures —<br />Never keep their company, know them as offenders.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmera mahimā sarva-śāstrete vākhāne</strong><br>
<strong>atistuti, heno kabhu nā bhāviha mane</strong><br>
<strong>agastya, ananta, brahmā, śivādi satata</strong><br>
<strong>je nāma-mahimā-gāthā saṅkīrtana-rata</strong><br>
<strong>se nāma-mahimā-sindhu ke pāibe pāra</strong><br>
<strong>atistuti bole jei—sei durāchāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The glories of the Name — all Scriptures’ exaltation;<br />Dare not think their praise to be exaggeration.<br />Agastya, Ananta, Brahmā, Shiva, etc., ever<br />Sing the glories of that Name with full-hearted fervour.<br />Who can cross the ocean of the glories of that Name?<br />Whoever says ‘exaggeration’ have their sin to blame.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāmāboli nitya golokera dhana</strong><br>
<strong>kalpita, prākṛta, bhāve—aparādhi-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Names of Krishna — eternal wealth of Goloka:<br />Who thinks those Names imaginary, mundane — he’s offender.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāme sarva-pāpa-kṣaya sarva-śāstre kaya</strong><br>
<strong>sārā-dina pāpa kori sei bharasāya—</strong><br>
<strong>emata durbuddhi jāra sei aparādhi</strong><br>
<strong>māyā-pravañchita, duḥkha bhuñje niravadhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All Scriptures claim the Name all sin it can destroy,<br />But those who spend their time in sin making it a ploy —<br />Such a wicked attitude is that of an offender<br />Deceived by illusion, perpetually to suffer.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atulya śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma pūrṇa-rasa-nidhi</strong><br>
<strong>tāra sama nā bhāviha śubha-karma ādi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Incomparable Name of Krishna — the treasure of ecstasy:<br />Never dare compare it with auspicious piety.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāme śraddhā-hina-jana—vidhātā vāñchita</strong><br>
<strong>tāre nāma dāne aparādha suniśchita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who’re faithless t’ward the Name — deceived by Providence: <br />Giving them the Holy Name surely’s an offence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuniyāo kṛṣṇa-nāma-māhātmya apāra</strong><br>
<strong>je priti-rahita, sei narādhama chāra</strong><br>
<strong>allaṅtā mamatā jāra antare bāhire</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha kṛṣṇa-nāma tāra kabhu nāhi sphure</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Despite them hearing the infinite glories of Krishna’s Name,<br />Those whose hearts don’t melt in love are rascals of ill fame;<br />Only pride and avarice their thoughts and deeds do yield —<br />The Pure Name of Krishna to them is ne’er revealed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei daśa aparādha koriyā varjana</strong><br>
<strong>jena jana kore harināma saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>apūrva śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema labhya tāre hoy</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-prabhu tāra hṛde nitya vilasay</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Casting off these ten offences, leaving no exception,<br />Those pure souls who chant the Name in Holy Congregation —<br />The miracle of love for Krishna they will surely savour,<br />The Name Divine Himself will shine within their hearts forever.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nija-pati-bhuja-danda-chchhatra-bhavam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Govardhana-vāsa-prārthanā-daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>A Dwelling Near Śrī Govardhan</h3>
<h4>Ten prayers for a dwelling near Śrī Govardhan composed by Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī Prabhu.</h4>
<h4>The preferred prayer to Śrī Govardhan of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-pati-bhuja-daṇḍa-chchhatra-bhāvaṁ prapadya</strong><br>
<strong>pratihata-mada-dhṛṣṭoddaṇḍa-devendra-garva</strong><br>
<strong>atula-pṛthula-śaila-śreṇi-bhūpa priyaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />You became the umbrella atop the handle of the arm of your Lord and crushed the pride of proud, arrogant, impudent Indra. You are the incomparable king of all great mountains. Please give me a dear dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pramada-madana-līlāḥ kandare kandare te</strong><br>
<strong>rachayati nava-yūnor dvandvam asminn amandam</strong><br>
<strong>iti kila kalanārthaṁ lagnakas tad-dvayor me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />The Divine Couple liberally enact Pastimes of mad love in your caves. So that I can see Their Pastimes, please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anupama-maṇi-vedī-ratna-siṁhāsanorvī-</strong><br>
<strong>ruha-jhara-dara-sānu-droṇi-saṅgheṣu raṅgaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>saha bala-sakhibhiḥ saṅkhelayan sva-priyaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />You induce your beloved to joyfully play with Balarām and His friends amidst your trees, brooks, caves, plateaus, valleys, and incomparable jeweled terraces, which resemble jeweled thrones. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rasa-nidhi-nava-yūnoḥ sākṣiṇīṁ dāna-keler</strong><br>
<strong>dyuti-parimala-viddhāṁ śyāma-vedīṁ prakāśya</strong><br>
<strong>rasika-vara-kulānāṁ modam āsphālayan me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />You provide the dark, lustrous, fragrant terrace that witnesses the fare Pastime of the Young Couple, who are an ocean of rasa, and you delight the best amongst those who relish of rasa. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-dayitam apūrvaṁ rādhikā-kuṇḍam ātma-</strong><br>
<strong>priya-sakham iha kaṇṭhe narmaṇāliṅgya guptaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>nava-yuva-yuga-khelās tatra paśyan raho me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />Playfully embracing around the neck your dear friend Rādhā Kuṇḍa, the incomparable favourite of the Lord, you hide in a secluded place beside her and watch the Pastimes of the Young Couple. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthala-jala-tala-śaṣpair bhūruhach-chhāyayā cha</strong><br>
<strong>prati-padam anukālaṁ hanta samvardhayan gāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>tri-jagati nija-gotraṁ sārthakaṁ khyāpayan me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />You always fully nourish the cows everywhere with your land, water, plains, and grass, and the shade of your trees, and thus proclaim the fulfilment of your name throughout the three worlds. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sura-pati-kṛta-dīrgha-drohato goṣṭha-rakṣāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tava nava-gṛha-rūpasyāntare kurvataiva</strong><br>
<strong>agha-baka-ripuṇochchhair datta-māna drutaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />You are highly honoured by Śrī Krishna, the enemy of Agha and Baka, who protected His community from the onslaught of Indra beneath your form as their new home. Please quickly give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>giri-nṛpa hari-dāsa-śreṇi-varyeti nāmā-</strong><br>
<strong>mṛtam idam uditaṁ śrī-rādhikā-vaktra-chandrāt</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-nava-tilakatve kḷpta vedaiḥ sphuṭaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />O king of mountains! When the nectar of your name “<i>Hari-dāsa-varya</i>: the best of the Lord’s servants” came forth from the moon-like face of Śrī Rādhikā, you were clearly established by the Vedas as the new tilak of Vraja. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-jana-yuta-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-maitrī-rasākta-</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-nara-paśu-pakṣi-vrāta-saukhyaika-dātaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>agaṇita-karuṇatvān mām urīkṛtya tāntaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />You alone give happiness to the people, animals, and birds of Vraja, who are filled with the mood of friendship towards Rādhā, Krishna, and Their companions. You are immeasurably merciful. So, please accept me, a wearied soul, and give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nirupadhi-karuṇena śrī-śachī-nandanena</strong><br>
<strong>tvayi kapaṭi-śaṭho ’pi tvat-priyeṇārpito ’smi</strong><br>
<strong>iti khalu mama yogyāyogyatāṁ tām agṛhṇan</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Govardhan!<br />Although I am insincere and deceitful, I have been given to you by Your dear Śrī Śachī Nandan, who is unconditionally merciful. So, please do not judge whether I am qualified or unqualified, and give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rasada-daśakam asya śrīla-govardhanasya</strong><br>
<strong>kṣiti-dhara-kula-bhartur yaḥ prayatnād adhīte</strong><br>
<strong>sa sapadi sukhade ’smin vāsam āsādya sākṣāch</strong><br>
<strong>chhubhada-yugala-sevā-ratnam āpnoti tūrṇam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who carefully studies these ten nectarean verses describing the king of mountains, Śrī Govardhan, will soon attain a dwelling at this joyous place and quickly attain the precious jewel of personal service to the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Morning Songs</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-girirajer-arati-vishiala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa Gupta-Govardhan Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya girirājer ārati viśāla</strong><br>
<strong>śrī gaura-maṇḍala-mājhe bhuvana-maṅgala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories, to the grand ārati of Śrī Girirāj! In the midst of Śrī Gaura Maṇḍal, it is benefitting the entire universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koladvīpe śobhe gupta-govardhanarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>vyakta hoilā śrī govinda kuṇḍe aparūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The hidden Govardhan graces Koladwīp with its beauty. Now it has manifested its exquisite beauty in Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mālatī-mādhavī-kuñja kandare kandare</strong><br>
<strong>nigūḍha-nikuñja-līlā hari-manohare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are groves of <i>mālatī</i> and <i>mādhavī</i> flowers in its various caves. In these groves, Lord Hari plays His hidden, mysterious, and enchanting Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva kuṇḍera śobhā jena sudhābhāṇḍa</strong><br>
<strong>surendra-surabhī-sevā-saubhāgya-mārtaṇḍa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The incomparable beauty of the Kuṇḍa is like a reservoir of honey. Even Lord Indra and the divine Surabhī cow are rendering their service under the shining sun of good fortune.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī bhakti-rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong><br>
<strong>prakāśilā tava sevā dīpta manohara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj has manifested the effulgent and enchanting devotional service to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī chaitanya-sārasvata maṭha suśobhana</strong><br>
<strong>yathā nitya sevā kore sārasvata-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh is a beautiful place where devotees of the Maṭh are always rendering their devotional service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gosvāmī śrī-raghunātha dāsere smariyā</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevā kore antarmaṇā hoiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remembering Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, they are continuously serving in an introspective mood.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya gupta-govardhana divya-rasāloy</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-raghunāthānuga-bhaktera āśroy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the hidden Govardhan, which is a reservoir of transcendental rasa. It is the refuge of the devotees who are following in the footsteps of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuliyā-prakāśa tava achintya-mahimā</strong><br>
<strong>divāniśi bhakta-vṛnda kore parikramā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By Your inconceivable glories and magnanimity, You have manifested Kuliyā (where all offences are forgiven). Day and night, the multitudes of devotees are circumambulating this place.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sevya o sevaka-rūpe nitya parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati dekhe e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are eternally manifested both as the worshipable Lord and as the servitor Lord. This fallen servant sees Your ārati with great joy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-girirajer-arotiko-shobha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Girirāj- Govardhan Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya girirājer ārotiko śobhā</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-vraja-maṇḍala mājhe jaga mana lobhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the beautiful ārati of Śrī Girirāj which is going on in Śrī Vraja Maṇḍal, attracting the minds of all living entities of the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pramoda-madana-līla śrī-rādhā-ramaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>yathā nitya-līlā kore loye sakhī-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Śrī Rādha-Ramaṇa [Krishna who gives pleasure to his beloved Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī] is eternally playing His joyful Loving Pastimes there in the association of the <i>Sakhīs</i> of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mālatī-mādhavī-kuñja kandare kandare</strong><br>
<strong>nigūḍha nikuñja-līla hari manohare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Here deep within the groves of <i>mālatī</i> flowers and <i>mādhavī</i> creepers, the confidential Pastimes of the charming Lord Hari are going on.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śyāma-kuṇḍa rādhā-kuṇḍa kusum sarovara</strong><br>
<strong>mānasa gaṅgā dāna-ghāṭī jāra abhyantara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Within this holy place are Śyāma Kuṇḍa, Rādhā Kuṇḍa, Kusum Sarovar, Mānasa Gaṅgā and Dāna-ghāṭī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-govinda-kuṇḍa līlāsthalī nāhi sīmā</strong><br>
<strong>divāniśi-bhakta-vṛnda kore parikramā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa, these holy places of divine Pastimes are without limit. Day and night the devotees are performing circumambulation in worship.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘śrīdhar-svāmī-sevāśram’ parama śobhana</strong><br>
<strong>yathā nitya-sevā kore sārasvata-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Here there is the supremely beautiful Śrīdhar Swāmī Sevāśram where devotees of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh are performing their eternal service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>abhinna śrī kṛṣṇa jñāne bhajibāra tore</strong><br>
<strong>mahāprabhu samarpilā raghunātha kore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīman Mahāprabhu gave Śrī Śrī Girirāj Jīu to Śrī Raghunāth Goswāmī so that Śrī Dās Goswāmī could worship His Lordship knowing Him to be non-different from Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gosvāmī śrī-raghunātha dāsere smariyā</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevā kore antarmanā hoiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The devotees of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh worship Śrī Śrī Girirāj Jīu in a deep mood of devotion in remembrance of Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya giri-govardhana divya-rasāloy</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-sanātana-jīva-raghunāthāśroy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Giri Govardhan which is the abode of divine rasa and the shelter of Śrī Rūpa, Sanātan, Jīva and Raghunāth Goswāmīs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>surendra-munīndra-śiva-śuka-mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>dekhena ārati-śobhā durllabha-darśana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The beauty of the ārati of Śrī Girirāj Jīu which is rare and precious is being seen by Lord Indra, Nārada Muni, Lord Shiva, Śukadeva Goswāmī and all the great personalities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sevya o sevaka-rūpe nitya parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati dekhe e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Śrī Girirāj Jīu is eternally manifested as both the worshipable Lord and the servitor Lord. This fallen servant sees the <i>ārati</i> of Śrī Girirāj Jīu with great ecstasy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-guru-maharaja-karuna-sagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanya-Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>for the Āratis in our Jagannāth Puri Branch Temple</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya guru mahārāja karuṇā-sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>śrī bhakti rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrīla Guru Mahārāj, Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj who is like an ocean of mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prakāśile nīlāchale bhuvana-maṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>nitāi-chaitanya-deva-sevā samujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By his mercy, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanyadeva have manifested in Puri Dhām bestowing auspiciousness upon the whole world and revealing their effulgent devotional service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gorāpreme mātoyārā nityānanda-rūpa</strong><br>
<strong>rasarāja mahābhāva chaitanya-svarūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu’s form is dancing ecstatically intoxicated with divine love for Śrī Gaura, whose manifestation is Rasarāj Śrī Krishna taking the heart and halo of Mahābhāva, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kasita-kāñchana jini śrī-aṅga labaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>du̐hu̐gale vanamālā bhāvera dolanī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Both Lords are standing fully effulgent, glowing just like freshly cut gold, as garlands of various beautiful forest flowers play on their necks.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>murachita koṭikāma rūparāsaraṅge</strong><br>
<strong>madhura nartana-bhāva varābhaya bhaṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Millions and millions of Cupids fall unconscious before the divine feet of Their Lordships, seeing their beauty as their divine forms playfully sway to and fro. They are dancing, touching the hearts of everyone and freely bestowing mercy upon the conditioned souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koṭi-chandra-bhānuśobhā ratna-siṁhāsane</strong><br>
<strong>prema netre dekhe mahā-bhāgyavāna jane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Brilliant like millions of moons They stand effulgent upon Their jewelled throne. The fortunate souls, their devotional eyes full of love and affection can see Their divine forms.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mṛdaṅga mandirā bāje suśaṅkha-dhvanita</strong><br>
<strong>śruti-mauli-ratnamālā-dīpa nirājita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the temple during the <i>ārati</i>, the <i>mṛdaṅgas</i> resound sweetly as conchshells are blown and the ghee lamp is offered. The different parts of the Vedas within bhakti yoga have taken form in each light as a bhakti yoga light and through this form they are worshipping Their Lordships in the ārati.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhuvana-mohana du̐hu̐ rūpera ārati</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-vṛndāraṇya-vāsī dekhe niravadhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This type of <i>ārati</i> is only seen by the residents of Nabadwīp Dhām and the dear associates of Śrī Chaitanya. Anyone who sees it will be extremely charmed by it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī svarūpa rāmānanda rūpa-sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>raghunātha haridāsa gadādhara dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya’s dear associates, Śrī Swarūp Dāmodar, Rāmānanda Rāya, Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Haridās Ṭhākur and Gadādhara Paṇḍit are seeing and performing this <i>ārati</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sārvabhauma gopīnātha jīvānuga-jana</strong><br>
<strong>dekhena ārati-śobha durllabha-darśana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭāchārya, Gopīnāth Āchārya and the followers in the <i>sampradāya</i> of Jīva Goswāmī are all seeing that ārati which is unable to be seen by others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā prakāśe nityānanda-gauranidhi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana-kṣetre milāila vidhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Nityānanda and Śrī Gaura Mahāprabhu have manifested eternally in Nabadwīp Dhām, Nadia. But now Their divine forms have manifested in Puri Dhām, mercifully given by our fortune-maker, Śrī Gurudeva.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avichintya nityānanda-chaitanya-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī guru-prasāde dekhe ei adhama dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>(śrī guru-prasāde dekhe śrī govinda dāsa)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Inconceivably Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanya have manifested and through the mercy of Śrī Gurudeva this fallen soul sees Their ārati and joins within the saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-gurudever-arati-ujjvala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Glorification of Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>by Śrīpād B. A. Paramahaṁsa Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>translated by Devamoyī Devī Dasī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya gurudever ārati ujjvala</strong><br>
<strong>govardhana-pāda-pīthe bhuvana-maṅgala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the effulgent arati of Śrī Gurudeva, which is going on at the foot of Govardhan, benefitting the whole world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī bhakti sundara deva prabhu śiromaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>gosvāmī govinda jaya ānandera khani</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Bhakti Sundar Govinda Goswāmī, who is our supreme master and is like a reservoir of joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ājānu lambita bhuja divya kalevara</strong><br>
<strong>ananta pratibhā bharā divya guṇadhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His graceful long hands touch his knees and his body is beautiful and transcendental. He is an ocean of infinite genius and transcendental qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-kṛṣṇe jāni tava abhinna svarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra tarite ebe śuddha-bhakta-rūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">We consider your real identity to be non-different from Krishna and Gaurāṅga. But now, to deliver this material world, you have assumed the form of a pure devotee.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpānuga-dhārā tumi kara ālokita</strong><br>
<strong>prabhākara sama prabhā bhuvana-vidita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are brightening the line of the followers of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī. Your effulgence, which is comparable to that of the sun, is known throughout the whole world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha bhakti prachārite tomā sama nāi</strong><br>
<strong>akalaṅka indu jena dayāla nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no one like you to preach pure devotion. You are the spotless moon, and you are merciful like Lord Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ullasita viśvavāsī labhe prema-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>ānande nāchiyā gāhe tava guṇagaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As ecstatic people all over the world are attaining the treasure of love of Krishna, they are dancing and singing your glories.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthāpilā āśrama bahu jagata mājhāre</strong><br>
<strong>pāramahaṁsa-dharma-jñāna śikṣāra prachāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To teach the essence of the highest knowledge and to preach it to all, you have established many ashrams all over the world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chintyāchintya veda-jñāne tumi ādhikārī</strong><br>
<strong>sakala saṁśaya chettvā susiddhāntadhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You have mastery over the knowledge of the Vedas, both conceivable and inconceivable. You are the treasurer of bona fide conclusions, which can destroy all doubt.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mahimā gāhe goloka maṇḍale</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-siddha parikare tava līlāsthale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your glories are being sung in the spiritual world of Goloka, the realm of your Pastimes, by your servants who are eternally liberated.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita pāvana tumi dayāra samīra</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-kārye sunipuṇa satya-sugambhīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To deliver the fallen souls, you are like a breeze of mercy. You are expert in all service, and you are established in truth and gravity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apurva lekhanī dhārā pragāḍha pāṇḍitya</strong><br>
<strong>sadā hāsya miṣṭa bhāśī suśila kavitva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your beautiful and unprecedented writing reflects your deep scholarship. You are always smiling and speaking sweet words, which remind one of your soothing poetry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sādhu-saṅge sadānandī sarala vinayī</strong><br>
<strong>sabhā-madhye vaktā śreṣṭha sarvatra vijayī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are always straightforward and humble, and you are always joyful in the association of pure devotees. In a spiritual gathering, you are always the best speaker. In every field of life, you always come out victorious.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīya gagane tumi āchārya-bhāskara</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevāpriya miṣṭa kaṇṭhasvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the sky of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava world, you are a preceptor who is prominent like the sun. You are always fond of service, and your sweet voice inspires others to do service also.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra karuṇā māge trikāla vilāse</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvikā-giridhārī sevā-mātra āśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Past, present, and future—for all eternity, we beg your mercy. Only by your mercy will one receive devotional service to Śrī Śrī Gāndharvikā-Giridhārī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛpā kara ohe prabhu śri-gaura-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>ārati karaye sadā e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my lord, who is the manifestation of the mercy of Śrī Gaurāṅga, please be merciful to me. This insignificant servant of yours is always praying this way while worshipping you.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-guru-maharaja-jati-rajeshvara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru Ārati-stuti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya ‘guru-mahārāja’ jati-rājeśvara</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glory to you, ‘Guru Maharaj,’<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Of sannyāsī kings, the Emperor:<br />The glorious Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Dev-Goswami Śrīdhar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-pāvana-līlā vistāri’ bhuvane</strong><br>
<strong>nistārilā dīna-hīna āpāmara jane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Extending in this world your<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Pastimes as saviour of the fallen,<br />You delivered all the suffering souls,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;including the most forsaken.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra karuṇāghana murati heriyā</strong><br>
<strong>preme bhāgyavāna jīva poḍe murachiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding your holy form<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of concentrated mercy<br />The fortunate souls fall in a swoon<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of divine love’s ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sudīrgha supībya deho divya-bhāvāśray</strong><br>
<strong>divya-jñāna dīpta-netra divya-jyotirmoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your lofty form full of divine emotion<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;is nectar for our eyes;<br />With divine knowledge and shining eyes,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;your effulgence fills the skies.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>suvarṇa-sūraja-kānti aruṇa-vasana</strong><br>
<strong>tilaka, tulasī-mālā, chandana-bhūṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A golden Sun resplendent,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in robes of saffron dressed;<br />Adorned with tilak, Tulasī beads,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and with sandal fragrance blessed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva śrī-aṅga-śobhā kore jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>audārya-unnata-bhāva mādhurya-ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The matchless beauty of your holy form,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dazzling in its brightness;<br />Your benevolence of most noble heart,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in the moonglow of love’s sweetness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>achintya-pratibhā, snigdha, gambhīra, udāra</strong><br>
<strong>jaḍa-jñāna-giri-vajra divya-dīkṣādhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Inconceivable genius, affection,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gravity, magnanimity —<br />A thunderbolt crushing mundane knowledge,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the reservoir bestowing divinity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-saṅkīrtana-rāsa-rasera āśray</strong><br>
<strong>“dayāla nitāi” nāme nitya premamoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the Golden Lord’s Saṅkīrtan Dance,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you revel ecstatically;<br />“Dayāl Nitāi, Dayāl Nitāi!”<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you ever call so lovingly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sāṅgopāṅge gaura-dhāme nitya-parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-govardhane divya-līlāra-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Eternally present in Nabadwīp Dhām<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with your associates, by your sweet will,<br />You enjoy your holy Pastimes<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;on hidden Govardhan Hill.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīya-āchārya-goṣṭhī-gaurava-bhājana</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍīya-siddhāntamaṇi kaṇṭha-vibhuṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You’re the fit recipient of your dignity<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;In the Gauḍīya Āchārya assembly,<br />The jewel of the Gauḍīya-<i>siddhānta</i><br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adorning your chest so nobly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-sarasvatī-sphūrta siddhāntera khani</strong><br>
<strong>āviṣkṛta gāyatrīra artha-chintāmaṇi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You’re the living message of Śrī Gaura,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you’re the mine of perfect conclusions;<br />You revealed the Gāyatrī’s inner purport:<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the gem fulfilling all aspirations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eka-tattva varṇanete nitya-nava-bhāva</strong><br>
<strong>susaṅgati, sāmañjasya, e saba prabhāva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When you speak, one subject alone<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;is revealed in ever-new light;<br />Proper adjustment, and harmony —<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;All these are your spiritual might.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra satīrtha-varga sabe eka-mate</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-sarasvatī-dhārā dekhena tomāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All your dear Godbrothers<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;declare unanimously<br />They see within your Holiness<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the line of Rūpa-Saraswatī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tulasī-mālikā-haste śrī-nāma-grahaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dekhi’ sakalera hoy ‘prabhu’ uddīpana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing you take the Holy Name,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with Tulasī beads in hand,<br />Awakens the thought of Saraswatī Ṭhākur<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in everybody’s mind.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koṭī-chandra-suśītala o pada bharasā</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvā-govinda-līlāmṛta-lābha-āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your feet, cooling like ten million moons —<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;In their service all faith do I place;<br />The nectar of Rādhā-Govinda’s Pastimes<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;one day we may drink, by your grace.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avichintya-bhedābheda-siddhānta-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati stuti kore dīna-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O personification of perfect conclusions<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;inconceivably one, yet different!<br />I sing this prayer at your <i>ārati</i>,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with joy — your humble servant.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kali-kukkura-kadana-jadi-chao-he" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Prabhāti Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-kukkura-kadana jadi chāo he</strong><br>
<strong>kali-yuga-pāvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-bhaya-nāśana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-śachī-nandana gāo he</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">kali</i> — Kali Yuga; <i class="wbw__term">kukkura</i> — dog; <i class="wbw__term">kadana</i> — tame; <i class="wbw__term">jadi</i> — if; <i class="wbw__term">chāo</i> — you want; <i class="wbw__term">he</i> — hey!; <i class="wbw__term">kali</i> — Kali; <i class="wbw__term">yuga</i> — Yuga; <i class="wbw__term">pāvana</i> — purifier; <i class="wbw__term">kali</i> — Kali Yuga; <i class="wbw__term">bhaya</i> — fear; <i class="wbw__term">nāśana</i> — destroyer; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — Śrī; <i class="wbw__term">śachī</i> — Śachī's; <i class="wbw__term">nandana</i> — son; <i class="wbw__term">gāo</i> — chant; <i class="wbw__term">he</i> — hey!</p>
<p class="translation">Oh mind, you are like a miserable dog of <i>Kali-yuga</i>! If you want deliverance, then sing the Holy Name of the son of Śachī, for He is the saviour and destroyer of fear in this dark age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gadādhara-mādana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-era prāṇa-dhana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;advaitera prapūjita gorā</strong><br>
<strong>nimāi viśvambhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrīnivāsa-īśvara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakata-samūha-chita-chorā</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">gadādhara</i> — Gadādhar; <i class="wbw__term">mādana</i> — delight; <i class="wbw__term">nitāi</i> — Nitãi; <i class="wbw__term">era</i> — of; <i class="wbw__term">prāṇa</i> — life; <i class="wbw__term">dhana</i> — wealth; <i class="wbw__term">advaitera</i> — Advaita; <i class="wbw__term">prapūjita</i> — greatly worshipped; <i class="wbw__term">gorā</i> — Gaurāṅga; <i class="wbw__term">nimāi</i> — Nimāi; <i class="wbw__term">viśvambhara</i> — maintainer of the universe; <i class="wbw__term">śrīnivāsa</i> — Śrīvās; <i class="wbw__term">īśvara</i> — Lord; <i class="wbw__term">bhakata</i> — devotees; <i class="wbw__term">samūha</i> — assembly; <i class="wbw__term">chita</i> — heart; <i class="wbw__term">chorā</i> — stealer.</p>
<p class="translation">He is the transcendental cupid of Gadādhara, the life and soul of Nityānanda Prabhu, and the worshipable Lord of Śrī Advaita Āchārya. He is known as Nimai as He was born under a Neem tree; Viśvambhara as He is the sustainer of the universe; He is the master of Śrīvās Ṭhākur, and He steals the hearts of all of the devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-śaśadhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māyāpura-īśvara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-prabartana-śūra</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhī-jana-śikṣaka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nyāsi-kula-nāyaka,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhava rādhā-bhāva-pūra</strong></p>
