<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">nadīyā</i> — Nadia; <i class="wbw__term">śaśadhara</i> — moon; <i class="wbw__term">māyāpura</i> — Māyāpur; <i class="wbw__term">īśvara</i> — Lord; <i class="wbw__term">nāma</i> — Holy Name; <i class="wbw__term">prabartana</i> — inaugurator; <i class="wbw__term">śūra</i> — hero; <i class="wbw__term">gṛhī</i> — householder; <i class="wbw__term">jana</i> — people; <i class="wbw__term">śikṣaka</i> — teacher; <i class="wbw__term">nyāsi</i> — sannyāsī; <i class="wbw__term">kula</i> — group; <i class="wbw__term">nāyaka</i> — leader; <i class="wbw__term">mādhava</i> — Mādhava; <i class="wbw__term">rādhā</i> — Rādhā; <i class="wbw__term">bhāva</i> — mood; <i class="wbw__term">pūra</i> — filled.</p>
<p class="translation">He is the moon of Nadia, the Lord of Māyāpur, and heroic saviour who inaugurated the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna. He is the teacher of all householders and the commander-in-chief of all <i>sannyāsīs</i>. Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is Krishna, filled with the ecstatic heart’s sentiment of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sārvabhauma-śodhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gajapati-tāraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāmānanda-poṣaṇa-vīra</strong><br>
<strong>rūpānanda-vardhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sanātana pālana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haridāsa-modana-dhīra</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">sārvabhauma</i> — Sārvabhauma; <i class="wbw__term">śodhana</i> — purifier; <i class="wbw__term">gajapati</i> — King (of Orissa); <i class="wbw__term">tāraṇa</i> — deliverer; <i class="wbw__term">rāmānanda</i> — Rāmānanda; <i class="wbw__term">poṣaṇa</i> — nourisher; <i class="wbw__term">vīra</i> — hero!; <i class="wbw__term">rūpānanda</i> — Rūpa's joy; <i class="wbw__term">vardhana</i> — increaser; <i class="wbw__term">sanātana</i> — Sanātan; <i class="wbw__term">pālana</i> — sustainer; <i class="wbw__term">haridāsa</i> — Haridās; <i class="wbw__term">modana</i> — delighter; <i class="wbw__term">dhīra</i> — sober.</p>
<p class="translation">He purified Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭāchārya and rescued Pratāparudra Maharaj, the great king of Orissa. He is the hero and protector of Rāmānanda Rāy. He increases the ecstasy of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī and He is the guardian of Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī. He delights the sober Haridās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-rasa-bhāvana duṣṭa-mata-śātana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kapaṭī-vighātana-kāma</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha-bhakta-pālana śuṣka-jñāna-tāḍana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhala bhakti-dūṣaṇa-rāma</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">vraja</i> — Vraja; <i class="wbw__term">rasa</i> — rasa; <i class="wbw__term">bhāvana</i> — cultivation; <i class="wbw__term">duṣṭa</i> — wicked; <i class="wbw__term">mata</i> — doctrine; <i class="wbw__term">śātana</i> — cutter; <i class="wbw__term">kapaṭī</i> — deceit; <i class="wbw__term">vighātana</i> — destroyer; <i class="wbw__term">kāma</i> — desire; <i class="wbw__term">śuddha</i> — pure; <i class="wbw__term">bhakta</i> — devotees; <i class="wbw__term">pālana</i> — maintainer; <i class="wbw__term">śuṣka</i> — dry; <i class="wbw__term">jñāna</i> — knowledge; <i class="wbw__term">tāḍana</i> — dispeller; <i class="wbw__term">chhala</i> — pseudo; <i class="wbw__term">bhakti</i> — devotion; <i class="wbw__term">dūṣaṇa</i> — destroyer; <i class="wbw__term">rāma</i> — Pleasure personified!</p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Krishna Chaitanya is absorbed in the mellows of Vṛndāvan. He removes the filthy thoughts of the mind, destroying lust and deceit. He drives away dry empiric knowledge which obscures the beauty of pure devotion. He is the protector, maintainer and constant guardian of His pure devotees.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jashomati-nandana-vraja-vara-nagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nām-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaśomatī-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-vara nāgara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gokula-rañjana kāna</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-parāṇa-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madana manohara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kālīya-damana-vidhāna</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">jaśomatī</i> — Yaśodā; <i class="wbw__term">nandana</i> — darling son; <i class="wbw__term">vraja</i> — Vraja; <i class="wbw__term">vara</i> — beloved; <i class="wbw__term">nāgara</i> — paramour; <i class="wbw__term">gokula</i> — Gokula; <i class="wbw__term">rañjana</i> — delight; <i class="wbw__term">kāna</i> — Kãna!; <i class="wbw__term">gopī</i> — gopis; <i class="wbw__term">parāṇa</i> — life; <i class="wbw__term">dhana</i> — wealth; <i class="wbw__term">madana</i> — Cupid; <i class="wbw__term">manohara</i> — stealer of the mind; <i class="wbw__term">kālīya</i> — Kaliya Serpant; <i class="wbw__term">damana</i> — subduer; <i class="wbw__term">vidhāna</i> — enacted.</p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Krishna is the beloved son of Mother Yaśodā, the transcendental lover of the land of Vraja and the delight of Gokula. One of His nicknames is Kāna. He is the life and soul of the <i>gopīs</i>. He is the enchanter of Cupid and the conqueror of the Kālīya serpent.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amala hari-nām amiya-vilāsā</strong><br>
<strong>vipina-purandara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navīna nāgara-vara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-badana suvāsā</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">amala</i> — pure; <i class="wbw__term">hari</i> — Hari; <i class="wbw__term">nām</i> — Name; <i class="wbw__term">amiya</i> — nectar eternal; <i class="wbw__term">vilāsā</i> — play; <i class="wbw__term">vipina</i> — forest; <i class="wbw__term">purandara</i> — lord, hero; <i class="wbw__term">navīna</i> — youthful; <i class="wbw__term">nāgara</i> — gallant lover; <i class="wbw__term">vara</i> — the best; <i class="wbw__term">vaṁśī</i> — flute; <i class="wbw__term">badana</i> — playing; <i class="wbw__term">suvāsā</i> — beautifully dressed.</p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Names of Krishna are spotless and full of sweetness and beauty. Krishna is the king of the forest of Vṛndāvan. He is the ever-fresh youth, an excellent dresser, and the best of lovers. He always plays sweet melodies upon His flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-jana-pālana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asura-kula-nāśana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nanda-godhana rākhoyālā</strong><br>
<strong>govinda mādhava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navanīta-taṣkara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sundara nanda-gopālā</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">vraja</i> — Vraja; <i class="wbw__term">jana</i> — people; <i class="wbw__term">pālana</i> — guardian; <i class="wbw__term">asura</i> — demons; <i class="wbw__term">kula</i> — section; <i class="wbw__term">nāśana</i> — destroyer; <i class="wbw__term">nanda</i> — Nanda; <i class="wbw__term">godhana</i> — cows; <i class="wbw__term">rākhoyālā</i> — keeper; <i class="wbw__term">govinda</i> — Govinda; <i class="wbw__term">mādhava</i> — Mādhava; <i class="wbw__term">navanīta</i> — butter; <i class="wbw__term">taṣkara</i> — thief; <i class="wbw__term">sundara</i> — beautiful; <i class="wbw__term">nanda</i> — Nanda; <i class="wbw__term">gopālā</i> — cowherd boy.</p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the guardian of all who live in Vṛndāvan, and the destroyer of demons. He tends the cows and is known as Govinda, for He delights the cows and the senses. He is Mādhava, the husband of the goddess of fortune, He loves to steal butter and He is the beautiful cowherd boy of Nanda Mahārāj.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yamunā-taṭa-chara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-vasana-hara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāsa-rasika kṛpāmoya</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-naṭavara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakati-vinod-āśraya</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">yamunā</i> — Yamunā; <i class="wbw__term">taṭa</i> — banks; <i class="wbw__term">chara</i> — spy; <i class="wbw__term">gopī</i> — gopis; <i class="wbw__term">vasana</i> — garments; <i class="wbw__term">hara</i> — stealer; <i class="wbw__term">rāsa</i> — Rāsa; <i class="wbw__term">rasika</i> — relisher; <i class="wbw__term">kṛpāmoya</i> — merciful one; <i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — Śrī; <i class="wbw__term">rādhā</i> — Rādhā; <i class="wbw__term">vallabha</i> — beloved; <i class="wbw__term">vṛndāvana</i> — Vrndāvan; <i class="wbw__term">naṭavara</i> — supreme dancer; <i class="wbw__term">bhakati</i> — Bhakti; <i class="wbw__term">vinod</i> — Vinod; <i class="wbw__term">āśraya</i> — shelter.</p>
<p class="translation">Wandering on the banks of the Yamunā River, Krishna stole the garments of the <i>gopīs</i> while they were bathing. He enjoys the mellows of the rāsa dance. He is the most merciful, beloved lover of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. He is the great dancer of Vṛndāvan, and the supreme shelter of Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-shiachinandana-sura-muni-vandana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śachīnandana-vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya śachī-nandana sura-muni-vandana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava-bhaya-khaṇḍana jayo he</strong><br>
<strong>jaya hari-kīrtana nartanā vartana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-mala-kartana jayo he</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">jaya</i> — Glories; <i class="wbw__term">śachī</i> — Śachi's; <i class="wbw__term">nandana</i> — son; <i class="wbw__term">sura</i> — gods; <i class="wbw__term">muni</i> — sages; <i class="wbw__term">vandana</i> — worship; <i class="wbw__term">bhava</i> — worldy existence; <i class="wbw__term">bhaya</i> — fear; <i class="wbw__term">khaṇḍana</i> — destroyer; <i class="wbw__term">jayo</i> — Glories; <i class="wbw__term">he</i> — O!; <i class="wbw__term">jaya</i> — Glories; <i class="wbw__term">hari</i> — Hari; <i class="wbw__term">kīrtana</i> — chanting; <i class="wbw__term">nartanā</i> — dancing; <i class="wbw__term">vartana</i> — inaugurator; <i class="wbw__term">kali</i> — Kali Yuga; <i class="wbw__term">mala</i> — contamination; <i class="wbw__term">kartana</i> — severs; <i class="wbw__term">jayo</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">he</i> — O!</p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Krishna Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, the son of Śachī Devī. Great sages and demigods always sing His praise, for by doing so, the fear of repeated birth and death is cut to pieces. All glories to the <i>kīrtan</i> of the Holy Name of Krishna, for when one chants the Holy Name of Krishna and dances in ecstasy, the filth of this age of Kali disappears.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nayana-purandara viśvarūpa snehadhara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvambhara viśvera kalyāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>jaya lakṣmī-viṣṇupriyā viśvambhara priya-hiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya priya kiṅkara īśāna</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">nayana</i> — eyes; <i class="wbw__term">purandara</i> — king; <i class="wbw__term">viśvarūpa</i> — Viśvarūp; <i class="wbw__term">snehadhara</i> — who has affection; <i class="wbw__term">viśvambhara</i> — universe maintainer; <i class="wbw__term">viśvera</i> — universe of; <i class="wbw__term">kalyāṇa</i> — good fortune; <i class="wbw__term">jaya</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">lakṣmī</i> — Lakṣmī Priyā; <i class="wbw__term">viṣṇupriyā</i> — Viṣṇu Priyā; <i class="wbw__term">viśvambhara</i> — Viśvambhar; <i class="wbw__term">priya</i> — dear; <i class="wbw__term">hiyā</i> — heart!; <i class="wbw__term">jaya</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">priya</i> — dear; <i class="wbw__term">kiṅkara</i> — servant; <i class="wbw__term">īśāna</i> — Īśān.</p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya delights all eyes with His charming beauty. He is the most dearly beloved Lord of all and the intimate brother of Viśvarūp. He brings the highest auspiciousness to everyone, for He floods the entire universe with the nectar of love of Godhead and thus saves all living beings. All glories to Lakṣmī Priyā and Viṣṇu Priyā Devī, who are very dear to Śrī Nimāi Paṇḍit. All glories to Their family servant, Īśāna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī sītā-advaita-rāy mālinī-śrīvāsa jay,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jay chandraśekhara āchārya</strong><br>
<strong>jay nityānanda rāy gadādhara jay jay,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya haridās nāmāchārya</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">śrī</i> — Śrī; <i class="wbw__term">sītā</i> — Sītā; <i class="wbw__term">advaita</i> — Advaita; <i class="wbw__term">rāy</i> — Rāy; <i class="wbw__term">mālinī</i> — Mālinī; <i class="wbw__term">śrīvāsa</i> — Śrīvās; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">chandraśekhara</i> — Chandraśekhar; <i class="wbw__term">āchārya</i> — āchārya; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">nityānanda</i> — Nityānanda; <i class="wbw__term">rāy</i> — Rāy; <i class="wbw__term">gadādhara</i> — Gadādhar; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">jaya</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">haridās</i> — Haridās; <i class="wbw__term">nāmāchārya</i> — Holy Name Acharya.</p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Sītā Ṭhākurāṇī and Śrī Advaita Āchārya, all glories to Mālinī-devī and Śrīvās Ṭhākur, and all glories to Āchārya Chandraśekhara. All glories to Śrīla Nityānanda Prabhu, Śrīla Gadādhara Paṇḍit and Śrīla Haridās Ṭhākur, the Nāmāchārya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>murāri mukunda jay premanidhi mahāśaya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya jata prabhu pāriṣad</strong><br>
<strong>vandi savākāra pāya adhamere kṛpā hoy,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti sapārṣada-prabhupād</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">murāri</i> — Murāri; <i class="wbw__term">mukunda</i> — Mukunda; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — All glory; <i class="wbw__term">premanidhi</i> — Premanidhi; <i class="wbw__term">mahāśaya</i> — magnanimous (Pundarika Vidyanidhi); <i class="wbw__term">jaya</i> — Glory; <i class="wbw__term">jata</i> — all; <i class="wbw__term">prabhu</i> — Lord; <i class="wbw__term">pāriṣad</i> — associates; <i class="wbw__term">vandi</i> — I bow; <i class="wbw__term">savākāra</i> — to everone's; <i class="wbw__term">pāya</i> — feet; <i class="wbw__term">adhamere</i> — fallen soul; <i class="wbw__term">kṛpā</i> — mercy; <i class="wbw__term">hoy</i> — happens; <i class="wbw__term">bhakti</i> — devotion; <i class="wbw__term">sapārṣada</i> — with associates; <i class="wbw__term">prabhupād</i> — Śrīla Prabhupāda (Śrīla B.S. Saraswati Thākur).</p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Murāri Gupta, and all glories to Mukunda Datta. All glories to all the other eternal associates of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. By singing the glories of Śrīla Prabhupād and all those great souls who are eternal associates of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, even the lowest of men can obtain the highest treasure of love.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gurudeva-kripa-bindu-diya-kara-ei-dase" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gurudeva <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurudeva!</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-bindu diyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara’ ei dāse,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tṛṇāpekṣā ati dīna</strong><br>
<strong>sakala-sahane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bala diyā kara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-māne spṛhā-hīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Gurudeva, give to this servant just one drop of mercy. I am lower than a blade of grass. Give me all help. Give me strength. Let me be as you are, without desires or aspirations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakale sammān,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;korite śakati,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deho nātha! yathā yatha</strong><br>
<strong>tobe to’ gāibo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;harināma-sukhe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aparādha hobe hoto</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Give me energy so that I can properly give respect and honour to all. Then I shall be able to happily chant the Holy Name, and my offences will go away.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe heno kṛpā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobhiyā e jana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛtārtha hoibe, nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>śakti-buddhi-hīn,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi ati dīn,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara more ātma-sātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my Lord! When will such mercy fall to this person. I am weak, bereft, and devoid of intelligence. Please allow me to be with you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jogyatā-vichāre,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichu nāhi pāi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra karuṇā-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>karuṇā nā hoile,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa nā rākhibo āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If you examine me, you will find no qualities. Your mercy is everything to me. If you are not merciful unto me, I can only weep, and I will not be able to maintain my life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-udilo-aruna-puraba-bhage" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aruṇodoya-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 1</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>udilo aruṇa pūraba-bhāge,</strong><br>
<strong>dvija-maṇi gorā amani jāge,</strong><br>
<strong>bhakata-samūha loiyā sāthe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gelā nagara-brāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just before sunrise, as the eastern sky turned crimson, the jewel of the twice-born, Śrī Gaurahari, awoke. Taking His devotees with Him He began the kīrtan, wandering through the countryside towns and villages.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘tāthai tāthai’ bājalo khol,</strong><br>
<strong>ghana ghana tāhe jhāṅjera rol,</strong><br>
<strong>preme ḍhala ḍhala sonāra aṅga,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe nūpura bāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The mṛdaṅgas went “tāthai tāthai” to the rhyming chiming of the cymbals, as Mahāprabhu’s golden form shivered in ecstasy and His footbells jingled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mukunda mādhava jādava hari,</strong><br>
<strong>bolore bolore vadana bhori’,</strong><br>
<strong>miche nida-vaśe gelo re rāti,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divasa śorīra sāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mahāprabhu would call out to the still sleeping people, “Don’t waste your nights uselessly sleeping and your days decorating your bodies! Cry out the Names Mukunda, Mādhava, Jādava and Hari!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>emona durllabha mānava-deho,</strong><br>
<strong>pāiyā ki kara bhāva nā keho,</strong><br>
<strong>ebe nā bhajile yaśodā-suta,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charame poḍibe lāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“You have attained this rare human form of life. What are you doing? Don’t you care for this gift? If you don’t worship the darling of Mother Yaśodā now, then great sorrow awaits you at the time of death.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>udita tapana hoile asta,</strong><br>
<strong>dina gelo-boli’ hoibe vyasta,</strong><br>
<strong>tobe keno ebe alasa hoy,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā bhaja hṛdoya-rāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“With every rising and setting of the sun, a day passes and is lost. Why then do you remain idle, refusing to serve the Lord of the heart?”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana anitya jānaha sār,</strong><br>
<strong>tāhe nānā-vidha vipada-bhār,</strong><br>
<strong>nāmāśroya kori’ jatane tumi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākaha āpana kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Know for certain that this life is full of various miseries. Take shelter of the Holy Name as your only business.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāma-sudhā koriyā pān</strong><br>
<strong>juḍāo bhakativinoda-prāṇ,</strong><br>
<strong>nāma vinā kichu nāhiko āro,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudda-bhuvana-mājhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Drink the pure nectar of the Holy Name of Krishna, and thus satisfy the soul of Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod. There is nothing but the Name to be had in all the fourteen worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvera kalyāṇa-sādhana-kām,</strong><br>
<strong>jagate āsi’ e madhura nām,</strong><br>
<strong>avidyā-timira-tapana-rūpe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛd-gagane birāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To penetrate the darkness of ignorance and bless all souls within this universe, the beautiful Name of Krishna has arisen like the shining sun.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jiv-jago-jiv-jago-gaurachanda-bole" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aruṇodoya-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 2</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīv jāgo, jīv jāgo, gaurāchā̐da bole</strong><br>
<strong>kota nidrā jāo māyā-piśāchīra kole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga calls, “Wake up, sleeping souls! Wake up, sleeping souls! You have slept so long in the lap of the witch Māyā.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajibo boliyā ese saṁsāra-bhitore</strong><br>
<strong>bhuliyā rohile tumi avidyāra bhore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“You came into this world saying, ‘O my Lord, I will certainly worship You’, but having forgotten this promise, you have remained in great ignorance.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāre loite āmi hoinu avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>āmi vinā bandhu āra ke āche tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I have descended just to save you. Other than Myself, who else is your friend?”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>enechi auṣadhi māyā nāśibāro lāgi’</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma mahā-mantra lao tumi māgi’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I have brought the medicine for destroying the illusion of <i>māyā</i>. Now pray for this Hari-nāma-mahāmantra and take it.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda prabhura-charaṇe poḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>sei hari-nāma-mantra loilo māgiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod fell at the lotus feet of Lord Gaurāṅga, and after begging for the Holy Name he received that <i>mahāmantra</i>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-samsara-davanala-lidha-loka" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gurvāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-</strong><br>
<strong>trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam</strong><br>
<strong>prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who is the ocean of auspicious qualities. Just to deliver all planes of life that are suffering in the forest fire of the material world, he, as the rain-cloud of mercy, melts to shower down the waters of compassion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprabhoḥ kīrtana-nṛtya-gīta-</strong><br>
<strong>vāditra-mādyan-manaso rasena</strong><br>
<strong>romāñcha-kampāśru-taraṅga-bhājo</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, whose heart is maddened in nectarean love for Śrīman Mahāprabhu by the grand congregational chanting of the Holy Name. Dancing, singing, and playing the khol and karatāls,etc.; his body thrills with joy, trembles, and waves of tears flow from his eyes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-vigrahārādhana-nitya-nānā-</strong><br>
<strong>śṛṅgāra-tan-mandira-mārjanādau</strong><br>
<strong>yuktasya bhaktāṁś cha niyuñjato ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who personally engages in many types of worshipful services such as dressing the deity and cleansing the Lord’s holy temple, and similarly engages the devotees (in his retinue).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chatur-vidha-śrī-bhagavat-prasāda</strong><br>
<strong>svādv-anna-tṛptān hari-bhakta-saṅghān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛtvaiva tṛptiṁ bhajataḥ sadaiva</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who fully satisfies the devotees of Śrī Krishna with four types of nectarean prasādam foodstuffs that are chewed, sucked, licked, and drunk (that is, he brings about the dispellation of mundanity and the awakening of the joy of love that develops from respecting <i>prasādam</i>), and he is thereby himself satisfied.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhikā-mādhavayor apara</strong><br>
<strong>mādhurya-līlā-guṇa-rūpa-nāmnām</strong><br>
<strong>prati-kṣaṇāsvādana-lolupasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇaravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, whose heart is insatiably desirous of tasting the infinite Holy Names, Forms, Qualities and Pastimes of Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava that are full of the sweetness of love divine.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nikuñja-yūno rati-keli-siddhyair</strong><br>
<strong>yā yālibhir yuktir apekṣaṇīyā</strong><br>
<strong>tatrāti-dākṣyād ati-vallabhasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who is extremely dear to the Supreme Divine Couple who sport in the groves of Vraja, because he is greatly expert in those appropriate engagements that are performed by the <i>sakhīs</i> in order to serve the amorous Pastimes of the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair</strong><br>
<strong>uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master (who is the simultaneously inconceivably distinct and nondistinct manifest embodiment of the Supreme Personality of Godhead): all the Scriptures sing the glories of his being the direct non-different form of the Supreme Lord Śrī Hari, and the pure saints also concede this; yet, he is the most beloved of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo</strong><br>
<strong>yasyāprasādāt na gatiḥ kuto ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>dhyayaṁ stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇaravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, ever singing his glories and meditating upon him at the three junctions of the day. By his grace alone one gains the grace of the Lord, and if he is displeased the soul has no shelter anywhere.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmad guroraṣṭakam etad uchair</strong><br>
<strong>brāhme muhūrte paṭhati prayātnāt</strong><br>
<strong>yas tena vṛndāvana-nātha-sākṣāt-</strong><br>
<strong>sevaiva labhyā januṣo ’nta eva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who, during the Brāhma-muhūrta (nintysix minutes before sunrise) with meticulous regularity wholeheartedly sings these eight prayers to the Divine Master, attains, upon leaving the body in his spiritual perfection, the qualification to engage in the direct service of the Lord of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-jive-daya-kori" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Auspicious Invocation to Śaraṇāgati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu jīve dayā kori’</strong><br>
<strong>svapārṣada svīya dhāma saha avatari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Śrī Krishna Chaitanya,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Being gracious upon the <i>jīva</i>-souls,<br />Along with Your associates and abode<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;You descended to this world</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atyanta durlabha prema koribāre dāna</strong><br>
<strong>śikhāya saraṇāgati bhakatera prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To distribute freely<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the love divine most rare;<br />By teaching exclusive surrender,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the life and soul of the devotee</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dainya, ātmanivedana, goptṛtve varaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>‘avaśya rakṣibe kṛṣṇa’—viśvāsa pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humility, self-giving,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Embracing His guardianship,<br />‘Krishna will certainly protect me’ —<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Maintaining this faith.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakti-anukūla mātra kārjera svīkāra</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-pratikūla bhāva—varjanāṅgīkāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Acceptance of all activities<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;favourable to devotion;<br />Embracing rejection of<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adversity to devotion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṣaḍ-aṅga śaraṇāgati hoibe jā̐hāra</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra prārthanā śune śrī-nandakumāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who surrender in this sixfold way —<br />Their prayer will be heard<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;by the divine son of Nanda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-sanātana-pade dante tṛṇa kori’</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda poḍe dui pada dhori’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Taking in his mouth a straw, falling<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;at the lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa-Sanātan;<br />Śrī Bhakti Vinod clasps their holy lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐diyā kā̐diyā bole, āmi to’ adhama</strong><br>
<strong>śikhāye śaraṇāgati karahe uttama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping, weeping, I implore thee,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘O I am afallen!<br />Please teach me to surrender,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;teach me pure devotion.’</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhaja-re-bhaja-re-amar-mana-ati-manda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajan-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja re bhaja re āmār mana ati manda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(bhajan vinā gati nāi re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(vraja-bane rādhā-kṛṣṇa bhajan vinā gati nāi re)<br>
(bhaja)<strong> vraja-bane rādhā-kṛṣṇa-charaṇāravinda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jñāna-karma parihari’ re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śuddha rāga patha dhari jñāna-karma parihari)</p>
<p class="translation">O my mind, how foolish you are. Just worship the lotus feet of Rādhā and Krishna in the forest of Vraja. Without such worship there is no means of spiritual advancement. Give up all speculative knowledge and materialistic activities and just worship Rādhā and Krishna in the forests of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(bhaja)<strong> gaura-gadādharādvaita-guru-nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-kṛṣṇe abhede jene)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(guru kṛṣṇa-priya jene gaura-kṛṣne abheda jene re)<br>
<strong>śrīnivāsa, haridāsa, murāri, mukunda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-preme smara smara re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śrīnivāsa, haridāse, gaura-preme smara re)</p>
<p class="translation">You  should  worship  and  serve  Lord  Gaura, Gadādhara, Advaita, and the original Spiritual Master, Lord Nityānanda. Knowing Lord Gaura and Lord Krishna to be the same, and knowing the Spiritual Master to be very dear to Krishna, you should remember the dear associates of Lord Chaitanya, such as Śrīvās Ṭhākur, Haridās Ṭhākur, Murāri Gupta, and Mukunda Datta. In deep love for Lord Gaura, you should remember the two great personalities Śrīvās Ṭhākur and Haridās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-sanātana-jīva-raghunātha-dvandva</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jadi bhajan korbe re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(rūpa-sanātane smaran jadi bhajan korbe re)<br>
<strong>rāghava-gopāla-bhaṭṭa-svarūpa-rāmānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(kṛṣṇa prema jadi chāo re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(svarūpa-rāmānande smara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-prema jadi chāo re)</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You should remember the Goswāmīs of Vṛndāvan: Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Jīva Goswāmī, Raghunāth  Bhaṭṭa  Goswāmī,  and  Raghunāth  Dās Goswāmī. If you worship Lord Krishna, then you should remember the two great souls Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī and Sanātan Goswāmī. You should also remember Rāghava Paṇḍit, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Swarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī, and Rāmānanda Rāya. If you really seek love of Krishna, then you must remember Swarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī and Rāmānanda Rāya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>goṣṭhī-saha karṇapura-sen-śivānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ajasra smara smara re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(goṣṭhī-saha-sen śivānande ajasra smara re)<br>
<strong>rūpānuga sādhu-jana bhajana-ānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(vraje bās jadi chāo re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(rūpānuga sādhu-jane smara vraje bās jadi chāo re)<br>
<strong>rūpānuga sādhu-jana bhajana-ānanda</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja re bhaja re āmār mana ati manda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You should remember Śrīla Kavi Karṇapura and all his family members, who are all sincere servants of Lord Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. You should also remember the father of Kavi Karṇapura, Śivānanda Sena. Always remember, always remember Śrī Karṇapura and his family. You should remember all those Vaiṣṇavas who strictly follow the path of Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī and who are absorbed in the ecstasy of <i>bhajan</i>. If you actually want residence in the land of Vraja, then you must remember all of the Vaiṣṇavas, who are followers of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vibhavari-shiesha-aloka-praveshia" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vibhāvarī Śeṣa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vibhāvarī śeṣa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āloka-praveśa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nidrā chāḍi’ uṭho jīva</strong><br>
<strong>bolo hari hari,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukunda murāri,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāma kṛṣṇa hayagrīva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The night has come to an end and the light of dawn is entering. O <i>jīva</i>-soul, arise and give up your sleep. Chant the Holy Names of Lord Hari, who is the giver of liberation; the enemy of the Mura demon; the Supreme Enjoyer; the all-attractive one; and the horse-headed incarnation, Hayagrīva.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nṛsiṁha vāmana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-madhusūdana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vrajendra-nandana śyāma</strong><br>
<strong>pūtanā-ghātana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaiṭabha-śātana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya dāśarathi-rāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Hari incarnated as the half-man, half-lion, Nṛṣiṁha. He appeared as a  dwarf-<i>brāhmaṇ</i> named Upendra and is the killer of the Madhu demon. He is the beloved son of the King of Vraja, Nanda Mahārāj, and is blackish in complexion. He is the slayer of the Pūtanā witch and the destroyer of the demon Kaiṭabha. All glories to Lord Hari, who appeared as Lord Rāma, the son of King Dāśarath.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśodā dulāla,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda-gopāla,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana purandara</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-priya-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhikā-ramaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuvana-sundara-vara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the darling of Mother Yaśodā; the giver of pleasure to the cows, land, and spiritual senses; and the protector of the cows. He is the Lord of the Vṛndāvan forest; the <i>gopīs</i>’ beloved; the lover of Rādhikā; and the most beautiful personality in all the worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rāvāṇāntakara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mākhona-taskara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-jana-vastra-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>vrajera rākhāla,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopa-vṛnda-pāla,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta-hārī baṁśī-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As Rāmachandra He brought about the end of the demoniac King Rāvaṇa; as Krishna He stole the older gopīs’ butter; He stole the younger <i>gopīs</i>’ clothes while they were bathing in the Yamunā. He is a cowherd boy of Vraja and the protector of the cowherd boys. He steals the hearts of all and always holds a flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jogīndra-bandana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-nanda-nandana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-jana-bhaya-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>navīna nīrada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūpa manohara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mohana-vaṁśī-bihārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Krishna is worshiped by the best of yogis and is the son of Nanda. He removes all the fears of the inhabitants of Vraja. He is the colour of a fresh rain cloud, and His form is enchanting. When He wanders about, playing His flute, He looks very charming.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśodā-nandana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaṁsa-nisūdana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikuñja-rāsa-vilāsī</strong><br>
<strong>kadamba-kānana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāsa-parāyaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndā-vipina-nivāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the son of Yaśodā and the killer of King Kaṁsa, and He sports in the Rāsa dance among the groves of Vraja. Krishna engages in this Rāsa dance underneath the kadamba trees, and He resides in the forest of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānanda-vardhana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-niketana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phula-śara-jojaka kāma</strong><br>
<strong>gopāṅgaṇāgaṇa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta-vinodana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;samasta-guṇa-gaṇa-dhāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He increases the ecstasy of His devotees. He is the reservoir of all love and is the transcendental Cupid who uses His flowered arrows to increase the loving desires of the gopīs. He is the pleasure of the <i>gopīs</i>’ hearts and the abode of all wonderful qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāmuna-jīvana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keli-parāyaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mānasa-chandra-chakora</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-sudhā-rasa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāo kṛṣṇa-jaśa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākho vachana mana mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Krishna is the life of the River Yamunā. He is always absorbed in amorous Pastimes, and He is the moon of the <i>gopīs</i>’ minds, which are like the chakora birds that subsist only upon moonlight. O mind, obey these words of mine and sing the glories of Śrī Krishna in the form of these Holy Names, which are full of nectarean mellows.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-thakura-vaishnava-gana-kori-ei-nivedana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ṭhākura-vaiṣṇava-gaṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭhākura vaiṣṇava-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kori ei nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mo boḍo adhama durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>dāruṇa-saṁsāra-nidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe ḍhūbailo vidhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keśe dhori more kara para</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O saintly Vaiṣṇavas, although I am very fallen and misbehaved, I nevertheless place this appeal before you. My destiny has immersed me in this fearsome ocean of repeated birth and death. Please grab me by the hair, and pull me to the opposite shore.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidhi boḍo valavān&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na śune dharama-jñāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadāi karama-pāśe bāndhe</strong><br>
<strong>nā dekhi tāraṇa leśa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jata dekhi saba kleśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anātha kātare te̐i kānde</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My destiny is very powerful. I am not able to hear the knowledge of spiritual life, and I am always bound by the ropes of <i>karma</i>. I cannot see any way to escape my condition. I see only suffering. Helpless and stricken with grief, I cry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāma krodha lobha moha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada abhimāna saha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana āpana sthāne ṭāne</strong><br>
<strong>aichāna āmāra mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phire jena andha jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;supatha vipatha nāhi jāne</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am just like a blind man unable to see what path is auspicious or inauspicious. I am helplessly dragged from here to there by lust, anger, greed, bewilderment, madness, and pride.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>na loinu sat mata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asate majilo chita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā pāye na korinu āśa</strong><br>
<strong>narottama dāse koy&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhi śuni lage bhay</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarāiyā loho nija pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind became immersed in the impermanent satisfaction of the senses, and I could not understand the real goal of life. For this reason I did not aspire to attain Your lotus feet. Narottam Dās says, “Now that I am able to see and hear the Vaiṣṇavas, I have become free from all fear.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-haraye-namah-krishna-yadavaya-namah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nām-saṅkīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari haraye namaḥ kṛṣṇa yādavāya namaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>yādavāya mādhavāya keśavāya namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Hari, O Lord Krishna, I offer my obeisances to You, who are known as Yādava, Hari, Mādhava, and Keśava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopāla govinda rāma śrī madhusūdana</strong><br>
<strong>giridhārī gopīnātha madana-mohana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopāla, Govinda, Rāma, Śrī Madhusūdan, Giridhārī, Gopīnāth, Madana-mohan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-chaitanya nityānanda śrī advaita-chandra</strong><br>
<strong>gadādhara śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta-vṛnda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Chaitanya and Nityānanda. All glories to the moonlike Śrī Advaita Āchārya. All glories to Śrī Gadādhara Paṇḍit and all the devotees of Śrī Chaitanya headed by Śrīvās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rūpa sanātana bhaṭṭa raghunātha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-jīva-gopāla-bhaṭṭa dāsa raghunātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Allgloriesto Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, Gopāla Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, and Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei choy gosāir kori charaṇa vandana</strong><br>
<strong>jāhā hoite bighna-nāś abhīṣṭa pūraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisances to the Six Goswāmīs. By offering them my obeisances all obstacles to devotion are destroyed and all spiritual desires are fulfilled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei choy gosāir jā̐ra mu̐i tā̐ra dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐-sabāra pada-reṇu mora pañcha-grāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am a servant of that person who is a servant of these six Goswāmīs. The dust of their holy feet is my five kinds of foodstuffs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐dera charaṇa sebi bhakta sane vāsa</strong><br>
<strong>janame janame mora ei abhilāṣa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is my desire, that birth after birth I may live with those devotees who serve the lotus feet of these six Goswāmīs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei choy gosāi jabe vraje koilā vāsa</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nitya-līlā korilā prakāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When these six Goswāmīs lived in Vraja they revealed and explained the eternal Pastimes of Rādhā and Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānande bolo hari bhaja vṛndāvana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-pade majāiyā mana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Absorbing your mind in meditation upon the divine feet of the Spiritual Master and the holy Vaiṣṇavas, chant the names of Lord Hari in ecstasy, and worship the transcendental realm of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-pāda-padma kori āśa</strong><br>
<strong>(hari) nāma-saṅkīrtana kohe narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Desiring the lotus feet of Śrī Guru and the Vaiṣṇavas, Narottam Dās sings the <i>saṅkīrtan</i> of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Midday Songs</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-bhaja-bhakata-vatsala-shri-gaurahari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Bhog Ārati Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja bhakata-vatsala śrī-gaurahari</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurahari sohi goṣṭha-bihārī,</strong><br>
<strong>nanda-jaśomatī-chitta-hārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just  worship  Śrī  Gaurahari,  who  is  always affectionate toward His devotees. He is the same Supreme Godhead, Krishna, who sported in the cowherd pastures of Vraja and stole the hearts of Nanda and Yaśodā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>belā ho’lo, dāmodara, āisa ekhano</strong><br>
<strong>bhoga-mandire bosi’ koraho bhojana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mother Yaśodā calls to Krishna, “My dear Dāmodar, it is very late. Please come right now, sit down in the dining hall, and take your lunch.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nandera nirdeśe baise giri-vara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>baladeva-saha sakhā baise sāri sāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On the direction of Nanda Mahārāj, Krishna, the holder of Govardhan Hill, sits down, and then all the cowherd boys, along with Krishna’s elder brother, Śrī Baladeva, sit down in rows to take their lunch.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuktā-śākādi bhāji nālitā kuṣmāṇḍa</strong><br>
<strong>ḍāli ḍālnā dugdha-tumbī dadhi mochā-ghaṇṭa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They are then served a feast of śuktā and various kinds of green leafy vegetables, then nice fried things, and a salad made of the green leaves of the jute plant. They are also served pumpkin, baskets of fruit, small square cakes made of lentils and cooked down milk, then thick yogurt, squash cooked in milk, and vegetable preparations made from the flower of the banana tree.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mudga-boḍā māṣa-boḍā roṭikā ghṛtānna</strong><br>
<strong>śaṣkulī piṣṭaka kṣīr puli pāyasānna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then they receive fried squares of mung dal patties, and urad dal patties, chapātīs, and rice with ghee. Next, sweets made with milk, sugar, and sesamum; rice flour cakes; thick cooked-down milk; cakes floating in milk; and sweet rice.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karpūra amṛta–kelī rambhā kṣīra-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>amṛta rasālā, amla dvādaśa prakāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is also sweet rice that tastes just like nectar due to its being mixed with camphor. There are bananas, and cheese which is nectarean and delicious. They are also served twelve kinds of sour preparations made with tamarinds, limes, lemons, oranges, and pomegranates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>luchi chini sarapurī lāḍḍu rasāvalī</strong><br>
<strong>bhojana korena kṛṣṇa ho’ye kutūhalī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are purīs made with white flour and sugar; purīs filled with cream; laḍḍus; and dal patties boiled in sugared rice. Krishna eagerly eats all of the food.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikāra pakka anna vividha vyañjana</strong><br>
<strong>parama ānande kṛṣṇa korena bhojana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In great ecstasy and joy Krishna eats the rice, curried  vegetables,  sweets,  and  pastries  cooked  by Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chole-bole lāḍḍu khāy śrī-madhumaṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>bagala bājāy āra deya hari-bolo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna’s funny <i>brāhmaṇ</i> friend Madhumaṅgal, who is very fond of laḍḍus, gets them by hook or by crook. Eating the laḍḍus he shouts, “Haribol! Haribol!” and makes a funny sound by slapping his sides under his armpits with his hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikādi gaṇe heri’ nayanera koṇe</strong><br>
<strong>tṛpta ho’ye khāy kṛṣṇa yaśodā-bhavane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding Rādhārāṇī and Her <i>gopī</i> friends out of the corners of His eyes, Krishna eats at the house of Mother Yaśodā with great satisfaction.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhojanānte piye kṛṣṇa suvāsita vāri</strong><br>
<strong>sabe mukha prakṣāloy ho’ye sāri sāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After lunch, Krishna drinks rose-scented water. Then all of the boys, standing in lines, wash their mouths.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hasta-mukha prakṣāliyā jata sakhā-gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ānande viśrāma kore baladeva-sane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After all the cowherd boys wash their hands and mouths, in great bliss they take rest with Lord Balarām.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāmbula rasāla āne tāmbūla-maśālā</strong><br>
<strong>tāhā kheye kṛṣṇa-chandra sukhe nidrā gelā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two cowherd boys Jāmbula and Rasāla then bring Krishna <i>pān</i> made with betel nuts, fancy spices, and catechu. After eating that <i>pān</i>, Śrī Krishnachandra then happily goes to sleep.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśālākṣa śikhi-pucha-chāmara ḍhulāya</strong><br>
<strong>apūrva śayāya kṛṣṇa sukhe nidrā jāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While  Krishna  happily  takes  His  rest  on  an excellent bedstead, His servant Viśālākṣa cools Him with a fan of peacock feathers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaśomatī-ājñā peye dhaniṣṭhā-ānito</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-prasāda rādhā bhuñje hoye prīto</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At Mother Yaśodā’s request the <i>gopī</i> Dhaniṣṭhā brings remnants of food left on Krishna’s plate to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, who eats them with great delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalitādi sakhī-gaṇa avaśeṣa pāya</strong><br>
<strong>mane mane sukhe rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lalitā-devī and the other <i>gopīs</i> then receive the remnants, and within their hearts they sing the glories of Rādhārāṇī and Krishna with great joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-līlā ek-mātra jāhāra pramoda</strong><br>
<strong>bhogārati gāy ṭhākur bhakativinoda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod, whose one and only joy is the Pastimes of Lord Hari, sings this Bhog Ārati song.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja govinda govinda gopāla</strong><br>
<strong>giridhārī gopīnātha nanda dulala</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja govinda govinda gopāla</strong><br>
<strong>nanda dulala jaya jaya śachī dulala</strong><br>
<strong>śachī dulala jaya jaya gaura gopāla</strong></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-mahaprasade-govinde-nama-brahmani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Mahāprasāda <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprasāde govinde nāma brahmaṇi vaiṣṇave</strong><br>
<strong>svalpa pūṇya vatāṁ rājan viśvāso naiva jāyate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">For those who have not amassed sufficient pious credits they will never be able to honour Mahāprasāda, Lord Govinda, the Holy Name, the <i>brāhmaṇs</i> and the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhai-re-shorira-avidya-jal-jodendriya-tahe-kal" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Prasāda-sevāya Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi-re!</strong><br>
<strong>śorīra avidyā-jāl,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;joḍendriya tāhe kāl,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīve phele viṣaya-sāgore</strong><br>
<strong>tā’ra madhye jihvā ati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobhamoy sudurmati,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā’ke jetā koṭhina saṁsāre</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">bhāi</i> — brother; <i class="wbw__term">re</i> — O!; <i class="wbw__term">śorīra</i> — body; <i class="wbw__term">avidyā</i> — ignorance's; <i class="wbw__term">jāl</i> — net; <i class="wbw__term">joḍendriya</i> — material senses (jada indriya); <i class="wbw__term">tāhe</i> — therein; <i class="wbw__term">kāl</i> — deadly; <i class="wbw__term">jīve</i> — soul; <i class="wbw__term">phele</i> — throws; <i class="wbw__term">viṣaya</i> — sense enjoyment; <i class="wbw__term">sāgore</i> — into the ocean; <i class="wbw__term">tā’ra</i> — them; <i class="wbw__term">madhye</i> — among; <i class="wbw__term">jihvā</i> — the tongue; <i class="wbw__term">ati</i> — most; <i class="wbw__term">lobhamoy</i> — full of greed; <i class="wbw__term">sudurmati</i> — very wicked; <i class="wbw__term">tā’ke</i> — that; <i class="wbw__term">jetā</i> — to conquer; <i class="wbw__term">koṭhina</i> — difficult; <i class="wbw__term">saṁsāre</i> — in this material world.</p>
<p class="translation">Oh brothers! This material body is a network of ignorance, and the senses are one’s deadly enemies, for they throw the soul into the ocean of material sense enjoyment. Among the senses, the tongue is the most voracious and verily wicked; it is very difficult to conquer the tongue in this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa boḍo dayāmoy,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koribāre jihvā jay,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sva-prasād-anna dilo bhāi</strong><br>
<strong>sei annāmṛta pāo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme ḍāko chaitanya-nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇa</i> — Krsna; <i class="wbw__term">boḍo</i> — very; <i class="wbw__term">dayāmoy</i> — merciful; <i class="wbw__term">koribāre</i> — in order to; <i class="wbw__term">jihvā</i> — the tongue; <i class="wbw__term">jay</i> — conquer; <i class="wbw__term">sva</i> — His own; <i class="wbw__term">prasād</i> — prasādam; <i class="wbw__term">anna</i> — food, grains; <i class="wbw__term">dilo</i> — gave; <i class="wbw__term">bhāi</i> — O brother; <i class="wbw__term">sei</i> — that; <i class="wbw__term">annāmṛta</i> — food like nectar; <i class="wbw__term">pāo</i> — accept; <i class="wbw__term">rādhā</i> — Rādhā; <i class="wbw__term">kṛṣṇa</i> — Krşņa; <i class="wbw__term">guṇa</i> — glories; <i class="wbw__term">gāo</i> — sing; <i class="wbw__term">preme</i> — with love; <i class="wbw__term">ḍāko</i> — call out; <i class="wbw__term">chaitanya</i> — Chaitanya; <i class="wbw__term">nitāi</i> — Nitāi!.</p>
<p class="translation">Oh brothers! Lord Krishna is very merciful—just to control the tongue He has given us the remnants of His own food! Now please eat these nectarean grains while singing the glories of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī RādhāKrishna, and in love call out Chaitanya! Nitāi!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(preme bhore ḍākare)</strong><br>
<strong>(dayāl nitāi chaitanya bole, preme bhore ḍākare)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(nitāi gaura haribol)</strong></p>
<p class="wbw"><i class="wbw__term">preme</i> — with love; <i class="wbw__term">bhore</i> — filled; <i class="wbw__term">ḍākare</i> — chant!; <i class="wbw__term">dayāl</i> — merciful; <i class="wbw__term">nitāi</i> — Nitāi; <i class="wbw__term">chaitanya</i> — Chaitanya; <i class="wbw__term">bole</i> — chant; <i class="wbw__term">preme</i> — with love; <i class="wbw__term">bhore</i> — filled; <i class="wbw__term">ḍākare</i> — chant!; <i class="wbw__term">nitāi</i> — Nitāi; <i class="wbw__term">gaura</i> — Gaura; <i class="wbw__term">haribol</i> — chant "Hari!".</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Evening Songs</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-gaurachander-arotiko-shobha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Gaura-ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> jaya jaya gaurāchā̐der ārotiko śobhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāhṇavī-taṭa-vane jagamana-lobhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jaga janer mana-lobhā)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅger āroti śobhā jaga janer mana-lobhā)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the beautiful ceremony of worship to Lord Gaurachandra in a grove on the banks of the Jāhṇavī river! It is attracting the minds of all living entities in the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> dakṣiṇe nitāichā̐d bāme gadādhara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikaṭe advaita śrīnivāsa chatra-dhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On Lord Gaurachandra’s right side is Lord Nityānanda, and on His left is Śrī Gadādhara. Before Him stands Śrī Advaita, and Śrīvās Ṭhākur holds an umbrella over the Lord’s head.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> bosiyāche gaurāchā̐d ratna-siṁhāsane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ratna-simhāsana alo korere</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bosiyāchen gaurāchāṅd ratna-siṁhāsane)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ārati korena brahmā ādi-deva gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅger āroti kore)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(brahmā ādi-deva gaṇe gaurāṅger āroti kore)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya is seated upon a jewelled throne while the demigods headed by Lord Brahmā perform the ārati ceremony.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> narahari-ādi kori chāmara ḍhulāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sañjaya-mukunda-vāsu-ghoṣa-ādi gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Narahari Sarakār and other associates of Lord Chaitanya fan Him with whisks and the devotees headed by Sañjaya Paṇḍit, Mukunda Datta, and Vāsu Ghoṣa sing sweet kīrtan along with the other devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> śaṅkha bāje ghaṇṭā bāje bāje karatāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhura mṛdaṅga bāje parama rasāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅger āroti kāle madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śaṅkha bāje ghaṇṭā bāje madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(madhura mṛdanga bāje madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">Conchshells, bells, and karatālas resound, and the mṛdaṅgas play very sweetly. This kīrtan music is supremely sweet and relishable to hear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> bahu-koṭi chandra jini vadana ujjvala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gala-deśe bona-mālā kore jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jhalmol jhalmol jhalmol kore)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura gole bona phuler mālā jhalmol jhalmol jhalmol kore)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">The brilliance of Lord Chaitanya’s face conquers millions upon millions of moons, and the garland of forest flowers around His neck shines.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse">(kiba)<strong> śiva-śuka-nārada preme gada-gada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakativinoda dekhe gorāra sampada</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Shiva, Śukadeva Goswāmī and Nārada Muni are all present and their voices are choked with the ecstasy of transcendental love. Thus Bhakti Vinod envisions the glorious worship of Lord Gaurachandra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ṭhākura śrī bhaktivinoda ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ṭhākura śrī sarasvatī ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(patit-pāvan śrī gurudev ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(sapārṣada gaurahari ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">This time please give me your grace. Oh Śrī Bhakti Vinod, Śrī Saraswatī Ṭhākur, Oh Gurudeva, saviour of the fallen, Oh Lord Gaurahari along with Your associates, this time please give me Your grace.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayare-jayare-jaya-gaura-sarasvati" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Sāraswat-ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>Translated by Devamoyī Devī Dasī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayare jayare jaya gaura-sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda nvaya karuṇā murati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories, all glories to Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, who is intimately connected with Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur and is an embodiment of Śrī Gaurāṅga’s mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prakāśile gaura-sevā bhūvana maṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>bhakatisiddhānta śuddha prajñāna ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You  manifested  devotional  service  to  Lord Gaurāṅga, which is beneficial to the whole universe. Your pure devotional conclusions are effulgent with Absolute Knowledge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-śyāma ekatanu dakṣe gorā rāya</strong><br>
<strong>bāme rādha madhye svayaṁ śyāma-gopa jaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Rādhā and Śyāma (Krishna) who are combined in the one body of Śrī Gaurāṅga, who is on the right side; glories to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, who is on the left side; and glories to Him, who is in the middle and who is none other than the Supreme Cowherd Boy, Śrī Śyāmasundar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja rasa nava bhāve nabadvīpe rāje</strong><br>
<strong>udāre madhura rāga abhinava sāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The mellow of Vraja (Vṛndāvan) is present in Nabadwīp in a new mood; when this sweet loving sentiment is mixed with magnanimity, then it shows itself as uniquely beautiful.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhurya kaivalya rāga vrajera nirjāsa</strong><br>
<strong>prāpti parākāṣṭhā tāhe gaurāṅga vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mādhurya-rasa (conjugal love) is the essence of Vraja Dhām, and the Pastimes of Mahāprabhu take it to its highest perfection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā bhāva-kānti aṅgikari’ bhālo mate</strong><br>
<strong>dakṣiṇe āsana rasa garimā dekhāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Earnestly accepting Śrī Rādhā’s mood and beauty, He has taken His place on the right to show His glorious position.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-rasa-troyo-svāda rahasya proyāsa</strong><br>
<strong>nirakhi praphulla rādhā mukhe manda hāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing Krishna’s mischievous desire to understand the glory of Her love, Krishna’s wonderful sweetness that only She can relish through Her transcendental qualities, and the happiness She feels from Krishna’s sweet love for Her, Rādhārāṇī is very pleased and there is a subdued smile on Her face.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhye rahi vaṁśīrave ghoṣe vaṁśīdhara</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra sampade āmī gaurāṅga-sundara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the middle, Vaṁśīdhara (the Flute Player) is playing His flute, thinking that, “Only by the wealth of Rādhārāṇī do I become Gaurāṅga Sundar.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madabhīṣṭha rūpa rādhāra hṛdoya mandire</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga bhajile suṣṭhu sphūrti pāya tāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“My most cherished form is revealed in the temple of Rādhārāṇī’s heart, and that highest manifestation of Myself can be attained only by worshipping Gaurāṅga.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā prakāśe mahāprabhu gaura-nidhi</strong><br>
<strong>patita pāvana deve milāila vidhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By the Lord’s divine arrangement, the Supreme Savior of the fallen souls appeared in Nadīyā as the most beloved Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e-rūpa ārati brahmā śambhu agochara</strong><br>
<strong>gaura bhakta kṛpā pātra mātra siddhi sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This type of divine worship of Mahāprabhu is beyond the vision of even Lord Brahmā or Lord Shiva. Only by receiving the mercy of a devotee of Lord Gaurāṅga can one attain such perfection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī svarūpa, rāmānanda, rūpa, sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī raghu, jīvādi kṛpāya dekhe bhakta-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Only by the mercy of Śrī Swarūp Dāmodar, Śrī Rāmananda Rāya, Śrī Rūpa, Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Raghunāth, Śrī Jīva and their associates do the devotees attain that vision.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya guru-gaura-rādhā-govinda-sundara</strong><br>
<strong>jaya dāo bhakta vṛnda nitya nirantara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Guru, Gaurāṅga, Rādhā-Govindasundar. O devotees, always sing Their eternal glories.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-shri-chaitanya-more-koribena-daya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya Prārthanā Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe śrī chaitanya more-koribena dayā</strong><br>
<strong>kobe āmi pāibo vaiṣṇava-pada-chāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O when will Śrī Chaitanya bestow upon me his Grace? When will I get shelter in the shade of the holy feet of the Vaiṣṇavas?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe āmi chāḍibo e viṣoyābhimān</strong><br>
<strong>kobe viṣṇu-jane āmi koribo sammān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I give up the false ego of being an enjoyer and when will I offer all respects to the sincere devotees of Lord Viṣṇu?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>galavastra kṛtāñjali vaiṣṇava-nikaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>dante tṛṇa kori dā̐ḍāibo niṣkapaṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I submit myself by the feet of the Vaiṣṇava with the cloth of surrender around my neck and with straw between my teeth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐diyā kā̐diyā jānāibo duḥkhagrām</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-anala hoite māgibo viśrām</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping and weeping when will I submit my sorrowful tale at the feet of the Vaiṣṇava and when will I seek relief from the fire of material suffering praying to that Vaiṣṇava for shelter and rest?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuniyā āmāra duḥkha vaiṣṇava ṭhākura</strong><br>
<strong>āmā’ lāgi’ kṛṣṇe āvedibena prachura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Upon hearing the tale of my worldly sorrow that saintly Vaiṣṇava will petition Krishna on my behalf in prayer.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera āvedane kṛṣṇa dayāmoy</strong><br>
<strong>e heno pāmara prati hoben sadoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then satisfied by that Vaiṣṇava’s prayer Krishna will be merciful upon this wicked and sinful wretch.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vinodera nivedan vaiṣṇava-charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā kori saṅge loho ei akiñchane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur submits this prayer at the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas. “Give me your Grace and accept this lowly soul in your association.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namo-namah-tulasi-maharani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Tulasī-parikramā-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Chandraśekhara Kavi</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo namaḥ tulasī mahārāṇī,</strong><br>
<strong>vṛnde mahārāṇī namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Tulasī Mahārāṇī, O Vṛnda Devī, I offer my obeisances unto you again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo re namo re māiyā namo nārāyaṇi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Nārāyaṇi I offer you my obeisances again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāko daraśe, paraśe agha-nāśa-hoi</strong><br>
<strong>mahimā veda-purāṇe bākhāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By seeing or touching you all sins are destroyed. Your glories are sung in the Vedas and Purāṇas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāko patra, mañjarī komala</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-pati-charaṇa-kamale lapaṭāni</strong><br>
<strong>dhanya tulasi, pūraṇa tapa kiye,</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-śālagrāma-mahā-pāṭarāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your leaves and soft mañjarīs are entwined at the lotus feet of Lord Nārāyaṇa. O Tulasī by your performance of austerity you have become the worshipful consort of Śrī Śālagrām Śila.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhūpa, dīpa, naivedya, āroti,</strong><br>
<strong>phulanā kiye varakhā varakhāni</strong><br>
<strong>chāppānna bhoga, chatriśa vyañjana,</strong><br>
<strong>vinā tulasī prabhu eka nāhi māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You shower your mercy upon anyone who offers you incense, lamp, foodstuffs, worship and flowers. The Lord does not accept even one of fifty-six food offerings or thirty-six curries offered without Tulasī leaves.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śiva-śuka-nārada, āur brahmādiko,</strong><br>
<strong>ḍhuḍata phirata mahā-muni jñānī</strong><br>
<strong>chandraśekhara māiyā, terā jaśa gāoye</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-dāna dījiye mahārāṇi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Shiva, Śukadeva Goswāmī, Nārada Muni, all the demigods headed by Lord Brahmā, the mystics and great munis are all circumambulating you. O Tulasī Mahārāṇī, thus Chandraśekhara sings of your glories. Please bestow upon him the gift of devotion.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-guru-charana-padma-kevala-bhakati" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-vaiṣṇava Māhātmya-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
