<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-vinoda-mātra-tattva-lakṣaṇānvayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-saṅga-kṛṣṇa-nāma-sādhanaika-niśchayam</strong><br>
<strong>prema-sevanaika-mātra-sādhya-kṛṣṇa-tatparaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this manner Lord Gaurāṅgadev taught that all scriptural conclusions, as perfectly passed down by the various spiritual preceptors through the medium of sound, culminate in the embodiment of Śrī Rādhāvinoda, the all-encompassing basis of proper acquaintance with the environment. The means by which one may reach Lord Krishna is by chanting His Holy Names in the association of pure devotees, while the ultimate goal in pure love is service to Lord Krishna, who is so dear to the cowherd maidens of Vṛndāvan. Lord Gaurāṅgadev taught these essential scriptural conclusions in the assembly of scholarly persons. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="50">
<h5>50</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-rāma-vāchanaika-ṣaṣṭikārtha-darśitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rudra-saṁkhya-śabda-jāta-yad-yad-artha-sambhṛtam</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-sarva-yukta-tat-tad-artha-bhuridākaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurasundar explained this famous verse of the Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam sixty-one times to Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī, and later to Prakāśānanda Saraswatī:<br>
<i>ātmārāmaś cha munayo</i><br /><i>nirgranthā apy urukrame</i><br /><i>kurvanty ahaitukīṁ</i><br /><i>bhaktim itthaṁ-bhūta guṇo hariḥ</i><br>
“Even the liberated souls fully satisfied in the self are irresistibly attracted by the superexcellent qualities of Krishna and surrender to Him with unalloyed devotion.” The Lord separately combined each of the eleven words found in that verse and established that each one is an enormous storehouse of unlimited wealth supporting the conclusion of pure devotion. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-sanatananu-rupa-jiva-sampradayakam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="51">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Premadhāma-deva-stotram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Thursday</h3>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>Edited by Swāmī Bhakti Pāvan Janārdan</h4>
<h5>51</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī sanātanānu-rūpa-jīva-sampradāyakaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>lupta-tīrtha-śuddha-bhakti-śāstra-suprachārakam</strong><br>
<strong>nīla-śaila-nātha-pīṭha-naija-kārya-saukaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurasundar revealed His disciplic line by inspiring Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī, his younger brother Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, and Śrīla Jīva Goswāmī. The Lord requested them to excavate the lost places of pilgrimage and compose pure devotional literature which describes the application of spiritual practices and spontaneous devotion. Lord Gaurāṅga also lovingly revealed His worshipable personality to the servitors of Śrī Jagannāth Deva at Nīlāchala (Śrī Purī-dhām). I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="52">
<h5>52</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tyāga-bāhya-bhoga-buddhi-tīvra-daṇḍa-nindanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rāya-śuddha-kṛṣṇa-kāma-sevanābhi-nandanam</strong><br>
<strong>rāya-rāga-sevanokta-bhāgya-koṭi-duṣkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurasundar strongly condemned those who externally accepted the dress of a renunciate yet, like monkeys, actually harbored desires for sense pleasures. On the other hand, He honoured that great devotee Rāmānanda Rāya’s devotional efforts to intimately teach some young girls a form of dance drama for the pleasure of Lord Jagannāth. Considering Rāmānanda’s service to be on the platform of spontaneous devotion, the Lord proclaimed that such a level of service was a great fortune attainable only after millions of births. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="53">
<h5>53</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-prayāga-bhaṭṭa-vallabhaika-niṣṭha-sevanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nīla-śaila-bhaṭṭa-datta-rāga-mārga-rādhanam</strong><br>
<strong>śrī gadādharārpitādhikāra-mantra-mādhuraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The famous spiritual preceptor of the Viṣṇuswāmī disciplic succession <i>śuddhādvaita</i>, Śrī Vallabha Bhaṭṭa, lived in the village of Āḍhāila in sacred Prayāga, and personally served Lord Gaurāṅgadev with resolute devotion in his residence. Later in Jagannāth Purī, Lord Gaurāṅga allowed Vallabha Bhaṭṭa to enter into and relish the sweet taste experienced in parental attachment to baby Krishna. The Lord also arranged for him to learn the appropriate mantras for his worship from Śrī Gadādhara Paṇḍīt. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="54">
<h5>54</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-svarūpa-rāya-saṅga-gāmbhirāntya-līlanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dvādaśābda-vahni-garbha-vipralambha-śīlanam</strong><br>
<strong>rādhikādhirūḍha-bhāva-kānti-kṛṣṇa-kuñjaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Diving deep into the reality of His own beauty and sweetness, Lord Govinda stole the mood of Rādhārāṇī and, garbing Himself in Her brilliant lustre, appeared as Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. For the last twelve years of His manifest Pastimes He was deeply absorbed in union and separation, and He shared His heart’s inner feelings with His most confidential devotees such as Swarūp Dāmodar and Rāmānanda Rāya: <i>bāhire viṣajvālā hoy, antare ānandamoy</i>.<br>
“The wonderful characteristic of divine love of Krishna is that although externally it works like fiery lava, internally it is like sweet nectar that fills the heart with the greatest joy.” Volcanic eruptions of ecstasy flowed from the heart of Śrī Gaurāṅga in the agony of separation from Krishna. This Golden Lord, who appeared like an intoxicated elephant, is none other than Lord Govinda. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="55">
<h5>55</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-svarūpa-kaṇṭha-lagna-māthura-pralāpakaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rādhikānu-vedanārta-tīvra-vipralambhakam</strong><br>
<strong>svapnavat-samādhi-dṛṣṭa-divya-varṇanāturaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, embracing the neck of Śrī Swarūp Dāmodar, began to sorrowfully recite the prayers Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī had previously spoken in the agony of separation when Lord Krishna left Vrindāvan for Mathurā. The Lord would continuously taste the mood of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, who was experiencing untold agony from the burning fire of Her tragic separation from Lord Krishna. With a heavy heart, the Lord would describe what He was directly realising in divine trance. Those realisations are just like dreams to ordinary men. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="56">
<h5>56</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sātvikādi-bhāva-chihna-deha-divya-sauṣṭhavaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kūrma-dharma-bhinna-sandhi-gātra-puṣpa-pelavam</strong><br>
<strong>hrasva-dīrgha-padma-gandha-rakta-pīta-pāṇḍuraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The eightfold symptoms depicting the moods of pure dedication would decorate and radiantly enhance the beauty of the Lord’s bodily parts. Sometimes He appeared like a tortoise who had withdrawn his limbs into his shell, and at other times His body appeared elongated due to His joints becoming dislocated. Sometimes Lord Gaurasundar’s figure was as tender and aromatic as a lotus flower. At other times the Lord’s body would appear red, yellow, or brilliant with the white hue of the jasmine flower. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="57">
<h5>57</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tīvra-vipralambha-mugdha-mandirāgra-dhāvitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kūrma-rūpa-divya-gandha-lubdha-dhenu-veṣṭitam</strong><br>
<strong>varṇitāli-kūla-kṛṣṇa-keli-śaila-kandaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Grief-stricken and overwhelmed with tragic feelings of separation, Lord Chaitanya quickly ran to the lion-gate of Lord Jagannāth’s grand temple. Moments later, the Lord fell to the ground. In deep separation He manifested the form of the tortoise incarnation Kūrmadeva, and an extraordinarily sweet fragrance began to emanate from the Lord’s unconscious figure. This attracted the <i>telāṅgī</i> cows who resided at Lord Jagannāth’s Temple, and they surrounded the Lord. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="58">
<h5>58</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>indu-sindhu-nṛtya-dīpta-kṛṣṇa-keli-mohitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>ūrmi-śīrṣa-supta-deha-vāta-raṅga-vāhitam</strong><br>
<strong>yāmunāli-kṛṣṇa-keli-magna-saukhya-sāgaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Once on a moonlit night, Lord Gaurāṅga-deva, relishing the sweet mellows of Lord Krishna’s Pastimes, was wandering along the ocean’s shore with His associates. Suddenly the Lord saw the reflection of the moon dancing upon the rolling waves. Unexpectedly, the Lord became overwhelmed by recollections of Lord Krishna’s Pastimes in the Yamunā River and fell unconscious. Next, Lord Gaurāṅga’s body, unseen by the devotees, became as light as a piece of dried wood and was gracefully carried away by the wind-god while lying upon the crest of the ocean’s rolling waves in a divine trance. Realizing Lord Krishna’s water-sport Pastimes in the Kālindī River with His dearmost cowherd-maiden friends, the Lord dove deep into an ocean of divine ecstasy. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="59">
<h5>59</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rātri-śeṣa-saumya-veśa-śāyitārdra-saikataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhinna-sandhi-dīrgha-deha-pelavāti-daivatam</strong><br>
<strong>śrānta-bhakta-chakratīrtha-hṛṣṭa-dṛṣṭi-gocharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After searching for the Lord throughout the night, the exhausted devotees finally found Him at night’s end near Chakratīrtha, His elongated form lying upon the moist sand. The devotees’ eyes were filled with joy as they glanced upon the delicate and serene figure of their Lord. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="60">
<h5>60</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ārta-bhakta-kaṇṭha-kṛṣṇa-nāma-karṇa-hṛdgataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>lagna-sandhi-suṣṭhu-deha-sarva-pūrva-sammatam</strong><br>
<strong>ardha-bāhya-bhāva-kṛṣṇa-keli-varṇanāturaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the anxious devotees began to loudly chant the Holy Names of Krishna, this divine vibration entered the Lord’s ears and touched the depths of His heart. Immediately the Lord’s dislocated joints reunited, and He displayed the beautiful figure which their eyes were accustomed to relishing. In a semiconscious state, the Lord began to describe with deep feelings of separation what He had seen while absorbed in divine trance. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yamunambu-krishna-radhikali-keli-mandalam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="61">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Premadhāma-deva-stotram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Friday</h3>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>Edited by Swāmī Bhakti Pāvan Janārdan</h4>
<h5>61</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yāmunāmbu-kṛṣṇa-rādhikāli-keli-maṇḍalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vyakta-gupta-dṛpta-tṛpta-bhaṅgi-mādanākulam</strong><br>
<strong>gūḍha-divya-marma-moda-mūrchanā-chamatkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the sacred waters of the Yamunā in Vṛndāvan, Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, along with Their dearmost cowherd-maiden friends, perform various charming water-sport Pastimes. These divine Pastimes are sometimes revealed, sometimes hidden, sometimes in opposition to each other, and sometimes harmonious. In this way the Lord’s Pastimes are couched in different gestures which bring about different types of divine hankering. The melodious tune of this confidential spiritual treasure which astounds the entire universe was distributed by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="62">
<h5>62</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āsya-gharṣaṇādi-chāṭakādri-sindhu-līlanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-marma-bhedi-tīvra-duḥkha-saukya-khelanam</strong><br>
<strong>atyachintya-divya-vaibhavāśritaika-śaṅkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Upon glancing at the Chāṭaka hill, intolerable feelings of separation overwhelmed the Lord, and different Pastimes in the mood of profound adoration were incited, whereby the Lord began to rub His beautiful face on the ground. When Śrī Gaurāṅga would remember the water-sport Pastimes of Lord Krishna, He would jump into the ocean and exhibit the divine symptoms of one intoxicated by pure love. By performing these Pastimes, the Lord would inundate the devotees’ hearts with waves from the grand ocean of love of Krishna. Those desperate waves carried both transcendental anxiety and joy up onto the shore of the devotees’ hearts. Such inconceivable auspiciousness was bestowed only upon the Lord’s most surrendered associates. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="63">
<h5>63</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrotra-netra-gatyatīta-bodha-rodhitādbhutaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-labhya-bhāva-siddha-chetanā-chamatkṛtam</strong><br>
<strong>brahma-śambhu-veda-tantra-mṛgya-satya-sundaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is not only beyond the mundane experiences of sight and sound, but He also arrests all intellectual endeavors to know Him. Being situated in pure love of Godhead, He remains a mystery and astonishes even those living entities who are fixed in self-realization. The revealed Vedas of Lord Brahmā and the Tantric literatures of Lord Shiva are simply searching after Śrī Gaurasundar, reality the beautiful. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="64">
<h5>64</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vipra-śūdra-vijña-mūrkha-yāvanādi-nāmadaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vitta-vikramocha-nīcha-sajjanaika-sampadam</strong><br>
<strong>strī-pumādi-nirvivāda-sārvavādikoddharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By freely distributing the Holy Name of Krishna, Śrī Gaurāṅgadev purified the scholarly intellectual class, the labourer class, the illiterate, and even the lowest sections of society. The Lord alone was the embodiment of the greatest wealth for all sincere and gentle souls, both the rich and the poor, the aristocratic and the lowborn. Therefore, Lord Chaitanya is respected and honoured by men and women alike as the saviour of all, in both the mundane and spiritual worlds. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="65">
<h5>65</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sindhu-śūnya-veda-chandra-śāka-kumbha-pūrṇimā</strong><br>
<strong>sāndhya-chāndrakoparāga-jāta-gaura-chandramā</strong><br>
<strong>snāna-dāna-kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅga-tat-parātparaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like a golden moon, Śrī Gaurachandra appeared in the year 1407 Śakābda (1486 A.D.), at the onset of a lunar eclipse on a full-moon evening in the month of March. When the Absolute Truth Śrī Gaurāṅgadev incarnated in the beautiful courtyard of Śachī Devī, the residents of Māyāpur were bathing in the sacred Ganges, congregationally chanting the Holy Names of Hari, and making offerings of various types of jewels to the Lord. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="66">
<h5>66</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-siddha-sāvalīla-pūrṇa-saukhya-lakṣaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>svānubhāva-matta-nṛtya-kīrtanātma-vaṇṭanam</strong><br>
<strong>advayaika-lakṣya-pūrṇa-tattva-tat-parātparaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is the all-conquering conclusion: the highest conception of the Ultimate Reality must also be the highest form of <i>ānanda</i>, ecstasy. Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is Krishna, ecstasy Himself, tasting His own sweetness and dancing in ecstatic joy. His own Holy Name is the cause of His ecstasy, expressed as dancing, and the Holy Name is the effect of His ecstasy, expressed as chanting. The cause is the effect. The dynamo is creating ecstatic energy which makes Him dance, and His chanting distributes that ecstasy to others. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="67">
<h5>67</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-purīśvarānukampi-labdha-dīkṣa-daivataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>keśavākhya-bhāratī-sakāśa-keśa-rakṣitam</strong><br>
<strong>mādhavānudhī-kiśora-kṛṣṇa-sevanādaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Īśvara Purī became very fortunate when Lord Gaurāṅga exhibited His causeless mercy by accepting initiation from him. Śrī Keśava Bhāratī was similarly favoured in that in his presence, Lord Gaurāṅga shaved His beautiful curly hair and accepted from him the garments of a renunciate. Lord Chaitanya gave undivided attention to Śrī Mādhavendra Purī, stating that his attachment for serving Lord Krishna’s <i>kiśora</i> form in conjugal love was the highest expression of dedication. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="68">
<h5>68</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sindhu-bindu-veda-chandra-śāka-phālgunoditaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nyāsa-soma-netra-veda-chandra-śāka-bodhitam</strong><br>
<strong>vāṇa-vāṇa-veda-chandra-śāka-lochanāntaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respects unto Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who rose like a golden moon in Śrī Māyāpur in 1407 Śakābda, revealed the pastime of accepting the garments of the renounced order, <i>sannyās</i>, in 1431 Śakābda in order to benedict the entire material universe, and disappeared beyond the ordinary vision of the people of this world in 1455 Śakābda. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="69">
<h5>69</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-svarūpa-rāya-saṅga-harṣa-śeṣa-ghoṣaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śikṣaṇāṣṭakākhya-kṛṣṇa-kīrtanaika-poṣaṇam</strong><br>
<strong>prema-nāma-mātra-viśva-jīvanaika-sambharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With great joy Śrī Gaurasundar told His dearmost associates Śrī Swarūp Dāmodar and Rāmānanda Rāya that the process of congregationally chanting the Holy Names of Krishna bestows the highest benediction upon the living entities in this age of Kali:<br>
<i>harṣe prabhu kohena, “śuno svarūpa-rāma-rāya</i><br /><i>nāma saṅkīrtana kalau, parama upāya”</i><br>
In His famous composition Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam also, Lord Gaurāṅga has placed the chanting of Lord Krishna’s Names on the topmost level of importance. The Supreme Lord further taught that chanting Lord Krishna’s Names in pure love of Godhead perfectly nourishes and fully satisfies all the living entities in the universe. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Krishnaprema .</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="70">
<h5>70</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prema hema-deva dehi-dāsareṣa manyatāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kṣamyatāṁ mahāparādha-rāśireṣa-gaṇyatām</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-kiṅkareṣu rāmānanda-dāsa-sambharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my Golden Lord! (<i>Suvarṇa-varṇa hemāṅga</i>) Oh ocean of <i>prema</i>! Kindly grant me the wealth of Your divine love! Please give some slight attention to this fallen soul and pardon his unlimited offenses. There are so many servants of Śrī Rūpa who are so dear to You. My only aspiration is that You count me as one of those servitors. Oh Gaurasundar, You are the sole protector and embodiment of good fortune for me, Rāmānanda Dās. I offer my obeisances to you, Oh Gaurāṅga Sundar, my most beautiful Golden Lord, the divine form of Krishna-<i>prema</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="71">
<h5>71</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saśraddhaḥ sapta-daśakaṁ prema-dhāmeti-nāmakam</strong><br>
<strong>stavaṁ ko ’pi paṭhan gauraṁ rādhā-śyāma-mayaṁ vrajet</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whoever faithfully recites this composition of seventy prayers known as the Premadhāma-deva-stotram will be granted the opportunity to serve that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, who is none other than Śyāmasundar Himself covered by the heart and halo (<i>bhāva</i> and <i>kānti</i>) of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="72">
<h5>72</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pañchame śata-gaurābde śrī-siddhānta-sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdharaḥ ko ’pi tach chiṣyas tridaṇḍī-nauti-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">These prayers have been composed in the fifth century after the appearance of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu by a certain <i>sannyās</i> disciple of Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur named Śrīdhar [Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj].</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Songs to Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-nadiya-godrume-nityananda-mahajana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ājñā-Ṭahal <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-godrume nityānanda mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>pātiyāche nāma-haṭṭa jivera kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the land of Nadīyā, on the island of Godruma, the magnanimous Lord Nityānanda has opened up the Marketplace of the Holy Name, meant for the deliverance of all fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(śraddhāvān jon he, śraddhāvān jon he)<br>
<strong>prabhura ājñāya, bhāi, māgi ei bhikṣā</strong><br>
<strong>bolo ‘kṛṣṇa,’ bhajo kṛṣṇa, kara kṛṣṇa-śikṣā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O people of faith! O people of faith! By the order of Lord Gaurāṅga, O brothers, I beg these three requests of you: Chant “Krishna!”, worship Krishna, and teach others about Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aparādha-śūnya ho’ye loha kṛṣṇa-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa mātā, kṛṣṇa pita, kṛṣṇa dhana-prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being careful to remain free of offenses, just take the holy name of Lord Krishna. Krishna is your mother, Krishna is your father, and Krishna is the treasure of your life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇera saṁsāra kara chāḍi’ anāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>jīve dayā, kṛṣṇa-nāma – sarva-dharma-sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Giving up all sinful activities, carry on your worldly duties only in relation to Lord Krishna. The showing of compassion to other souls by loudly chanting the holy name of Krishna is the essence of all forms of religion.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nitai-pada-kamala-koti-chandra-sushitala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Manaḥ-Śikṣā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi-pada-kamala,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koṭī-chandra-suśītala,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;je chāyāya jagata juḍāy</strong><br>
<strong>heno nitāi bine bhāi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa pāite nāi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dṛḍha kori dharo nitāira pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The holy lotus feet of Nitāi,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;cooling like ten million moons —<br />In the shade of which<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the world gains soothing relief;<br />Without that Nitāi, O brother,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;No one can reach Rādhā and Krishna —<br />Clasp His lotus feet with full conviction.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se sambandha nāhi jāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛthā janma gelo tāra,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei paśu boḍo durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>nitāi nā bolilo mukhe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;majilo saṁsāra-sukhe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vidyā-kule ki koribe tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who never got His connection,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;passed his life in vain —<br />Such an animal is so very wicked;<br />He whose mouth never uttered ‘Nitāi,’<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;merged in mundane pleasures —<br />How can his aristocratic lineage save him?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ahaṅkāre matta hoiyā, nitāi-pada pāsariyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asatyere satya kori māni</strong><br>
<strong>nitāiyer karuṇā habe, vraje rādhā-kṛṣṇa pābe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharo nitāiyer charaṇ du’ khāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Maddened with pride,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;forgetting the lotus feet of Nitāi —<br />I embrace untruth as truth;<br />But when Nitāi gives His Grace<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you’ll reach Rādhā and Krishna in Vraja —<br />O embrace the lotus feet of Nitāi!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāiyer charaṇa satya, tā̐hāra sevaka nitya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-pada sadā kara āśa</strong><br>
<strong>narottama boḍo duḥkhī, nitāi more kara sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākho rāṅgā-charaṇera pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The lotus feet of Nitāi are truth,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;His servitors are eternal —<br />Ever aspire for the shelter of His feet;<br />Narottam is very sad — O Nitāi make me happy —<br />Please keep me at Your lovely lotus feet!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dhana-mora-nityananda-pati-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sva-Niṣṭha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhana mora nityānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pati mora gaurachandra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa mora jugala-kiśora</strong><br>
<strong>advaita āchārya bala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gadādhara mora kula</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narahari vilasai mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda is my wealth. Lord Gaurachandra is my master. The youthful Divine Couple is my life. Advaita Āchārya is my strength. Gadādhara is my family. Narahari Sarakara is my glory.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera pada-dhūli&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe mora snāna-keli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarpaṇa mora vaiṣṇavera nāma</strong><br>
<strong>vichāra koriyā mane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-rasa āsvādane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhyastha śrī-bhāgavata purāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The dust of the devotees’ lotus feet is my bathing water. The chanting of the devotees’ names is my satisfaction. Considering the merits of all Vedic literatures in the light of devotional service, I have concluded that the Śrīmad Bhāgavatam is the best of all scriptures.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera uchiṣṭa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe mora mano-niṣṭha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇavera nāmete ullāsa</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvane chaturā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe mora mano-gherā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kohe dīne narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind is firmly convinced of the spiritual benefit obtained by eating the remnants of foodstuff left by the devotees. The names of the devotees are my happiness. The land of Vṛndāvan is the enclosure within which I keep my mind. Poor-hearted Narottam Dās speaks in this way.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nitai-gunamani-amara-nitai-gunamani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nitāi guṇamaṇi āmāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi guṇamaṇi āmāra nitāi guṇamaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>āniyā premera vanyā bhāsāla abanī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord Nityānanda, the jewel of all good qualities; O Nitāi, the jewel of all good qualities has brought a flood of ecstatic love of Godhead that has drowned the whole world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>premavanyā loye nitāi āila gāuḍa deśe</strong><br>
<strong>ḍubilo bhakata-gaṇa dīnahīna bhāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda has brought this overwhelming deluge of love of Godhead to the land of Bengal on Lord Chaitanya’s order. The devotees have been inundated by this deluge but the unfortunate non-devotees have remained floating on that ecstatic ocean.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīnahīna patita pāmara nāhi bāche</strong><br>
<strong>brahmāra durlabha prema sabākāre jāce</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityananda freely offered this rare gift of love of Godhead, which is difficult for even Lord Brahmā to attain, to the fallen and wretched souls who were not desirous of receiving it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ābaddha karuṇā-sindhu kāṭiyā muhāna</strong><br>
<strong>ghore ghore bule prema-amiyāra bāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The ocean of mercy had formerly been tightly sealed, but Lord Nitāi cut a channel in its boundary to allow the great flooding waves of ecstatic love to wash down from house to house.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lochan bole heno nitāi jebā nā bhajilo</strong><br>
<strong>jāniyā śuniyā sei ātmaghātī hoilo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lochan Dās says, “Whoever has not worshipped this Lord Nitāi, or accepted the great gift offered by Him, that person knowingly commits suicide.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-akrodha-paramananda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Akrodha paramānanda <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>akrodha paramānanda nityānanda rāy</strong><br>
<strong>abhimāna-śūnya nitāi nagare beḍāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great personality, Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, is free from anger and full of transcendental ecstasy. He wanders throughout the towns, free from false pride and arrogance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhama patita jīver dvāre dvāre giyā</strong><br>
<strong>harināma mahāmantra dena bilāiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He travels from door to door to the homes of the fallen souls, freely distributing the Hare Krishna mahāmantra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāre dekhe tāre kohe dante tṛṇa kori</strong><br>
<strong>āmāre kiniyā loho bhaja gaurahari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With straw between His teeth, He begs all who cross his path, “Worship Lord Gaurahari and you will purchase me.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eta boli’ nityānanda bhūme goḍi jāy</strong><br>
<strong>sonāra parvata jena dhūlāte loṭāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Saying this Nityānanda falls to the ground, appearing like a golden mountain rolling in the dust.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>heno avatāre jāra rati nā janmilo</strong><br>
<strong>lochan bole sei pāpī elo āra gelo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where attachment for such an incarnation has not arisen, Lochan Dās says that such a sinful person comes and goes in the cycle of repeated birth and death.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-parama-karuna-pahu-dui-jana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nityānander Dayā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>parama karuṇa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pahu̐ dūi jana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi gaurachandra</strong><br>
<strong>saba avatāra-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sāra śiromaṇi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala ānanda-kanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nitāi and Lord Gaurachandra, are the two most merciful lords. They are the crest-jewels of all incarnations and the only source of transcendental ecstatic joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja bhaja bhāi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaitanya nitāi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sudṛḍha biśvāsa kori’</strong><br>
<strong>viṣoya chāḍiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se rase majiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukhe bolo hari hari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! I implore you to worship Śrī Chaitanya-Nitāi with firm faith. Give up your attachment to sense gratification and merge into this nectar by chanting the Names of Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dekho ore bhāi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tri-bhuvane nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emona dayāla dātā</strong><br>
<strong>paśu pākhī jhure,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṣāṇa vidore,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuni jāra guṇa-gāthā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Look, O brother! There are no benefactors in the three worlds who are as merciful as Them. Even birds and animals are fulfilled and stones melt, by hearing the glories of Their qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāre majiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rohili poḍiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se pade nahilo āśa</strong><br>
<strong>āpana karama,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuñjāye śamana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kohoye lochana-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Absorbed in the cycle of birth and death, having fallen by the wayside without any hope for achieving Their company, the lord of death will come and make you suffer the results of your activities—so sings Lochan Dās.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dayal-nitai-chaitanya-bole-nach-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dayāl Nitāi Chaitanya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘dayāl nitāi chaitanya’ bo’le nāch re āmār man</strong><br>
<strong>nāch re āmār man, nāch re āmār man</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my mind, just dance! Oh my mind, just dance! Oh my mind, please dance, chanting, “Dayāl Nitāi Chaitanya!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(emon dayāl to nāi he, mār kheye prema dey)<br>
(tabe)<strong> aparādha dūre jābe, pābe prema-dhan</strong><br>
(aparādhera-vichāra to nāi he)<br>
<strong>takhon kṛṣṇa-nāme ruchi habe, ghuchibe bandhan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Such a merciful personality as Nityānanda Prabhu is not to be found anywhere. He suffers a beating from Jagāi and Mādhāi and still gives them love of God.<br>
When you become offenceless, you will obtain love of God; but in these Names of Chaitanya and Nitāi there is no consideration of offences. Once you have a taste for the Holy Name of Krishna, bondage to this world will come to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse">(anurāg to habe he)<br>
<strong>tokhon anāyāse saphal habe jīvera jīvan</strong><br>
(kṛṣṇa-rati vinā jīvan to miche he)<br>
<strong>śeṣe vṛndāvane rādhā-śyāmer pābe daraśan</strong><br>
(gaura-kṛpā hole he)</p>
<p class="translation">When there is attachment to the Holy Name of Krishna, then, very easily, the life of a living being becomes successful. Without attachment to Krishna, life is simply false. If the mercy of Lord Gaura is there, then at the end of life you will obtain the beautiful vision of Rādhā and Śyāma in Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Songs to Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-gaurangera-duti-pada-jar-dhana-sampada" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sāvaraṇa-Śrī-Gaura-mahimā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera duṭi pada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jār dhana sampada,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jāne bhakati-rasa-sār</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅgera madhura-līlā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jār karṇe praveśilā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdoya nirmala bhelo tār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Anyone who has accepted the two lotus feet of Lord Chaitanya can understand the true essence of devotional service. Whoever hears of the sweet Pastimes of Lord Chaitanya, their heart will become pure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je gaurāṅgera nāma loy,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra hoy premodoy,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre mui jāi bolihāri</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga-guṇete jhure,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitya-līlā tāre sphure,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jana bhakati-adhikārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In one who takes the Holy Name of Gaurāṅga, there is an awakening of love of Godhead. To such a person I say, “Bravo, excellent!” If one appreciates the transcendental qualities of Lord Gaurāṅga, the eternal Pastimes manifest in their heart. Such a person has real devotional qualification.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera saṅgi-gaṇe, nitya-siddha kori māne,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jāy vrajendra-suta-pāś</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gauḍa-maṇḍala-bhūmi, jebā jāne chintāmaṇi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra hoy vraja-bhūme bās</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By considering the associates of Lord Gaurāṅga to be eternally perfect, one can attain the association of Śrī Krishna in Vraja Dhām. Whoever considers the land of Lord Gaurāṅga, Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām to be the land of transcendental touchstone, that person takes residence in Vraja Dhām, Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-prema-rasārṇave,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se taraṅge jebā ḍube,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se rādhā-mādhava-antaraṅga</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhe vā vanete thāke,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hā gaurāṅga’ bo’le ḍāke,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama māge tāra saṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whoever dives into the waves of the nectarean ocean of love of Lord Gaurāṅga becomes an intimate associate of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. Whether one lives in a house or in the forest, if they chant ‘Oh Lord Gaurāṅga!’ Narottam Dās begs to attain their association.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaura-pahu-na-bhajiya-mainu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gaurā Pahu̐ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurā pahu̐ nā bhajiyā mainu</strong><br>
<strong>prema ratana-dhana helāya hārāinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Gaura! I have not worshiped You and have been robbed of the jewel of divine love by my own negligence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhane jatana kori dhana teyā-ginu</strong><br>
<strong>āpana karama doṣe āpani ḍuvinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although I am in need of Your wealth, I have endeavoured to reject Your riches and instead I have dived into sinful material activity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sat-saṅga chāḍi koinu asate vilāsa</strong><br>
<strong>te-kāraṇe lāgilo je karma-bandha-phāṅsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Avoiding the association of saintly persons, I have enjoyed sporting with materialistic people. For that reason I have become bound in the noose of fruitive activity and its reactions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣoya-biṣama-biṣa satata khāinu</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-kīrtana rase magana nā hoinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have perpetually drunk the horrible poison of material sense gratification and have never become absorbed in the sweet nectar of the chanting of Lord Gaura’s Names.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>keno bā āchaye prāṇa ki sukha lāgiyā</strong><br>
<strong>narottama dāsa keno nā gelo moriyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O why has my heart not experienced any satisfaction and why has Narottam Dās not simply perished?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-avatara-sara-gora-avatara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Avatāra Sāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avatāra sāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno nā bhajili tā̐re</strong><br>
<strong>kori nīre vāsa gelo nā piyāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana karama phere</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The incarnation of Śrī Gaura is the most excellent of all incarnations. Why haven’t I worshipped Him? It is as if I was surrounded by water but denied myself a drink, returning to my own fruitive activity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kaṇṭa-kera taru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadāi sebili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amṛta pā’bāra āśe</strong><br>
<strong>prema-kalpataru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī gaurāṅga āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāre bhabili biṣe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I chewed the thorns of the tree of worldly life and considered the blood that flowed through my mouth to be nectar. When offered the fruits of the wish-fulfilling tree of ecstatic love of Lord Gaurāṅga, I rejected them, thinking them to be poison.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saura-bhera āśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;palāśa śuṅkili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāsāte paśilo kīṭa</strong><br>
<strong>‘ikṣu-daṇḍa’ bhāvi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāṭha chuṣili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone pāibi miṭha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Desirous of smelling a sweet scent, I sniffed at what I thought to be a fragrant flower. Alas, that flower was odourless like the palāśa flower and instead an insect flew up my nose and bit me. Thinking material life to be sweet like sugarcane, I tried to taste its nectar. Instead, it was like chewing dry wood. In this way all my attempts at enjoyment were false.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘hāra’ boliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;golāya parili (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śamana kiṅkora sāpa</strong><br>
<strong>‘śītala’ boliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āguna pohāli (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāili bajara-tāpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Admitting defeat, wasted and worn out, I await the snake of death. Declaring it to be cool and soothing, I have embraced the fire of material life, only to suffer intense misery, as if struck by lightning.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāra bhajili&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaurāṅga bhulili</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā śunili sādhura kathā</strong><br>
<strong>iha-parakāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dukāla khoyāli (mana)</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāili āpana māthā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Worshipping my family and my material life, I forgot Lord Gaurāṅga and didn’t listen to the instructions of the saints. Now in my final days, I realise I have died twice, for not only am I leaving this mortal body, but I am also dead while living, having wasted my life in material indulgence.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gay-gora-madhur-svare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāy gorā madhur svare</strong><br>
<strong>hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa, kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma, rāma rāma hare hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurasundar sings in a very sweet voice, “Hare Krishna, Hare Krishna, Krishna Krishna, Hare Hare, Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gṛhe thāko, vane thāko, sadā ‘hari’ bole’ ḍāko,</strong><br>
<strong>sukhe duḥkhe bhulo nāko, vadane harinām kara re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whether you live at home or in the forest always chant the Lord’s Holy Name; in happiness or sadness, don’t forget — chant the Holy Name and fill your lips with <i>Harinām</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyā-jāle baddha ho’ye, ācho miche kāja lo’ye,</strong><br>
<strong>ekhona o chetana peye, rādhā-mādhava-nām bolo re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bound in <i>māyā’s</i> net you’ve slaved and toiled in vain. But now that you have a human life and consciousness, chant the Lord’s Name, “Rādhā-Mādhava.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana hoilo śeṣa, nā bhajile hṛṣīkeśa</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktivinod-upadeśa, ekbār nām-rase māto re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This life must surely end soon, and you have not worshiped Hṛṣīkeśa, the master of the senses. Bhakti Vinod advises: at least once, taste the <i>nāma-rasa</i>, the nectar of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-gaura-vane-suradhuni-tate" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Siddhi-Lālasā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe gaura-vane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suradhunī-taṭe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hā rādhe hā kṛṣṇa’ bo’le</strong><br>
<strong>kāṅdiyā beḍābo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deho-sukha chāḍi’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā latā-taru-tole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O when, O when will this soul chant in Gaura-forest on the Ganges banks the Holy Names “Rādhā,” and “Krishna” renouncing all the body’s joys and drenched with all the tears I’ve wept amidst the herbs, beneath a tree.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śva-pacha-gṛhete,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māgiyā khāibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pibo sarasvatī-jala</strong><br>
<strong>puline puline&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaḍā-gaḍi dibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kori’ kṛṣṇa-kolāholo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, at some outcaste’s home shall I give up all thoughts of caste and beg to share with him humble <i>prasādam</i> and drink the water of the Saraswatī? Along the banks, my voice choked with my ecstasy I’ll chant “Krishna” in great delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhāma-vāsī jane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praṇati koriyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māgibo kṛpāra leśa</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;reṇu gāya mākhi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhari’ avadhūta-veśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And when will I bow down before a resident of the holy <i>dhām</i> and beg from him a drop of mercy; when Oh when will I smear my body with dust from a Vaiṣṇava’s holy feet and wear the mendicant’s cloth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍa-vraja-bane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bheda nā heribo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoibo varaja-vāsī</strong><br>
<strong>dhāmera svarūpa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sphuribe nayane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoibo rādhāra dāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then I shall see no difference between the forest of Gauḍa and Vraja, and I will be transformed into a resident of the <i>dhām</i>. The true nature of the Lord’s abode will manifest itself to my eyes, and I will become a maidservant of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kali-ghora-timire-garasala-jagajana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Kali-ghora timire <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Nayanānanda Dās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kali-ghora timire&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;garasala jagajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharama karama bahu-dūra</strong><br>
<strong>asādhane chintāmaṇi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vidhi milāola āni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā boḍo dayāla ṭhākura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The people of this world have now been devoured by the dense darkness of this age of Kali and all good activities have gone far away. Thus they are apathetic to the transcendental gems offered by the greatly merciful Lord Gaura.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi re bhāi!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā guṇa kohone nā jāya</strong><br>
<strong>koto śata-ānana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koto chatur-ānana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;boroṇiyā ora nāhi pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O my brother! O my brother! No one has told you the glories of Lord Gaura. How great is Lord Brahmā? How great is Lord Śeṣa? They cannot eclipse the greatness of Lord Gaura.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chāri veda ṣaḍ-dara-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śana kori adhyayana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jadi gaurāṅga nāhi bhaje</strong><br>
<strong>vṛthā tāra adhyayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lochana vihīna jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;darpaṇe andhe kibā kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“What use are the four Vedas and the six systems of philosophy if one does not worship Lord Gaurāṅga? Why study them? What use is a mirror to a blind man?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>veda vidyā dui&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichui nā jānata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jadi gaurāṅga jāne sāra</strong><br>
<strong>nayanānanda bhone&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei to sakali jāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarva-siddhi karatale tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“If one knows the two kinds of Vedic knowledge, he still does not know anything. But if one knows Lord Gaurāṅga, he knows the most precious knowledge. Nayanānanda says: Such a person knows everything. All perfections stay in the palm of his hand.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jadi-gaura-na-hoto-tobe-ki-hoito" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Jadi Gaura nā ho’to <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vāsudeva Ghoṣa</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jadi gaura nā ho’to,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tobe ki hoito,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone dhoritām de</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra mahimā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-rasa-sīmā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagate jānāta ke?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If Lord Gaura had not come, then what would have become of us? How could we have maintained our lives? If He had not come, then how could we know of the glories of Śrī Rādhā and the topmost limit of ecstatic love of Krishna?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhura vṛndā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vipina mādhurī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praveśa chāturī sāra</strong><br>
<strong>varaja-yuvatī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvera bhakati,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śakati hoito kāra?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who would have had the capacity to render ecstatic devotional service, following the mood of the damsels of Vraja? Indeed, the clever expertise of the Vraja-gopīs is essential for entering the supremely sweet forest of Vṛndā, Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāo punaḥ punaḥ,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaurāṅgera guṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarala koriyā mana</strong><br>
<strong>e bhava-sāgare,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emona dayāla,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā dekhiye eka-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sing again and again the wonderful qualities of Lord Gaurāṅga while keeping your heart simple. In the ocean of this material world, not a single person has ever seen such a magnanimous and merciful Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅga boliyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā genu goliyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone dhorinu de</strong><br>
<strong>vāsur hiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṣāṇa diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone goḍiyāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even though I am chanting ‘Gaurāṅga!’ somehow I have not melted in ecstatic love. So how have I maintained the burden of this body? How is it that the creator has given this Vāsu a stone in place of his heart?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-emona-gauranga-bine-nahi-ara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Emona Gaurāṅga bine nāhi āra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Premānanda Dās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emona gaurāṅga bine nāhi āra</strong><br>
<strong>heno avatāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hobe ki hoyeche</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heno prema parachāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No one is like Lord Gaurāṅga! Will there ever be an incarnation like Him, an incarnation that preached ecstatic love of Godhead as He did?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>duramati ati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita pāṣaṇḍī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇe nā mārilo kore</strong><br>
<strong>harināma diye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdoya śodhilo</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jachi giyā ghore ghore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He did not kill the fallen wicked-hearted blasphemers. Instead He went from house to house visiting them. He begged them to chant Lord Krishna’s Holy Names. He gave them the Holy Names and purified their hearts.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhava biriñchira&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāñchito je prema</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagata phelilo ḍhāli</strong><br>
<strong>kāṅgāle pāiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khā-ilo nāchiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bājāiye karatāli</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To the world He freely gave the rare gift of ecstatic love of Godhead, a gift even Brahmā and Shiva attain only with great difficulty. Attaining that gift, the poor people of this world danced and clapped their hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāsiye kā̐diye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme gaḍāgaḍi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulake byāpilo aṅga</strong><br>
<strong>chaṇḍāle brāhmaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kore kolākuli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobe bā chilo e raṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They laughed and wept. Overcome with ecstasy, they rolled on the ground. The hairs of their bodies stood erect. The <i>brāhmaṇs</i> and <i>chaṇḍāls</i> embraced. There was great bliss.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ḍākiye hā̐kiye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khol karatāle</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāiye dhāiye phire</strong><br>
<strong>dekhiyā śamana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarāsa pāiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kapāṭa hānilo dvāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Loudly they called out the Holy Names playing the <i>mṛdaṅga</i> and <i>karatāls</i>. They sang, ran, and danced in a circle. Seeing all this, peaceful composure became afraid, fled, and knocked on people’s doors.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e tina bhuvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande bhorilo</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uṭhilo maṅgala sora</strong><br>
<strong>kohe premānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emona gaurāṅga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rati nā janmilo mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">An auspicious tumult arose. The three worlds were filled with bliss. Premānanda says: “Alas! Attachment for Lord Gaurāṅga did not arise in me.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ke-jabi-ke-jabi-bhai-bhava-sindhu-para" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ke jabi ke jabi <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ke jabi ke jabi bhāi bhava-sindhu pāra</strong><br>
<strong>dhanya kali-yuge re chaitanya avatāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who will go, oh brother, who will go to cross the ocean of material existence? The wealth and great fortune of this age of Kali is the incarnation of Lord Chaitanya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra gaurāṅgera ghāṭe ujānakheyā boy</strong><br>
<strong>kaḍipāti nāhi lāge amani pāra hoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My Lord Gaurāṅga stands at the jetty ready to ferry his servants upstream across this material existence. For no charge he is immediately taking them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>harināmera tarīkhāni śrī-guru-kāṇḍārī</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana-keroyāla du’bahu pasāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The ferry-boat is the Holy Names of the Lord and the spiritual master is the captain. The oars are the outstretched arms in the <i>saṅkīrtan</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-jīva uddhāra hoilo premera bātāse</strong><br>
<strong>lochana paḍiyā roilo karamera doṣe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The favourable wind is divine love for Lord Krishna and all the fallen souls have been rescued on this boat. Only Lochan Dās is left behind due to the faults of his own misdeeds.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vimala-hemajin-tanu-anupama-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vimala hemajini <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vimala hemajin&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tanu anupama re!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe śobhe nānā phuladāma</strong><br>
<strong>kadamba keśara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jini ektī pulaka re!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra mājhe bindu bindu ghāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His peerless form defeats the purest gold. His form is decorated with a garland of many kinds of flowers. His form is covered with hairs standing erect, hairs that defeat the filaments of <i>kadamba</i> flowers. His form is covered with drop after drop of perspirations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jini’ madamatta hāti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gamana manthara ati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvāveśe ḍhuli ḍhuli jāya</strong><br>
<strong>aruṇa-vasana chhobi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jini prabhātera robi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā-aṅge laharī khelāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His slow walking defeats the walking of intoxicated elephants. Overcome with ecstatic love, He staggers and staggers as He walks. His saffron garments defeat the beautiful colour of the rising sun. His fair limbs play in the waves of spiritual pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chalite nā pāre gorā-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chā̐d go̐sāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bolite nā pāre ādha-bola</strong><br>
<strong>bhāvete āveśa hoiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari hari bolāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchaṇḍāle dhari’ deya kola</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Overcome, Lord Gauracandra has no power to walk. Now He has no power to speak. Half words are all He speaks. Overcome with ecstatic love, He chants, “Hari! Hari!” Everyone, even the lowest outcaste, He embraces in His arms.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e sukha-sampada-kāle&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā nā bhajinu hele</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heno pade nā korinu āśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-chandra ṭhākura śrī-nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guṇa gāya vṛndāvana dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alas! At that blissful fortunate time I did not worship Lord Gaura. I did not yearn to attain His feet. In this way Vṛndāvan das sings the glories of Lord Caitanyachandra and Lord Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gauranga-bolite-hobe-pulaka-shorira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>‘Gaurāṅga’ bolite habe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘gaurāṅga’ bolite hobe pulaka-śorīra</strong><br>
<strong>‘hari hari’ bolite nayane ba’be nīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will there be shivering of the body upon chanting Lord Gaurāṅga’s Holy Name? When will there be tears in my eyes while chanting the Lord’s Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āra kabe nitāi-chā̐da karuṇā koribe</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-vāsanā more kabe tucha ha’be</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I obtain the mercy of Lord Nītyānanda? When, by His mercy, will my desire for material enjoyment become insignificant?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>biṣoya chāḍiyā kabe śuddha ha’be mana</strong><br>
<strong>kabe hāma herabo śrī-vṛndāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my mind be completely purified, having given up all material desires? When will I attain the vision of Śrī Vṛndāvan Dhām?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-raghunātha-pade hoibe ākuti</strong><br>
<strong>kabe hāma bujhabo se jugala-pirīti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I be eager to follow in the footsteps of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth, and when will I properly understand the loving affairs of the divine couple?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-raghunātha-pade rahu mora āśa</strong><br>
<strong>prārthanā karaye sadā narottama-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My only aspiration is to attain the lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth. Narottam Dās continually submits this prayer.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-aruna-basane-sonara-suraja" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gaurasundarer Avirbhāva Vasare <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Holy Appearance Day of Śrī Gaurasundar</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aruṇa basane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sonara sūraja</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udiche keno re āja</strong><br>
<strong>vasanta suṣamā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ujāri āpanā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ḍhāle keno jagamājha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Why has the Lord appeared suddenly with golden complexion and with golden dress just like the morning sun? Why has He come in the middle of springtime as everything feels so beautiful and wonderful?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>taru gulmalatā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;apūrva baratā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bahe keno phole phule</strong><br>
<strong>bhṛṅga o bihaṅge&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno heno raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṅgīta taraṅga tule</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I cannot understand why, but all the trees, plants and creepers are full of fruits and flowers and they are making song and dance with Mahāprabhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita durjana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno re garjana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ullāse phāṭhiyā poḍe</strong><br>
<strong>vidyā kula dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhimānī jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno mlāna duḥkha bhore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am very fallen and unqualified. Why is this sound vibration coming to me, and why am I getting inspiration through it. Those who have high caste, vast knowledge and material wealth, they have so much ego and pride. Why are they suffering?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ākāśa bātāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghuchāiya trāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśvāse bhāsāye dey</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-jana mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukha vitaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āveśe unmāda hoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sky and the air are so beautiful in the spring season, and they are naturally nourishing and inspiring everybody. In the same way the sound vibration of the devotees spreads everywhere automatically as they distribute Krishna’s Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chaudikete dhvani&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki apūrva śuni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bahujana ucharola</strong><br>
<strong>hare kṛṣṇa rāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma divya-dhāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari hari hari bolo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What a remarkable sound is heard in all directions bringing jubilation to all the people. The devotees chant the divine Names “Hare, Krishna, Rāma,” and the transcendental abode manifests. Chant Hari Hari Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>phālgunī pūrṇimā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hindola raṅgimā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sujana-bhajana rāge</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana sane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;marama gahane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā jāni kibhāva jāge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On His holy appearance day, the festival of Holly takes place. All the devotees are chanting as well as all the Hindus. No-one can understand the feeling and the substance that comes within the heart from that saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sandhyā samāgama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tapana magana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno hema ghana kole</strong><br>
<strong>aparūpa kata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūraba parvata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suvarṇa chandramā bhāle</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the sunset approaches, the sun shines on the mountain illuminating it like it has golden ornaments. This is especially so in the springtime. Then as the moon rises it also beautifies that mountain with its rays.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>suvarṇa chandramā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paśiche nīlimā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se nīla bilīna heme</strong><br>
<strong>ithe kiba bhāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sādhu-jana gāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kalaṅka nā rahe preme</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The blue sky is covered by the Golden Moon and when the devotees glorify the Lord with their discussions, no other conception or motive can exist other than Krishna consciousness. At that time, <i>prema</i>, divine love descends automatically without any impediment.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahājane bole&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;grahaṇera chole</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṅge nāma saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>gaurachandrodaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāpa rāhu kṣaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chandraśobhā prema dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great devotees say, take up your spiritual path with the congregational chanting of the Lord’s Holy Names. With the appearance of Śrī Gaurachandra, all sinfulness is removed and everyone attains the treasure of love of Godhead.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>marmajña sakale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keho kutūhale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nīlimā bilīna chā̐de</strong><br>
<strong>channa avatāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lukāna kāhāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-ruchi-rūpa-chā̐de</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everyone who engages in this nāma saṅkīrtan of Mahāprabhu, comes to enquire sincerely and to know the innermost secret of the Lord’s bluish colour absorbed by his golden features. This is the hidden incarnation of the Lord and the concealed feature of His form is that he is tasting the threefold relish of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ithe heno stuti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-bhāva dyuti</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suvalita śyāmarāo</strong><br>
<strong>udilo gaurāṅga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-prema saṅga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya jaya gorā gāo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Thus I pray to the dark cowherd Śyāmasundar who has taken the mood and effulgence of Śrī Rādhā. That Lord, Śrī Chaitanya appeared with the chanting of the pure divine name; everyone chant the glories of that Golden Lord, Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yadi-te-hari-pada-saroja-sudha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Godrumachandra-bhajanopadeśa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Instructions for Worshiping Lord Chaitanya, the Moon of the Island of Godruma-dvīpa</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur (1893)</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadi te hari-pāda-saroja-sudhā-</strong><br>
<strong>rasa-pāna-paraṁ hṛdayaṁ satatam</strong><br>
<strong>parihṛtya gṛhaṁ kali-bhāva-mayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If your heart wishes to be always absorbed in drinking the ambrosial mellows of the lotus feet of Lord Hari, then give up mundane household life, full of <i>Kali-yuga</i> quarrels and strife, and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhana-yauvana-jīvana-rājya-sukhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>a hi nityam anukṣaṇa-nāśa-param</strong><br>
<strong>tyaja grāmya-kathā-sakalaṁ viphalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Material riches, youth, a long life, and royal happiness—none of these are certain; at every moment they are being destroyed. Give up all useless mundane conversations and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ramaṇī-jana-saṅga-sukhaṁ cha sakhe</strong><br>
<strong>charame bhaya-daṁ puruṣārtha-haram</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-sudhā-rasa-matta-matir</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O friend! The pleasure to be had in the company of beautiful young women ultimately turns to fearfulness, and distracts one from the true goal of human life. With your mind intoxicated by the nectarean mellows of the holy name of Lord Hari, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaḍa-kāvya-raso na hikvya-rasaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kali-pāvana-gaura-raso hi rasaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>alam anya-kathādy-anuśīlanayā</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The taste of mundane poetry never provides the sweet taste of actual poetry. The sweet mellows of true poetry are verily found in descriptions of Lord Gaura, the savior of all souls in the <i>Kali-yuga</i>. The diligent study of all other topics is useless! Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutānvita-vāma-tanuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yamunā-taṭa-nāgara-nanda-sutam</strong><br>
<strong>muralī-kala-gīta-vinoda-paraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His  left  side  enhanced  by  the  daughter  of Vṛṣabhānu, Nanda-Suta the transcendental paramour stands on the bank of the Yamunā river absorbed in the pleasure-giving pastime of playing soft, melodious songs on His flute. Just worship Him, the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-kīrtana-madhya-gataṁ sva-janaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>pariveṣṭita-jambunadābha-harim</strong><br>
<strong>nija-gauḍa-janaika-kṛpā-jaladhiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the midst of Hari-<i>kīrtana</i>, surrounded by His loving associates He is the same dark Lord Hari, but now shining with the luster of molten gold. He is the exclusive ocean of mercy for His own Gauḍīya devotees. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>girirāja-sutā-parivīta-gṛhaṁ</strong><br>
