<strong>nava-khaṇḍa-patiṁ yati-chitta-haram</strong><br>
<strong>sura-saṅgha-nutaṁ priyayā sahitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His island home is surrounded by the river Gaṅgā, daughter of the king of mountains. He is the Lord of the nine-fold islands of Navadvīpa and the stealer of the hearts of the renunciates. Along with His beloved wife Viṣṇu Priyā He is glorified by demigods and saintly persons. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kali-kukkura-mudgara-bhāva-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-mahauṣadha-dāna-param</strong><br>
<strong>patitārta-dayārdra-sumūrti-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His mood is that of wielding a stick for chasing away the mad dog of <i>Kali-yuga</i>. He is absorbed in distributing the holy name of Hari, the great remedy for material existence. His heart is melted with compassion for the fallen souls who are suffering, and His transcendental form is very beautiful. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ripu-bāndhava-bheda-vihīna-dayā</strong><br>
<strong>yad abhīkṣṇam udeti mukhābja-tatau</strong><br>
<strong>tam akṛṣṇam iha vraja-rāja-sutam</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Continually beaming from His lotus face is causeless mercy that makes no distinction between so-called ‘friends’ or ‘enemies.’ Although the son of Mahārāja Nanda, He appears here in a form unlike that of Krishna’s dark complexion. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>iha chopaniṣat-parigīta-vibhur</strong><br>
<strong>dvija-rāja-sutaḥ puraṭābha-hariḥ</strong><br>
<strong>nija-dhāmani khelati bandhu-yuto</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this world and in the Upaniṣads He is celebrated as the all-pervading Supreme Lord. He appears now as the son of the king of <i>brāhmaṇs</i> and is Lord Hari Himself, with a shining golden complexion. In His own abode He plays eternally with His cowherd boyfriends. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avatāra-varaṁ paripūrṇa-kalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>para-tattvam ihātma-vilāsam ayam</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-dhāma-rasāmbudhi-gupta-rasaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the most excellent of <i>avatāras</i>, and within Him exists the consummate expansions of Godhead. He is the Supreme Truth, enjoying Himself here in great playfulness. He embodies the most confidential mellows flowing within the ocean of <i>rasa</i> in Vraja Dhāma. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti-varṇa-dhanādi na yasya kṛpā-</strong><br>
<strong>janane balavad-bhajanena vinā</strong><br>
<strong>tam ahaituka-bhāva-pathena sakhe</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O friend! Without the devotional performance of purely intense <i>bhajana</i>, your vast scholarship, noble family lineage, wealth, and so on are completely ineffective for invoking the mercy of the Lord. With a service attitude that is unmotivated, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>api nakra-gatau hrada-madhya-gataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kam amochayad ārta-janaṁ tam ajam</strong><br>
<strong>avichintya-balaṁ śiva-kalpa-taruṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This same Lord once liberated the elephant Gajendra from the crocodile’s grasp within the lake. He is the unborn one, possessing inconceivable power, and is truly a desire-tree of pure auspiciousness. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>surabhīndra-tapaḥ parituṣṭa-manā</strong><br>
<strong>vara-vaṛṇa-dharo harir āvirabhūt</strong><br>
<strong>tam ajasra-sukhaṁ muni-dhairya-haraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being pleased with the penances of Indra and the <i>surabhi</i> cow, Lord Hari has appeared again in this world possessing a superexcellent complexion. He is endless happiness, and He plunders the patience of the learned sages. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>abhilāṣa-chayaṁ tad-abheda-dhiyam</strong><br>
<strong>aśubhaṁ cha śubhaṁ tyaja sarvam ida</strong><br>
<strong>anukūlatayā priya-sevanayā</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The multitude of worldly desires, philosophical meditations on the impersonal oneness of all beings, as well as the relative conceptions of what is auspicious or inauspicious—utterly renounce all of these things. In a favorable mood while rendering loving devotional service, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-sevaka-sevana-dharma-paro</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-rasāmṛta-pāna-rataḥ</strong><br>
<strong>nati-dainya-dayā-para-māna-yuto</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remaining fixed in the service of Hari’s servants, being addicted to drinking the sweet nectar of the holy name of Hari, and endowed with modesty, humility and compassion, offering all respect unto others, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vada yādava mādhava kṛṣṇa hare</strong><br>
<strong>vada rāma janārdana keśava he</strong><br>
<strong>vṛṣabhānusutā-priya-nātha sadā</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Incessantly call out to the Lord, “O Yādava! O Mādhava! O Krishna! O Hari!” Chant, “O Rāma! O Janārdana! O Keśava!” “O beloved Lord of V ṛṣabhānu’s daughter!”—and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vada yāmuna-tīra-vanādri-pate</strong><br>
<strong>vada gokula-kānana-puñja-rave</strong><br>
<strong>vada rāsa-rasāyana gaura-hare</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please chant, “O Lord of the forests and hills along the banks of the river Yamunā!” Chant, “O brilliant sun of the groves of Gokula!” Chant, “O elixir of life for the <i>rāsa</i> dance!” “O Gaurahari!”—and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chala gaura-vanaṁ nava-khaṇḍa-mayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>paṭha gaura-hareś charitāni mudā</strong><br>
<strong>luṭha gaura-padāṅkita-gāṅga-taṭaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please go to the forest of Lord Gaura, divided into nine islands. Please read and recite with great joy the pastimes of Lord Gaurahari. Please roll about in ecstasy on the bank of the river Gaṅgā marked with the footprints of Lord Gaura—and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="20">
<h5>20</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>smara gaura-gadādhara-keli-kalāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhava gaura-gadādhara-pakṣa-charaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śṛṇu gaura-gadādhara-chāru-kathāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just remember the artistically playful pastimes of Gaura and Gadādhara. Just become a loyal follower of Gaura and Gadādhara’s camp. Just listen to the captivating stories of Gaura and Gadādhara—and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Other Songs</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-thakura-vaishnava-pada-avanira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ṭhākura-vaiṣṇava-pada <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭhākura-vaiṣṇava-pada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avanīra susampada,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuno bhāi! hoiya eka mana</strong><br>
<strong>āśroya loiyā bhaje,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre kṛṣṇa nāhi tyaje</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro saba more akāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother, hear my words with rapt attention: The lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas are the most valuable treasure in the world. Take shelter of those Vaiṣṇavas, and worship them. Krishna cannot abandon one who worships Him in this way. All others meet a meaningless death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-jol,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-bhakti dite bol,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro keho nahe balavanta</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-renu,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mastake bhūṣaṇa vinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nāhi bhūṣaṇera anta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The water which has washed the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas bestows devotional service in pure love of Godhead. There is nothing as effective in attaining this divine love. I place the dust from the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas upon my head. I wear no other ornament.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tīrthajol-pavitra-guṇe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;likhiyāche purāṇe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se saba bhaktira pravañchana</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavera pādadoka,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sama nahe ei saba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāte hoya vāñchito pūraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The purifying power of the waters of the various places of pilgrimage is described in the Purāṇas, although this is something of a deceptive trick. Actually there is nothing which is as purifying as the water which has washed the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas. This water fulfills all desires.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-saṅgete mana, ānandita anukṣaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā hoy kṛṣṇa-parasaṅga</strong><br>
<strong>dīna narottama kā̐de, hiyā dhairja nāhi bāndhe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora dośā keno hoilo bhaṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Moment by moment my mind finds constant pleasure in the association of the Vaiṣṇavas. I always seek the company of the devotees of Lord Krishna. Poor-hearted Narottam Dās cries, “I cannot maintain my composure any longer. Why have I fallen into such a low condition of life that I cannot get the association of the Vaiṣṇavas?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-je-anilo-prema-dhana-karuna-prachura" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Viraha-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je ānilo prema-dhana karuṇā prachura</strong><br>
<strong>heno prabhu kothā gelā āchārya-ṭhākura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">That personality who delivered the treasure of prema-bhakti, who was so intense with compassion — where is such a personality to be found as Śrī Āchārya Ṭhākur (Śrīnivās Āchārya)?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐hā mora svarūp rūpa kā̐hā sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐hā dāsa raghunātha patita-pāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where are the saviours of the fallen souls? Where is my Svarūp Dāmodar, and where are Rūpa Goswāmī and Sanātan Goswāmī? Where is Raghunāth Dās to be found?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐hā mora bhaṭṭa-juga kā̐hā kavirāja</strong><br>
<strong>eka-kāle kothā gelā gaurā naṭa-rāja</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where are my Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa and Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmīs? Where am I to find Śrī Krishnadās Kavirāj now? All at once they have gone to join Lord Gaurāṅga, the great dancer.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pāṣāṇe kuṭibo māthā anale paśibo</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga guṇera nidhi kothā gele pābo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To reach such a perfect personality as Lord Chaitanya, I can only break my head against the stone in the anguish of separation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se-saba saṅgīra saṅge je koilo vilāsa</strong><br>
<strong>se-saṅga nā pāyā kānde narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They have all gone off together in their own Pastimes. Narottam Dās Ṭhākur says: “Unable to obtain their association, I must simply weep.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ei-vara-karuna-kara-vaishnava-gosani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vaiṣṇava Vijñāpti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei-vāra karuṇā kara vaiṣṇava gosāñi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana tomā vine keha nāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Vaiṣṇava Goswāmī, please be merciful to me this one time. You are the saviour of the fallen; without you there is no one.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāhāra nikaṭe gele pāpa dūre yāya</strong><br>
<strong>emana dayāla prabhu kevā kothā pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just by being in your presence, sins go far away. Where can anyone find such kindness?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaṅgāra paraśa haile paśchāte pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>darśane pavitra kara ei tomāra guṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Merely by the touch of the waters of the sacred Ganges one becomes liberated, even if he is the lowest of mankind; but just by seeing you, the same effect is achieved. Such is your quality.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-sthāne aparādhe tāre harināma</strong><br>
<strong>tomā-sthāne aparādhe nāhika eḍāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If one commits an offence at the feet of Lord Hari, he can be forgiven if he chants the Holy Name. But if one offends you, there is no salvation for him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra hṛdaye sadā govinda-viśrāma</strong><br>
<strong>govinda kahena mama vaiṣṇava parāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In your heart Govinda is always resting. Śrī Govinda Himself says, “My devotees are My life and soul.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prati janme kari āśā charaṇera dhuli</strong><br>
<strong>narottame kara dayā āpanāra vali</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I hope that in every birth I will obtain the dust of your lotus feet. Śrī Narottam prays, “Please, Oh Vaiṣṇava Goswāmī, be kind unto me.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kripa-kara-vaishnava-thakura" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Prārthanā Lalasāmayi <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpa kara’ vaiṣṇava ṭhākura</strong><br>
<strong>sambandha jāniyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajite bhajite,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhimān hau dūra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please give your mercy to me now, oh revered pure devotee! Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākur! Then only my false ego will go far away by my constant worship in full knowledge of my real eternal position.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘ami to’ vaiṣṇava’ e buddhi hoile,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amānī nā ho’bo āmi</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhāśā āsi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdoya dūṣibe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoibo nirayagāmī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If I falsely think that ‘I am a Vaiṣṇava’, then I shall look forward to receiving respect from others. And if the desire for fame and reputation pollute my heart, then certainly I shall go to live in hell instantly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra kiṅkora,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpane jānibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘guru’-abhimān tyaji’</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra uchiṣṭha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;padajala-reṇu,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā niṣkapaṭe bhaji</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Renouncing the false conception that I am a ‘guru’, I will understand myself to be your humble servant. I sincerely worship the remnants of your food, oh pure devotee, as well as the water that has washed the sacred dust of your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘nije sreṣṭha’ jāni’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchiṣṭhādi dāne,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha’be abhimān bhār</strong><br>
<strong>tāi śiṣya tava,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā sarvadā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā loibo pūjā kā’ra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By giving others the remnants of my food, I shall consider myself superior and shall be burdened with the weight of false pride. Therefore, always remaining your surrendered disciple, I shall not accept worship from anyone else.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>amānī mānada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoile kīrtane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhikār dibe tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;niṣkapaṭe āmi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā luṭibo bhūmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If you will bestow upon me the capacity to chant the Holy Name, I will then give all honour to others without expecting any respect for myself. Thus I will sincerely weep in ecstasy while rolling on the ground at your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-narada-muni-bajaya-vina" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāmāṣṭakam 8 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nārada muni,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bājāya vīṇā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘rādhikā-ramaṇa’-nāme</strong><br>
<strong>nāma amani,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udita hoya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakata-gīta-sāme</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the great soul Nārada Muni plays his stringed vīṇā, the Holy Name of Rādhikā-Ramaṇa descends and immediately appears amidst the kīrtan of the Lord’s devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>amiya-dhārā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bariṣe ghana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śravana-jugale giyā</strong><br>
<strong>bhakata-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saghane nāche,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhoriyā āpana hiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like a monsoon cloud, the Holy Name showers pure nectar into their ears. All the devotees, due to great ecstasy, enthusiastically dance to their heart’s content.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhurī-pūra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsabo paśi’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mātāya jagata-jane</strong><br>
<strong>keho vā kā̐de,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keho vā nāche,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keho māte mane mane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the inhabitants of the universe become maddened upon entering these intoxicating showers of divine sweetness. Some people cry, some dance and others become fully intoxicated within their minds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pañcha-vadana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nārade dhori’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premera saghana rol</strong><br>
<strong>kamalāsana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāchiyā bole,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘bolo bolo hari bolo’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The five-faced Lord Shiva embraces Nārada Muni and repeatedly makes loud screams of ecstatic joy, while Lord Brahmā dances very ecstatically and exclaims, “All of you chant ‘Haribol ! Haribol!’”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sahasrānana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-sukhe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hari hari’ boli gāy</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-prabhāve,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mātilo viśva,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-rasa sabe pāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In supreme happiness the thousand-faced Ananta Śeṣa sings and calls out, “Hari! Hari!” By the influence of the transcendental vibration of that Name, the whole universe becomes ecstatically maddened as everyone tastes and relishes the mellows of the holy name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasane sphuri’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrā’o āmāra āśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpa-pade,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāchaye ihā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakativinoda-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name of Śrī Krishna has fulfilled all my desires by thus manifesting on everyone’s tongue. Bhakti Vinod, the humble servant of the Lord, therefore prays at the feet of Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī that the chanting of Harinām may always continue in this way.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-krishnotkirtana-gana-nartana-parau" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Ṣaḍ-Gosvāmy-aṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Śrīnivās Āchārya</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇotkīrtana-gāna-nartana-parau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premāmṛtāmbho-nidhī</strong><br>
<strong>dhīrādhīra-janapriyau priya-karau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirmatsarau pūjitau</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-chaitanya-kṛpā-bharau bhuvi bhuvo</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhārāvahantārakau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who are always engaged in chanting the Holy Name of Krishna and dancing. They are just like the ocean of love of God, and they are popular both with the gentle and with the ruffians, because they are not envious of anyone. Whatever they do, they are all-pleasing to everyone, and they are fully blessed by Lord Chaitanya. Thus they are engaged in missionary activities meant to deliver all the conditioned souls in the material universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nānā-śāstra-vichāraṇaika-nipuṇau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sad-dharma-saṁsthāpakau</strong><br>
<strong>lokānāṁ hita-kāriṇau tri-bhuvane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mānyau śaraṇyākarau</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-padāravinda-bhajanā-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nandena mattālikau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs,  namely  Śrī  Rūpa  Goswāmī,  Śrī  Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who are very expert in scrutinizingly studying all the revealed scriptures with the aim of establishing eternal religious principles for the benefit of all human beings. Thus they are honoured all over the three worlds, and they are worth taking shelter of because they are absorbed in the mood of the gopīs and are engaged in the transcendental loving service of Rādhā and Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-guṇānuvarṇana-vidhau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śraddhā-samṛddhy-anvitau</strong><br>
<strong>pāpottāpa-nikṛntanau tanu-bhṛtāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda-gānāmṛtaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaika-nipuṇau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaivalya-nistārakau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī,  Śrī  Raghunāth  Bhaṭṭa  Goswāmī,  Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who are very much enriched in the understanding of Lord Chaitanya and who are thus expert in narrating His transcendental qualities. They can purify all conditioned souls from the reactions of their sinful activities by pouring upon them transcendental songs about Govinda. As such, they are very expert in increasing the limits of the ocean of transcendental bliss, and they are the saviours of the living entities from the devouring mouth of liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tyaktvā tūrṇam aśeṣa-maṇḍala-pati-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śreṇīṁ sadā tucha-vat</strong><br>
<strong>bhūtvā dīna-gaṇeśakau karuṇayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaupīna-kanthāśritau</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-bhāva-rasāmṛtābdhi-laharī-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kallola-magnau muhur</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs,  namely  Śrī  Rūpa  Goswāmī,  Śrī  Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who kicked off all association of aristocracy as insignificant. In order to deliver the poor conditioned souls, they accepted loincloths, treating themselves as mendicants, but they are always merged in the ecstatic ocean of the gopīs’ love for Krishna and bathe always and repeatedly in the waves of that ocean.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kūjat-kokila-haṁsa-sārasa-gaṇā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kīrṇe mayūrākule</strong><br>
<strong>nānā-ratna-nibaddha-mūla-viṭapa-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-yukta-vṛndāvane</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇam ahar-niśaṁ prabhajatau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvārthadau yau mudā</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī,  Śrī  Raghunāth  Bhaṭṭa  Goswāmī,  Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were always engaged in worshiping Rādhā-Krishna in the transcendental land of Vṛndāvan, where there are beautiful trees full of fruits and flowers which have under their roots all valuable jewels. The Goswāmīs are perfectly competent to bestow upon the living entities the greatest boon of the goal of life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṅkhyā-pūrvaka-nāma-gāna-natibhiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kālāvasānī-kṛtau</strong><br>
<strong>nidrāhāra-vihārakādi-vijitau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chātyanta-dīnau cha yau</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa-smṛter madhurimā-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nandena sammohitau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs,  namely  Śrī  Rūpa  Goswāmī,  Śrī  Sanātan Goswāmī,  Śrī  Raghunāth  Bhaṭṭa  Goswāmī,  Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were engaged in chanting the Holy Names of the Lord and bowing down in a scheduled measurement. In this way they utilised their valuable lives, and in executing these devotional activities they conquered over eating and sleeping and were always meek and humble, enchanted by remembering the transcendental qualities of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kuṇḍa-taṭe kalinda-tanayā-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tīre cha vaṁśīvaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>premonmāda-vaśād aśeṣa-daśayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;grastau pramattau sadā</strong><br>
<strong>gāyantau cha kadā harer guṇa-varaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvābhibhūtau mudā</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were sometimes on the bank of the Rādhākuṇḍa lake or the shores of the Yamunā and sometimes at Vaṁśīvaṭa. There they appeared just like madmen in the full ecstasy of love for Krishna, exhibiting different transcendental symptoms in their bodies, and they were merged in the ecstasy of Krishna consciousness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>he rādhe vraja-devīke cha lalite</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he nanda-sūno kutaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-govardhana-kalpa-pādapa-tale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kālindī-vanye kutaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>ghoṣantāv iti sarvato vraja-pure</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khedair mahā-vihvalau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were chanting very loudly everywhere in Vṛndāvan, shouting, “Queen of Vṛndāvan, Rādhārāṇī! O Lalitā! O son of Nanda Mahārāj! Where are you all now? Are you just on the hill of Govardhan, or are you under the trees on the bank of the Yamunā? Where are you?” These were their moods in executing Krishna consciousness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ohe-harinam-tava-mahima-apara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāmāṣṭakam 7 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ohe harinām, tava mahimā apāra</strong><br>
<strong>tava pade nati āmi kori bāro bāro</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Holy Name! Your glories are boundless! Therefore I bow down at Your lotus feet again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gokuler mahotsava ānanda-sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe poḍi hoiyā kātara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O grand festival of Gokula! O ocean of bliss! I fall down at Your lotus feet, for I am feeling very distressed and troubled at heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi kṛṣṇa, pūrṇa-vapu, rasera nidāna</strong><br>
<strong>tava pade poḍi tava guṇa kori gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are Lord Krishna, Your divine form is fully perfect and complete, and You are the origin of all transcendental mellows. Falling down at Your lotus feet, I sing of Your divine qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je kore tomār pade ekānta āśroya</strong><br>
<strong>tā’ra ārti-rāśi nāśa karaho niśchoya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You definitely destroy the multitude of afflictions of that person who takes exclusive shelter at Your holy feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva aparādha tumi nāśa kara tā’ra</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-aparādhāvadhi nāśaho tāhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You destroy all of their offences, even to the extent of the nāma-aparādhas (the ten offenses against the chanting of the Holy Name).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-doṣa dhauta kori’ tāhāra hṛdoya</strong><br>
<strong>siṁhāsane baiso tumi parama āśroya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Cleansing them of all impurities, You are then seated upon the throne of their heart as their supreme shelter.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ati-ramya chid-ghana-ānanda-mūrtimān</strong><br>
<strong>‘raso vai saḥ’ boli’ veda kore tuyā gān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are delightful and beautiful, the personification of complete cognizance and condensed bliss. The Vedas sing of You, saying, “The Supreme Personality of Godhead is verily the personification of all transcendental mellows.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktivinoda rūpa-gosvāmī-charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>māgaye sarvadā nāma-sphūrti sarva-kṣane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the lotus feet of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, Bhakti Vinod constantly begs at every moment for the direct revelation of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ki-rupe-paibo-seva-mui-durachara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ki-rūpe pāibo sevā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki-rūpe pāibo sevā mui durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇave rati na hoilo āmāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have no devotional attachment for either the Vaiṣṇavas or my Spiritual Master. How is it possible for a rascal like me to attain devotional service?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśeṣa māyāte mana magana hoilo</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavete leśa-mātra rati nā janmilo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind is always drowning in illusion. I have not even the smallest fragment of devotion for the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>biṣoye bhuliya andha hoinu divā-niśi</strong><br>
<strong>gole phā̐sa dite phere māyā se piśāchī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have become blind by constantly meditating on the objects of the senses. The witch Māyādevī has placed a hangman’s noose around my neck.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyāre koriyā jaya chāḍāna na jāya</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-kṛpā vinā āro nahiko upāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even if I can cure my blindness and defeat Māyā in the form of this hangman, I cannot become free from material illusion without the mercy of the saintly devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adoṣa daraśi-prabhu patita-uddhāra</strong><br>
<strong>ei-bāro narottame karaho nistāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh master, Oh purifier of the sinful, Oh saviour of the fallen souls, you do not see the faults of others, please rescue this Narottam Dās.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhajahu-re-mana-shri-nanda-nandana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajahu̐ Re Mana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Govinda Dās Kavirāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajahu̐ re mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-nanda-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhaya-charaṇāravinda re</strong><br>
<strong>dūrlabha mānava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama sat-saṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;taroho e bhava-sindhu re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, just worship the lotus feet of the son of Nanda, which make one fearless. Having obtained this rare human birth, cross over this ocean of worldly existence through the association of saintly persons.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śīta ātapa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bāta bariṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dina jāminī jāgi re</strong><br>
<strong>biphale sevinu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpaṇa durajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chapala sukha-laba lāgi’ re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Both in the day and at night I remain sleepless, suffering the pains of the heat and cold, the wind and the rain. For a fraction of flickering happiness I have uselessly served wicked and miserly men.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e dhana, jauvana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;putra, parijana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ithe ki āche paratīti re</strong><br>
<strong>kamala-dala-jala,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana ṭalamala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajahu̐ hari-pada niti re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What assurance of real happiness is there in all of one’s wealth, youthfulness, sons, and family members? This life is tottering like a drop of water on a lotus petal; therefore you should always serve and worship the divine feet of Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śravaṇa, kīrtana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇa, vandana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-sevana, dāsya re</strong><br>
<strong>pūjana, sakhī-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ātma-nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda-dāsa-abhilāṣa re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">It is the desire and great longing of Govinda dās to engage himself in the nine processes of bhakti, namely hearing the glories of Lord Hari and chanting those glories, constantly remembering Him and offering prayers to Him, serving the Lord’s lotus feet, serving the Supreme Lord as a servant, worshipping Him with flowers and incense and so forth, serving Him as a friend, and completely offering the Lord one’s very self.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-manasa-deho-geho-jo-kichu-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mānasa Deho Geho <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mānasa, deho, geho, jo kichū mora</strong><br>
<strong>arpilu̐ tuyā pade, nanda-kiśora!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mind, body, and family, whatever may be mine, I have surrendered at Your lotus feet, O youthful son of Nanda!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sampade vipade, jīvane-maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>dāy mama gelā, tuyā o-pada varaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In good fortune or in bad, in life or at death, all my difficulties have disappeared by choosing those feet of Yours as my only shelter.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mārobi rākhobi—jo ichā tohārā</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-dāsa prati tuyā adhikārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Slay me or protect me as You wish, for You are the master of Your eternal servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janmāobi moye ichā jadi tora</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-gṛhe jani janma hau mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If it is Your will that I be born again, then may it be in the home of Your devotee.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kīṭa-janma hau yathā tuyā dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>bahir-mukha brahma-janme nāhi āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">May I be born again even as a worm, as long as I may remain Your devotee. I have no desire to be born as a Brahmā averse to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhukti-mukti-spṛhā bihīna je bhakta</strong><br>
<strong>labhaite tā̐ka saṅga anurakta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I yearn for the company of the devotee completely devoid of all desire for worldly enjoyment or liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janaka, jananī, doyita, tanoy</strong><br>
<strong>prabhu, guru, pati—tuhu̐ sarva-moy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Father, mother, lover, son, Lord, preceptor, and husband — You are everything to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda kohe, śuno kāna!</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-nātha! tuhu̐ hāmāra parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod says, “O Krishna, please hear me! Oh Lord of Rādhā, You are my life and soul!”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-e-ghora-samsare-podiya-manava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>E ghora saṁsare <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Kālidās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e ghora saṁsāre,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍiyā mānava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na pāya duḥkhera śeṣa</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-saṅga kori,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari bhaja jodi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tobe anta hoy kleśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Fallen into this material world of birth and death, there is no end to a person’s unhappiness. Still, if they associate with devotees and worship Lord Hari, then all their sadness and suffering will come to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>biṣoya anale,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jvaliche hṛdaya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anale boḍe anal</strong><br>
<strong>aparādha chāḍi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;loy kṛṣṇa-nāma,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anale paḍaye jola</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The heart burns in the fire of sense gratification and the fire burns more and more. Avoiding offences, chant the Holy Names of Lord Krishna, for that will pour water on the fire.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi chaitanya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-kamale,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśroya loilo jei</strong><br>
<strong>kālidās bole&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvane maraṇe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra āśroya sei</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kālidās says, “Anyone who has taken shelter of the lotus feet of Lord Chaitanya and Lord Nityānanda is my shelter in life and death.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dushta-mana-tumi-kisera-vaishnava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vaiṣṇava Ke? <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>“Who is a Real Vaiṣṇava?”</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duṣṭa mana!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi kisera vaiṣṇava?</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhāra tore,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjanera ghore,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava ‘harināma’ kevala ‘kaitava’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh wicked mind! What kind of Vaiṣṇava do you think you are? Your pretentious show of chanting Lord Hari’s Holy Name in a solitary place is only for the sake of attaining the false prestige of a worldly reputation — it is nothing but pure hypocrisy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaḍera pratiṣṭhā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūkarer biṣṭhā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāno nā ki tāhā māyār vaibhava</strong><br>
<strong>kanaka kāminī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divasa-jāminī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāviyā ki kāja, anitya se saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Such materialistic prestige is as disgusting as the stool of a hog. Do you not know that it is only a mere illusion cast by the potency of Māyā? What is the value of contemplating day and night your plans for enjoying wealth and women? All these things are only temporary.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra kanaka,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhogera janaka,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kanakera dvāre sevaho mādhava</strong><br>
<strong>kāminīr kāma,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nahe tava dhāma,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhār—mālika kevala jādava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When you claim wealth as your own, it creates in you ever-increasing desires for material enjoyment. Your riches should be used for serving Mādhava, the Lord of all wealth. Neither is it your proper place to indulge in lust for women, whose only true proprietor is Lord Jādava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pratiṣṭhāśā-taru,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaḍa-māyā-maru,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pela rāvaṇa jujhiyā rāghava</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavī pratiṣṭhā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāte kara niṣṭhā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā nā bhajile labhibe raurava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The demon Rāvaṇa (lust-incarnate) fought with Lord Rāmachandra (love-incarnate) in order to gain the tree of worldly reputation — but that oasis turned out to be but a mirage cast in the desert wasteland of the Lord’s illusory material potency. Please cultivate fixed determination to attain only the steady and solid platform whereupon a Vaiṣṇava ever stands. If you neglect worshiping the Lord from this position, then you will ultimately attain a hellish existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>harijana-dveṣa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratisthāśā-kleśa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara keno tobe tāhāra gaurava</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇaver pāche,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratisthāśā āche,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā’te kabhu nahe anitya-vaibhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Why do you needlessly suffer the torment of blaspheming the devotees of Lord Hari, attempting to achieve their eminence, thereby only proving your own fruitless foolishness? The desire for spiritual eminence is easily fulfilled when one becomes a devotee of the Lord, for eternal fame automatically follows the heels of a Vaiṣṇava. And that fame is never to be considered a temporary worldly opulence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se hari-sambandha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūnya-māyā-gandha,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā kobhu noy jaḍer kaitava</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhā-chaṇḍālī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjanatā-jāli,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ubhaye jāniho māyika raurava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The relationship between a devotee and Lord Hari is devoid of even a trace of worldly illusion; it has nothing to do with the materialistic cheating propensity. The prestige of so-called popularity in the material realm is compared to a treacherous dog-eating witch, and the attempt to live in solitude to supposedly engage in unalloyed bhajan is compared to an entangling network of distraction. Please know that anyone striving in either of these ways verily lives in the hell of Māyā’s illusion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kīrtana chāḍibo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā māgibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki kāja ḍhuḍiyā tādṛśa gaurava</strong><br>
<strong>mādhavendra purī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāva-ghore churi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā korilo kobhu sadāi jānabo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I shall give up chanting the Lord’s Name publicly in kīrtan and retire to solitude, thus smearing myself with worldly honour.” Dear mind, what is the good of seeking such so-called glory? I will always remind you that the great soul Mādhavendra Purī never deceived himself in that regard by committing theft in his own storehouse of perception the way you do.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra pratiṣṭhā,—&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūkarera biṣṭhā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tār-saha sama kabhu nā mānava</strong><br>
<strong>matsaratā-vaśe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi jaḍa-rase,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ma’jecho chāḍiyā kīrtana-sauṣṭava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your cheap reputation is equal to the stool of a hog. An ordinary ambitious man like you can never be equated with a devotee of Mādhavendra Purī’s eminence. Under the sway of envy, you have drowned yourself in the filthy waters of material enjoyment after having abandoned the excellent perfection of congregational kīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tāi duṣṭa mana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjana bhajan,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prachāricho chole kuyogī-vaibhava</strong><br>
<strong>prabhu sanātane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama jatane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śikṣā dilo jāhā, chinto sei saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Truly, Oh wicked mind, the glories of so-called solitary worship are propagated only by false yogīs using unscrupulous means to deceive others. To save yourself from these pitfalls, please contemplate the instructions that the Supreme Lord Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu kindly gave us while addressing Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī with the utmost care.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei duṭi kathā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhulo’ nā sarvathā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchaiḥ-svare kara hari-nāma-rava</strong><br>
<strong>phalgu āro jukta,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baddha āro mukta,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nā bhāviho, ‘ekākār’ saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Do not forget for a moment the two most valuable concepts that He taught: 1) the principle of dry, apparent renunciation as opposed to real, appropriate renunciation; and 2) the principle of a soul being trapped in the bondage of matter as opposed to a soul who is liberated. Don’t ever make the mistake of thinking that these conflicting concepts are on the same level. Please remember this while engaging yourself in chanting the Lord ’s Holy Names as loud as you possibly can.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kanaka-kāminī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā-bāghinī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍiyāche jāre, sei to’ vaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>sei anāsakta,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei śuddha-bhakta,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra tathā pāy parābhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One is truly a Vaiṣṇava who has given up the habit of falling victim to the ferocious tigress of wealth, beauty, and fame. Such a soul is factually detached from material life, and is known as a pure devotee. Someone with this consciousness of detachment has thereby become victorious over the mundane world of birth and death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yathā-jogya bhoga,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi tathā roga,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anāsakta sei, ki āro kahabo</strong><br>
<strong>āsakti-rohita,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sambandha-sohito,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣoya-samūha sakali mādhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One is indeed detached who moderately partakes of worldly things that are deemed necessary for living in devotional service; a devotee acting in that manner does not fall prey to the disease of material infatuation. Thus devoid of selfish attachment, and endowed with the ability to see things in relation to the Lord, all sense objects are then directly perceived as being Lord Mādhava Himself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se jukta-vairāgya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā to’ saubhāgya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāi jaḍete harir vaibhava</strong><br>
<strong>kīrtane jāhār,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā-sambhār,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhār sampatti kevala kaitava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is the standard of befitting renunciation, and one who realizes this is most fortunate indeed. Everything involved in such a devotee’s life represents Lord Hari’s personal spiritual opulence as manifest in the world of matter. On the other hand, one who engages in chanting the Lord’s Name with hopes of enhancing his own material reputation finds that all his activities and paraphernalia represent only the riches of hypocrisy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣoya-mumukṣu,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhoger bubhukṣu,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;du’ye tyajo mana, dui avaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇer sambandha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aprākṛta-skandha,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobhu nāhe tāhā jaḍer sambhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh mind, please reject the company of two types of persons — those desiring impersonal liberation from the material world, and those who desire to enjoy the pleasure of material sense objects. Both of these are equally non-devotees. The things that are used in relation to Lord Krishna are objects belonging directly to the transcendental realm, and thus having nothing to do with matter they cannot be either owned or forsaken by persons interested in mundane enjoyment or renunciation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyāvādī jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇetara mana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukta abhimāne se ninde vaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavera dāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava bhakti-āśa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno vā ḍākicho nirjana-āhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">An impersonal philospher is opposed to thinking of Krishna as an object of devotion, and thus being puffed up with the false pride of imaginary liberation he dares to criticize the true devotees of the Lord. Oh mind, you are the servant of the Vaiṣṇavas, and you should always hope for attaining devotion. Why then do you make such a loud commotion by calling to me and trying to prove the supposed supremacy of your practice of solitary worship?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je phalgu-vairāgī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kohe nije tyāgī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se nā pāre kobhu hoite vaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>hari-pada chāḍi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjanatā bāḍi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhiyā ki phol, phalgu se vaibhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who falsely gives up things that could actually be used in the Lord’s service proudly calls himself a ‘renunciate,’ but unfortunately he can never become a Vaiṣṇava by such an attitude. Abandoning his servitorship to the lotus feet of Lord Hari, and resigning himself to his solitary home — whatever is gained by that exercise can only be the worthless treasure of deception.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-dāsye rohi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍi bhoga-ahi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhāśā nahe kīrtana-gaurava</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-nitya-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā chāḍi mana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keno vā nirjana-bhajana-kaitava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ever engage yourself in the service of Śrī Rādhā, and keep aloof from the vicious snake of materialistic sense gratification. The glory of participating in the Lord’s kīrtan is not meant to bolster anyone’s ambitions for personal recognition. Oh mind, why then have you abandoned the identity of being Rādhā’s eternal servant in favour of retiring to a solitary place to practice the cheating process of so-called bhajan?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-vāsī-gaṇa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prachāraka-dhana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā-bhikṣuka tārā nahe śava</strong><br>
<strong>prāṇa āche tāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se-hetu prachāra,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhāśā-hīna-‘kṛṣṇa-gāthā’ saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The most valuable treasures amongst the Lord’s preachers are the eternal personalities residing in Vraja-dhām. They never occupy themselves with begging for worthless material reputation, which is cherished only by the living dead. The Vraja-vāsīs are truly infused with life, and therefore they preach in order to give life to the walking corpses of the mundane world. All the songs that the Vraja-vāsīs sing about the glories of Lord Krishna are devoid of any tinge of desire for fame.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-dayita-dāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kīrtanete āśa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara uchaiḥ-svare harināma rava</strong><br>
<strong>kīrtana-prabhāve,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇa svabhāve,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se kāle bhajana-nirjana sambhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This humble servant of Rādhā and Her beloved Krishna always hopes for kīrtan, and he begs all to loudly sing the Names of Lord Hari. The transcendental power of congregational chanting automatically awakens remembrance of the Lord and His divine Pastimes in relation to one’s own eternal spiritual form. Only at that time does it become possible to go off to a solitary place and engage in the confidential worship of Their Lordships.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-emona-durmati-samsara-bhitore" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainyātmikā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(prabhu he!)</strong><br>
<strong>emona durmati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra bhitore,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍiyā āchinu āmi</strong><br>
<strong>tava nija-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kono mahājane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṭhāiyā dile tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A wicked mind brought me into this world, O Lord, but one of Your pure and elevated devotees has come to bring me out.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kori more,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita dekhiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kohilo āmāre giyā</strong><br>
<strong>ohe dīna-jon,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuno bhālo kathā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ullasita ho’be hiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He saw me so fallen and wretched, took pity, and came to me saying, “O humbled soul, please listen to this good tiding, for it will gladden your heart.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāre tārite,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nabadvīpe avatār</strong><br>
<strong>tomā heno koto,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna hīna jone,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;korilena bhava-pār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Krishna Chaitanya has appeared in the land of Nabadwīp to deliver you. He has safely conducted many miserable souls such as you across the sea of worldly existence.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vedera pratijñā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhibāra tore,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rukma-varṇa vipra-suta</strong><br>
<strong>mahāprabhu nāme,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nadīyā mātāya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;soṅge bhāi avadhūta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“To fulfil the promise of the Vedas, the son of a <i>brāhmaṇ</i>, bearing the Name Mahāprabhu of golden complexion, has descended with His brother, the avadhūt Nityānanda. Together They have overwhelmed all of Nadia with divine ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nanda suta jini,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaitanya gosāi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-nāma kori’ dāna</strong><br>
<strong>tārilo jagat,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi-o jāiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;loho nija-paritrāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Chaitanya, who is Krishna Himself, the son of Nanda, has saved the world by freely distributing His own Holy Name. Go also and receive your deliverance.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se kathā śuniyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchi, nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa-tole</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana-kāhinī bole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, hearing those words, Bhakti Vinod has come weeping to the soles of Your lotus feet and tells the story of his life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ki-jani-ki-bale-tomara-dhamete" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Goptṛtve Varaṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki jāni ki bale,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra dhāmete,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoinu śaraṇāgata</strong><br>
<strong>tumi dayāmoy,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita-pāvana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita-tāraṇe rata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By what personal knowledge or strength has one such as I come to Your shelter? Surely it is by Your mercy alone, for You are ever seeking the purification and deliverance of the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bharasā āmāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei mātra nāth!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi to’ karuṇāmoya</strong><br>
<strong>tava dayā pātra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi mora sama,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśya ghuchāre bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are my only hope, for You are full of compassion and mercy. There is no one who needs Your mercy more than I. You will surely drive away all fear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāre tārite,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāhāro śakati,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avanī-bhitore nāhi</strong><br>
<strong>dayāla ṭhākura!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghoṣaṇā tomāra,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama pāmare trāhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No one else has the power to deliver me. O merciful Lord, by Your declaration, kindly deliver this vile and lowly sinner.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala chāḍiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchi āmi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇe nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>āmi nitya-dāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi pālayitā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi goptā, jagannātha!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have given up everything and come to Your lotus feet. I am Your eternal servant, and You are my protector and maintainer, O Lord of the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra sakala,&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi mātra dāsa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra tāribe tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇa, korinu varaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra nahi to’ āmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everything is Yours. I am merely a servant, certain that You will deliver me. I have chosen Your lotus feet as my only shelter. I no longer belong to myself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diya śaraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lo’yeche tomāra pāya</strong><br>
<strong>khomi aparādha,&nbsp;&nbsp;nāme ruchi diyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pālana karahe tāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping, Bhakti Vinod takes shelter at Your feet. Forgive his offences, afford him a taste for the Holy Name, and kindly maintain him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhuliya-tomare-samsare-asiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhuliyā tomāre,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāre āsiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;peye nānābidha byathā</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchi āmi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bolibo duḥkhera kathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, forgetting you and coming to this material world, I have experienced various pains and sorrows. Now I am approaching your lotus feet to submit my tale of woe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jananī-joṭhore,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chilāma jokhon,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣama vandhana-pāśe</strong><br>
<strong>ekabāra prabhu!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhā diyā more,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vañchile e dīna dāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While I was bound up tightly in the unbearable confines of my mother’s womb, O Lord, You once revealed Yourself before me, but briefly, and then abandoned this poor servant of Yours.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tokhona bhāvinu,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama pāiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koribo bhajana tava</strong><br>
<strong>janama hoilo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍi māyājāle</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā hoilo jñāna-laba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At that moment, I promised that having taken birth I would worship you. But after taking birth, I fell into the entangling network of worldly illusion, devoid of even a drop of true knowledge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ādarera chele.&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svajanera kole,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hāsiyā kāṭānu kāla</strong><br>
<strong>janaka-jananī-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;snehete bhuliyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsara lāgilo bhālo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As a boy fondled in the lap of relatives, I passed my time smiling and laughing. My parents’ affection helped me to forget You still more, my Lord, and I began to think the material world to be a very nice place.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>krame dina dina,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bālaka hoiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khelinu bālaka saha</strong><br>
<strong>āro kichu dine,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna upajilo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṭha poḍi aharahaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Day by day I grew and soon began playing with other boys. My powers of understanding emerged and I read and studied my school lessons incessantly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>idyāra gaurave,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhrami deśe deśe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhana upārjana kori’</strong><br>
<strong>svajana-pālana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kori eka mane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhulinu tomāre, hari!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Travelling from place to place, proud of my education, I grew wealthy and maintained my family with undivided attention. O Lord Hari, I forgot You!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vārdhakye ekhona,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakativinoda,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kātara ati</strong><br>
<strong>nā bhajiyā tore,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dina vṛthā gelo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekhon ki ho’be gati!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now in old age, this Bhakti Vinod very sadly weeps. I failed to worship You, O Lord, and instead passed my life in vain. What will be my fate now?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-he-prapanche-podiya-agati-hoiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajan-Lālasā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari he!</strong><br>
<strong>prapañche poḍiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;agati hoiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā dekhi upāya āro</strong><br>
<strong>agatira gati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe śaraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāya korinu sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord Hari! Having fallen into the illusion of this world, thus rendered helpless, I see no other means of deliverance but You. Since You are the only recourse for the helpless, I accept the shelter of Your lotus feet as most essential.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karama geyāna,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichu nāhi mora,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sādhana bhajana nāi</strong><br>
<strong>tumi kṛpā-moya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi to’ kāṅgāla,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ahaitukī kṛpā chāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have no background of pious activities, nor any knowledge, nor any regulated devotional practice. But You are full of compassion and kindness; therefore I, being indeed destitute, solicit Your causeless mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vākya-mano-vega,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodha-jihvā-vega,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udara-upastha-vega</strong><br>
<strong>miliyā e saba,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāre bhāsāye,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diteche paramodvega</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The powerful urges of speech, mind, anger, tongue, belly, and genitals have banded together to cast me adrift on the sea of this material world, thus causing me grievous trouble.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>oneka jatane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se saba damane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍiyāchi āśā āmi</strong><br>
<strong>anāthera nātha!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ḍāki tava nāma,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekhona bharasā tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After numerous endeavours to subdue these material demands, I have completely given up all hope. Oh Lord of the destitute! I call upon Your Holy Name, for now You are my only hope.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-hari-biphole-janama-goyainu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Iṣṭa-deve Vijñapti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari hari!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;biphole janama goyāinu</strong><br>
<strong>manuṣya-janama pāiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa nā bhajiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāniyā śuniyā biṣa khāinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord Hari, I have spent my life uselessly. Having obtained a human birth and having not worshipped Rādhā and Krishna, I have knowingly drunk poison.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>golokera prema-dhana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;harināma-saṅkīrtana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rati nā janmilo keno tāya</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-biṣānale,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divā-niśi hiyā jvale,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;juḍāite nā koinu upāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The treasure of divine love in Goloka Vṛndāvan has descended as the congregational chanting of Lord Hari’s Holy Names. Why did my attraction for that chanting never come about? Day and night my heart burns from the fire of the poison of worldliness, and I have not taken the means to relieve it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendra-nandana jei,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śachī-suta hoilo sei,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;balarāma hoilo nitāi</strong><br>
<strong>dīna-hīna jata chilo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;harināme udhārilo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra śākṣī jagāi mādhāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Krishna, who is the son of the King of Vraja, became the son of Śachī (Lord Chaitanya), and Balarām became Nitāi. The Holy Name delivered all those souls who were lowly and wretched. The two sinners Jagāi and Mādhāi are evidence of this.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā prabhu nanda-suta,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛṣabhānu-sutā-juta,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karuṇā karaho ei-bāro</strong><br>
<strong>narottama-dāsa koy,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā ṭheliho rāṅgā pāy,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomā bine ke āche āmāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord Krishna, son of Nanda, accompanied by the daughter of Vṛṣabhānu, please be merciful to me now. Narottam Dās says, “O Lord, please do not push me away from Your reddish lotus feet, for who is my beloved except for You?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-atma-nivedana-tuya-pade-kori" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ātma-nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-nivedana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā pade kori,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoinu parama sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>duḥkha dūre gelo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chintā nā rohilo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudike ānanda dekhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have become supremely joyful by surrendering myself at Your holy feet. Unhappiness has gone away, and there are no more anxieties. I see joy in all directions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśoka-abhaya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amṛta-ādhāra,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa-dvaya</strong><br>
<strong>tāhāte ekhona,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśrāma lobhiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍinu bhavera bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your two lotus feet are reservoirs of immortal nectar where one may live free from sorrow and fear. I have found peace there now and have given up the fear of worldly existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra saṁsāre,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koribo sevana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nahibo phalera bhāgī</strong><br>
<strong>tava sukha jāhe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koribo jatana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ho’ye pade anurāgī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I shall render service in Your household and not endeavour to enjoy the fruits of that service, but rather I shall strive for whatever pleases You, fully devoted to Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra sevāya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duḥkha hoya jato,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;seo to’ parama sukha</strong><br>
<strong>sevā-sukha-duḥkha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama sampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāśoye avidyā-duḥkha</strong></p>
