<p class="translation">Troubles encountered in Your service shall be the cause of great happiness, for in Your devotional service joy and sorrow are equally great riches. Both destroy the misery of ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūrva itihāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhulinu sakala,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevā-sukha pe’ye mane</strong><br>
<strong>āmi to’ tomāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi to’ āmāra,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki kāja apara dhane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have completely forgotten all past history by feeling great joy in my mind. I am most certainly Yours, and You are indeed mine. What need is there of any other treasure?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande ḍubiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra sevāra tore</strong><br>
<strong>saba cheṣṭā kore,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava ichā-mata,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā tomāra ghore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, diving into the ocean of bliss, devotes all his efforts for Your service and dwells in Your house according to Your wishes.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-amara-jivana-sada-pape-rata" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āmāra jīvana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra jīvana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā pāpe rata,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhiko puṇyera leśa</strong><br>
<strong>porere udvega,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyāchi je koto,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyāchi jīvere kleśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My life is always attached to sinful activity and there is not even a small part of goodness in it. I have caused others anxiety and given trouble to other souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija sukha lāgi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāpe nāhi ḍori,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayā-hīna svārtha-paro</strong><br>
<strong>para-sukhe duḥkhī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā mithya-bhāṣī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;para-duḥkha sukha-kara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">For the sake of my own happiness I have openly performed sinful activity. Devoid of compassion, I am concerned only with my own selfish interests. Always telling lies, I become miserable to see the happiness of others, while the misery of others brings great happiness to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśeṣa kāmanā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdi mājhe mora,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodhī, dambha-parāyana</strong><br>
<strong>mada-matta sadā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣoye mohita,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hiṁsā-garva vibhūṣana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are limitless material desires within the core of my heart. I am full of anger, attached to arrogance, intoxicated by vanity, and always bewildered by enjoying sense objects. Violence and pride are my ornaments.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nidrālasya hata,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukārje virata,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;akārje udyogī āmi</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭha lāgiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāṭhya-ācharaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobha-hata sadā kāmī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ruined by laziness and sleep, I resist all pious deeds, yet am very enthusiastic to perform wicked acts. For the sake of worldly fame and reputation I engage in the practice of deceitfulness. I am always lustful and a victim of my own greed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e heno durjana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sajjana-varjita,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aparādhi nirantara</strong><br>
<strong>śubha-kārja-śūnya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadānartha-manāḥ,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā duḥkhe jara jara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A vile, wicked man such as this, rejected by saintly persons, is a constant offender, devoid of auspicious works, always in the mentality of separate interest, and wasted away by various kinds of miseries.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bārdhakye ekhona,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upāya-vihīna,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā’te dīna akiñchana</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhura charaṇe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kore duḥkha nivedana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now in old age, deprived of all means of relief, thus humbled and destitute, this Bhakti Vinod submits his tale of grief at the feet of the Supreme Lord.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tumi-sarveshvareshvara-vrajendra-kumara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Tumi sarveśvareśvara <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi sarveśvareśvara, vrajendra-kumāra!</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra ichāya viśve sṛjana saṁhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O youthful son of the King of Vraja, You are the Lord of all lords. According to Your will, creation and destruction take place in the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava ichā-mato brahmā korena sṛjana</strong><br>
<strong>tava ichā-mato viṣṇu korena pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to Your desire Lord Brahmā creates and according to Your desire Lord Viṣṇu maintains.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava ichā-mate śiva korena saṁhāra</strong><br>
<strong>tava ichā-mate māyā sṛje kārāgāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to Your will Lord Śiva destroys, according to Your will Māyā constructs the prison house of this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava ichā-mate jīver janama-maraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>samṛddhi-nipāta duḥkha sukha-saṅghaṭana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to Your will the living beings take birth and die, and according to Your will they meet with prosperity and ruin, happiness and sorrow.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>miche māyā-baddha jīva āśā-pāśe phire</strong><br>
<strong>tava ichā vinā kichu korite nā pāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The tiny soul bound up by Māyā vainly struggles in the fetters of worldly desire. Without Your sanction the soul is unable to do anything.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi to’ rakṣaka āro pālaka āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇa vinā āśā nāhi āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are my only protector and maintainer. Except for Your lotus feet there is no other hope for me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-bala-cheṣṭa-prati bharasā chāḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra ichāya āchi nirbhara koriyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No longer confident of my own strength and endeavour, I have become solely dependent on Your will.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda ati dīna akiñchana</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra ichāya tā’ra jīvana maraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod is most poor, and his pride has been leveled. Now in accordance with Your will he lives and dies.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sarvasva-tomara-charane-sanpiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sarvasva tomāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarvasva tomāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe sa̐piyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍechi tomāra ghore</strong><br>
<strong>tumi to’ ṭhākur,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra kukkur,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;boliyā jānaho more</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now taking all that I possess and surrendering it unto Your lotus feet, O Lord, I throw myself down before Your house. You are the master of the house; kindly consider me Your own dog.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bā̐dhiyā nikaṭe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāre pālibe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rohibo tomāra dvāre</strong><br>
<strong>pratīpa-jonere,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsite nā dibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhibo goḍera pāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Chaining me nearby, You will maintain me, and I shall lie at Your doorstep. I will not allow Your enemies to enter, but will keep them outside the bounds of the surrounding moat.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava nija-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prasād seviyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchiṣṭa rākhibe jāhā</strong><br>
<strong>āmāra bhojana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-ānande,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prati-dina hobe tāhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever food remnants Your devotees leave behind after honouring Your prasād will be my daily sustenance. I will feast on those remnants in great bliss.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bosiyā śuiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chintibo satata āmi</strong><br>
<strong>nāchite nāchite,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikaṭe jāibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jokhona ḍākibe tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While sitting up or lying down, I will constantly meditate on Your lotus feet. Whenever You call me, I will immediately run to You and dance in rapture.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nijera poṣaṇa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobhu nā bhāvibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rohibo bhāvera bhore</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra pālaka,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;boliyā varaṇa kore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will never even think about arranging for my own nourishment, and will remain absorbed in ever cherishing love for my master. Bhakti Vinod now accepts You as his only maintainer.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tatala-saikate-vari-bindu-sama" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Tātala Saikate <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vidyāpati</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tātala saikate&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāri bindu-sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suta-mita-ramaṇī-samāje</strong><br>
<strong>tohe visari mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe samarpala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava majhu hobo kon kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, completely forgetting You, I have offered my mind unto the society of women, children, and friends — but this experience has been just like offering a drop of water unto the burning hot sands of the beach. How can I possibly be relieved of this great misery?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhava! hāma pariṇām-nirāśā</strong><br>
<strong>tuhu̐ jaga-tāraṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna dayāmoy</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ataye tohāri viśoyāsā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Madhava! As a consequence, I am rendered totally despondent. You are the savior of the universe, and are merciful to the helpless souls. Therefore, I place my hope only in You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ādha janama hāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ni̐de go̐yāola</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jarā śiśu koto-dina gela</strong><br>
<strong>nidhuvane ramaṇī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasa-raṅge mātala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tohe bhajabo kon bela</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wandering about in a half-alive condition, I spent my life in utter disgrace. Uncountable days passed as a frivolous child and a useless old man. I have been intoxicated by the pleasure of sharing romantic adventures with beautiful young women. When will I ever get a chance to worship You?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koto chaturānana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mari mari jāota</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na tuyā ādi avasānā</strong><br>
<strong>tohe janami puna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tohe samāota</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sāgara laharī samānā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Innumerable Brahmās have died one after another, whereas You are without beginning or end. All of them take birth from You and are again absorbed in You, just like the waves in the ocean.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaṇaye vidyāpati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śeṣa śamana bhaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā viṇu gati nāhi ārā</strong><br>
<strong>ādi anādika&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nātha kahāosi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava tāraṇa-bhāra tohārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Vidyāpati confesses that now, at the end of his life, he is fearful of death. O Lord! There is no shelter other than You. You will always remain celebrated as being the Lord of both that which has beginning and the beginningless. Now the responsibility for my deliverance from the material world is entirely Yours.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-he-dayala-mora-jaya-radha-natha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya O Prapatti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari he dayāla mora jaya rādhā-nātha</strong><br>
<strong>bāro bāro ei-bāro loho nija sātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Hari! O my merciful Lord! All glories to You, the Lord of Rādhā! Many times I have avoided You, but this time please take me as Your own.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bahu joni bhrami nātha! loinu śaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>nija-guṇe kṛpā kara adhama tāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, after wandering through many wombs, I have taken refuge in You. Be merciful and deliver this wretched soul by Your divine power.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagata-kāraṇa tumi jagata-jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>tomā chāḍā kāro na’hi he rādhā-ramaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O lover of Rādhā, You are the cause of the universe and the life of the universe. Without You no-one has anything.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhuvana-maṅgala tumi bhuvanera pati</strong><br>
<strong>tumi upekṣile nātha, ki hoibe gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You bring about auspiciousness for the world, and You are the master of all the worlds as well. O Lord, what will be my refuge if You neglect me?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāviyā dekhinu ei jagata-mājhāre</strong><br>
<strong>tomā vinā keho nāhi e dāse uddhāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have considered that in this world there is no one except You who can deliver this fallen servant.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-atma-samarpane-gela-abhimana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ātma-samarpaṇe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-samarpaṇe gelā abhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi karabu̐ nija rakṣā-vidhān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Surrendering my soul unto You has lifted from me the burden of false pride. No longer will I try to provide for my own safety.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuyā dhana jāni’ tuhu̐ rākhobi, nāth!</strong><br>
<strong>pālya godhana jāni kori’ tuyā sāth</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I know that You will give protection to Your treasured possessions, Oh Lord. I now understand the mentality of Your treasured cows safely maintained by Your side.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>charāobi mādhava! yamunā-tīre</strong><br>
<strong>baṁśī bājāoto ḍākobi dhīre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When You lead Your herds to pasture, Oh Mādhava, on the banks of the Yamunā river, You will call to them by softly playing on Your flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>agha-baka mārato rakṣā-vidhāna</strong><br>
<strong>karabi sadā tuhu̐ gokula-kāna!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By slaying great demons such as Aghāsura and Bakāsura You will always provide full protection, Oh Kān of Gokul!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rakṣā karabi tuhu̐ niśchoy jāni</strong><br>
<strong>pāna karabu̐ hām jāmuna-pāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Fearless and confident of Your protection, I will drink the waters of the Yamunā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kālīya-dokha karabi vināśā</strong><br>
<strong>śodhobi nadī-jala, bāḍāobi āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Kālīya serpent’s venom poisoned the Yamunā’s waters, yet that poison will be vanquished. You will purify the Yamunā, and by such heroic deeds enhance our faith.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>piyato dāvānala rākhobi moy</strong><br>
<strong>‘gopāla’, ‘govinda’ nāma tava hoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You will surely protect me by swallowing the forest fire. Thus You are called Gopāl (protector of the cows) and Govinda (pleaser of the cows).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sura-pati-durmati-nāśa vichāri’</strong><br>
<strong>rākhobe varṣaṇe, giri-vara-dhāri!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In order to curb the malice of Indra, king of the demigods, You will protect me from his torrents of rain, Oh lifter of the mighty Govardhan Hill!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chatur-ānana korabo jabo chori</strong><br>
<strong>rakṣā karabi meye, gokula-hari!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the four-headed Brahmā abducts me along with Your cowherd boyfriends and calves, then also You will surely protect me, Oh Gokul Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda—tuyā gokula-dhan</strong><br>
<strong>rākhobi keśava! korato jatan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod is now the property of Gokul, Your holy abode. Oh Keśava! Kindly protect him with gentle loving care.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-hobe-bolo-se-dina-amar" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Kobe ho’be bolo <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobe ho’be bolo se-dina āmār</strong><br>
<strong>(āmār) aparādha ghuchi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuddha nāme ruchi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā-bole ho’be hṛdoye sañchār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please tell me, when will that day be mine—when my offences will end and a taste for the pure Holy Name will be infused within my heart by the power of divine grace?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇādhika hīna,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobe nije māni,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sahiṣṇutā-guṇa hṛdoyete āni’</strong><br>
<strong>sakale mānada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpani amānī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoye āsvādibo nāma-rasa-sār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Considering myself lower than a blade of grass, bringing the quality of forbearance into my heart, showing respect to all, myself being freed from all false pride — when will I taste the essence of the liquid nectar of the Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhona jon āra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobitā-sundarī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bolibo nā chāhi deho-sukha-karī</strong><br>
<strong>janme-janme dāo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ohe gaurahari!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ahaitukī bhakti charaṇe tomār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wealth, following, beautiful women, as described in worldly poetry—I do not want any such bodily pleasures. O Lord Gaurahari, please give me unmotivated devotion to Your lotus feet, birth after birth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(kobe) korite śrī-kṛṣṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma uchāraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulakita deho gadgada vachana</strong><br>
<strong>baibarṇya-bepathu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hobe saṅghaṭana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirantara netre bo’be aśru-dhār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, while articulating the divine Name of Śrī Krishna, will my body be thrilled in ecstatic rapture and my words choked with emotion? When will pallor and ecstatic trembling occur, and when will streams of tears flow constantly from my eyes?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe navadvīpe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suradhunī-taṭe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘gaura-nityānanda’ boli’ niṣkapaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>nāchiyā gāiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;beḍāibo chuṭe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bātulera prāya chāḍiyā vichār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, in the land of Nabadwīp, on the banks of the Ganges, will I run about, guilelessly calling, “O Gaura! O Nityānanda!” dancing and singing like a madman, giving up all considerations?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe nityānanda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;more kori’ dayā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍāibe mora viṣoyera māyā</strong><br>
<strong>diyā more nija-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇera chāyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāmera hāṭete dibe adhikār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will Lord Nityānanda be merciful to me and release me from the illusion of worldliness? Giving me the shade of His lotus feet, when will He allow me to enter the marketplace of the Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kinibo, luṭibo,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-rasa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-rase māti’ hoibo vivaśa</strong><br>
<strong>rasera rasika-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa paraśa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koriyā mojibo rase anibār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I shall buy and plunder the mellow of the Name Hari, and becoming thoroughly intoxicated by those liquid mellows of the Holy Name, I shall become stunned. By touching the feet of those great souls who are able to relish those mellows, I will be constantly immersed in the sweet nectar of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe jīve dayā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoibe udoya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-sukha bhuli’ sudīna-hṛdoya</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koriyā binoya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-ājñā-ṭoholo koribe prachār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will there be an awakening of compassion for all fallen souls, and when will this Bhakti Vinod, forgetting his own happines, with a meek heart set out to propagate by humble entreaty the sacred order of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gopinath-mama-nivedana-shiuno" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 1</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mama nivedana śuno</strong><br>
<strong>viṣoyī durjana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā kāma-rata,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichu nāhi mora guṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, Lord of the gopīs, please hear my submission. I am a wicked materialist, always addicted to worldly desires, and no good qualities do I possess.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra bharasā tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;loinu śaraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra kiṅkara āmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, You are my only hope, and therefore I have taken shelter at Your lotus feet. I am now Your eternal servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone śodhive more</strong><br>
<strong>nā jāni bhakati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karme jaḍamati,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍechi saṁsāra ghore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, how will You purify me? I do not know what devotion is, and my materialistic mind is absorbed in fruitive work. I have fallen into this dark and perilous worldly existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakali tomāra māyā</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi mama bala,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna sunirmala,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svādhīna nahe e kāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, everything here is Your illusory energy. I have no strength or transcendental knowledge, and this body of mine is not independent and free from the control of material nature.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;niyata charaṇe sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>māge e pāmara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karahe karuṇā dāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, this sinner, who is weeping and weeping, begs for an eternal place at Your divine feet. Please give him Your mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi to sakali pāro</strong><br>
<strong>durjane tārite,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra śakati,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ke āche pāpīra āro</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, You are able to do anything, and therefore You have the power to deliver all sinners. Who is there that is more of a sinner than myself?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi kṛpā-pārābāra</strong><br>
<strong>jīvera kāraṇe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyā prapañche,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;līlā koile subistāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, You are the ocean of mercy. Having come into this phenomenal world, You expand Your divine Pastimes for the sake of the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi ki doṣera doṣī</strong><br>
<strong>asura sakala,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāilo charaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vinoda thākilo bosi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, I am so sinful that although all the demons attained Your lotus feet, Bhakti Vinod has remained in worldly existence.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gopinath-ghuchao-samsara-jvala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 2</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghuchāo saṁsāra-jvālā</strong><br>
<strong>avidyā-jātanā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro nāhi sahe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama-maraṇa-mālā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, please remove the torment of worldly existence. I can no longer tolerate the pain of ignorance and the repeated succession of rebirth and death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi to kāmera dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>viṣoya-vāsanā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāgiche hṛdoye,</strong><br>
<strong>phāṅdiche karama phāṅsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, indeed I am a servant of lust. Worldly desires are awakening in my heart, and thus the noose of fruitive work is beginning to tighten.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kobe vā jāgibo āmi</strong><br>
<strong>kāma-rūpa ari,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dūre teyāgibo,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdoye sphuribe tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, when will I wake up and abandon afar this enemy of lust, and when will You manifest Yourself in my heart?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi to’ tomāra jon</strong><br>
<strong>tomāre chāḍiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra bhajinu,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuliyā āpana-dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, I am Your devotee, but having abandoned You and thus having forgotten my real treasure, I have worshiped this mundane world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi to sakali jāno</strong><br>
<strong>āpanāra jane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;daṇḍiyā ekhona,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-charaṇe deho sthāno</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, You know everything. Now, having punished Your servant, please give him a place at Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei ki vichāra tava</strong><br>
<strong>vimukha dekhiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍa nija-jane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na kara karuṇā-laba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, is this Your judgement, that seeing me averse to You, You abandon Your servant and don’t bestow even a particle of mercy upon him?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi to mūrakha ati</strong><br>
<strong>kise bhālo hoya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nā bujhinu,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāi heno mama gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, I am certainly very foolish, and I have never known what is good for me. Therefore such is my condition.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi to paṇḍita-vara</strong><br>
<strong>mūḍhera maṅgala,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā anveṣibe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dāse nā bhāva para</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, You are indeed the wisest person. Please look for a way to bring about auspiciousness for this fool, and please do not consider this servant as an outsider.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gopinath-amara-upaya-nai" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part 3</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra upāya nāi</strong><br>
<strong>tumi kṛpā kori,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāre loile,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāre udhāra pāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, I have no means of success, but if You take me, having bestowed Your mercy upon me, then I will obtain deliverance from this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍechi māyāra phere</strong><br>
<strong>dhon, dārā, suta,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghireche āmāre,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāmete rekheche jere</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, I have fallen into the perils of material illusion. Wealth, wife, and sons have surrounded me, and lust has wasted me away.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mana je pāgala mora</strong><br>
<strong>nā māne śāsana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā achetana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣoye royeche bhora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, my mind is crazy and does not care for any authority. It is always senseless and has remained in the dark pit of worldly affairs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hāra je menechi āmi</strong><br>
<strong>oneka jatana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoilo biphala,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekhona bharasā tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, I have accepted my defeat. All of my various endeavours were useless. Now You are the only hope.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone hoibe gati</strong><br>
<strong>prabala indriya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bośī-bhūta mana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā chāḍe viṣoya-rati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, how shall I make any advancement when my mind has come under the control of the powerful senses and does not abandon its attachment to materialism?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdoye bosiyā mora</strong><br>
<strong>manake śamiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;loho nija pāne,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghuchibe vipada ghora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, after sitting down in the core of my heart and subduing my mind, please take me to You. In this way the horrible dangers of this world will disappear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anātha dekhiyā more</strong><br>
<strong>tumi hṛṣīkeśa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛṣīka damiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāro’ he saṁsṛti-ghore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, You are Hṛṣīkeśa, the Lord of the senses. Seeing me so helpless, please control these senses of mine and deliver me from this dark and perilous worldly existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopīnāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;golāya legeche phā̐sa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-asi dhori,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bandhana chediyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vinode karaho dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Gopīnāth, the noose of materialism has become fixed around my neck. Taking up the sword of Your mercy and cutting this bondage, make this Bhakti Vinod Your humble servant.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-rupa-manjari-pada-sei-mora-sampada" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī-Rūpa-Mañjarī-pada <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rūpa-mañjarī-pada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora sampada,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora bhajana-pūjana</strong><br>
<strong>sei mora prāṇa-dhana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora ābharaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora jīvanera jīvana</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei mora rasa-nidhi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora vāñchā-siddhi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora vedera dharama</strong><br>
<strong>sei brata, sei tapa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora mantra-japa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora dharama-karama</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anukūla hobe viddhi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se-pade hoibe siddhi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirakhibo e dui nayane</strong><br>
<strong>se rūpa-mādhurī-rāśi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa-kuvalaya-śaśī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praphullita hobe niśi-dine</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuyā adarśana-ahi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorole jāralo dehi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chiro-dina tāpita jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>hā hā rūpa kara dayā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deho more pada-chāyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama loilo śaraṇa</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse-subtitle">Commentary by Śrīla B.R. Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Śrī-rūpa-mañjarī-pada, sei mora sampada, sei mora bhajana-pūjana</i> — my everything is Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī’s holy feet. We are to discuss so many classifications and positions of rasa: <i>śānta</i>, <i>dāsya</i>, <i>sakhya</i>, <i>vātsalya</i>, <i>madhura</i>. And in <i>madhura-rasa</i> Rādhārāṇī’s camp is special. Then again there are so many gradations of <i>sakhīs</i>. Then there is the class of the <i>mañjarīs</i>, the younger girls, and they have more freedom to approach: when Rādhā and Govinda are in union in a private environment, the <i>mañjarīs</i> can still approach. They have such freedom to visit Them. If any materials of service are necessary, the <i>sakhīs</i> send the <i>mañjarīs</i> to that place. The <i>sakhīs</i> do not approach there. In that way the <i>mañjarīs</i> enjoy the best confidence.<br>
The most secret service of both can be supplied through the <i>mañjarīs</i>. In the highest position they have free entrance, and their leader is Rūpa Mañjarī. She is understood to be the leader of the whole group of younger girls, the <i>mañjarīs</i>, therefore in <i>madhura-bhajan</i> she is all-in-all. This has been taught to us by Narottama Ṭhākur. For us — and the younger batch — she is our highest resort.<br>
<i>Sei mora sampada</i> — my wealth is there in her feet. <i>Sei mora bhajana-pūjana</i> — my worship and service is also in her. <i>Sei mora prāṇa-dhana, sei mora ābharaṇa</i> — the very wealth and gist of my life is there; and the ornaments of my life, if any there may be, that is within her grace. <i>Sei mora jīvanera jīvana</i> — indeed, the very life of my life, if there is anything, that is also her. I am for her pleasure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Sei mora rasa-nidhi</i> — if there is anything, the source of any and all desirable ecstasy is only to be found there. The mine, source, and fountainhead of all rasa is there in her feet. <i>Sei mora vāñchā-siddhi</i> — and if I am to expect any other fulfilment in life, that is also there in her feet. <i>Sei mora vedera dharama</i> — if any duty is recommended by the Vedas for me, I would like that such duty be at her feet.<br>
<i>Sei brata, sei tapa</i> — if at all there is any desired accumulation from any penance or observance of vows, that is also to be found there — <i>sei mora mantra-japa</i> — and the continuous repetition of a mantra in japa all ends there: all things have only one end in my case, and that is that they all meet in different phases in the feet of Rūpa Mañjarī. If she is satisfied then the variegated nature of all sorts of my devotional practices are satisfied. <i>Sei mor dharama-karama</i> — and any of my conceptions of duty or activity of any phases or form all have one end and meet in one point: the holy feet of Rūpa Mañjarī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Anukūla habe viddhi, se-pade hoibe siddhi</i> — I only pray to the controller who is at the root of all these arrangements of affairs in this world, “Please connect me there. Oh Absolute Manager, may you grant my prayer that all my tendencies may go to attain her favour. <i>Nirakhibo e dui nayane</i> — and it will be so very intense and concrete as if I can see it with these eyes. It will not be vague, abstract, or imagination, but I want the most concrete realisation by the grace of the Absolute Manager.” <i>Se rūpa-mādhurī-rāśi, prāṇa-kuvalaya-śaśī</i> — what sort of high class beauty is there in her holy feet! I want to jump. Let my heart be a lotus.<br>
There are two classes of lotus: the white lotus came from the sun and the red from the moon. The <i>kuvalaya</i> is a mixture of the two. The lustre of her body will feed me day and night as the life of my life which is compared to a <i>kuvalaya</i>. The <i>kuvalaya</i> is fed and nourished by the lustre of the moon, and her holy feet are the moon, and my very vitality is likened to the lotus. May the lustre of her beauty maintain and nourish my heart of hearts. <i>Praphullita hobe niśi-dine</i> — and the lotus of my heart will grow by that ray, and dance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Tuyā adarśana-ahi</i> — this is my prayer, but what is my present condition? My present condition is in the negative side, my Mistress. My present position is such that without having a sight of you, Your Grace, my heart will burn into ashes. That is my present position. <i>Gorole jāralo dehī</i> — this is just like the poison of a cobra which produces pain within my heart. I have been bitten by a snake, and what is that snake? It is that I am not getting any direct contact with you, Your Grace. The serpent’s poison has captured my heart and I am going to die. <i>Chiro-dina tāpita jīvana</i> — and this is not a sudden thing, but from the beginning I have been suffering this sort of pain of separation from such a beautiful and hopeful life of fulfilment.<br>
<i>Hā hā rūpa kara dayā, deho more pada-chāyā, narottama loilo śaraṇa</i> — but I have now reached the extreme position and for the last time I am begging for your favour; otherwise I am going to be finished.<br>
This was composed by Narottam Ṭhākur in praise of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī. In this way he has given us light of the higher quarter of bhajan life. This sampradāya is named as the Rūpānuga-sampradāya, and our Guru-paramparā and all these things are adjusted according to that. He wrote many songs and they are of very substantive firmament, very ecstatic, and of deep faith.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-durlabha-manava-janma-labhiya-samsare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Durlabha mānava-janma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>durlabha mānava-janma labhiyā saṁsāre</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa nā bhajinu duḥka kohibo kāhāre?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The human form of life is the rarest opportunity for attaining spiritual perfection. But now I am lamenting, because I’ve somehow or other been born with such an opportunity, and I wasted it by never worshiping Lord Krishna. Oh, to whom shall I tell the tale of this misery?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘saṁsār’ ‘saṁsār’, kori miche gelo kāl</strong><br>
<strong>lābha nā koilo kichu, ghaṭilo jañjāl</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Having married and entered into the entanglements of materialistic family life, I passed my time in vain. I never got any tangible gain or permanent benefit, only trouble and botheration.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kisera saṁsār ei chāyābāji prāy</strong><br>
<strong>ihāte mamatā kori vṛthā dina jāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What kind of world is this anyway? It seems to be just like a magic lantern show that I saw at a carnival, wherein so many shadows and optical illusions dance magically before my eyes. I feel great attachment and identification with such a world, and thus day after day passes by fruitlessly, without any purpose whatsoever.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e deho patana ho’le ki robe āmār?</strong><br>
<strong>keho sukha nāhi dibe putra paribār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When this body drops dead on the ground then what will remain mine? At that moment, all of my sons and dearest loved ones will not be able to give me any happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gardabher mata āmi kori pariśram</strong><br>
<strong>kāra lāgi’ eto kori, nā ghuchilo bhram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I work hard just like an ass every day, and now I am wondering for whom am I working so hard? I am still surrounded by so many illusions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dina jāy michā kāje, niśā nidrā-vaśe</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi bhāvi maraṇa nikaṭe āche bose</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I waste every day in useless, insignificant work, and I waste every night controlled by sleep. And in every twenty-four hours I never for one second consider that death is sitting very close by my side.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhālo manda khāi, heri, pari, chintā-hīna</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi bhāvi, e deho chāḍibo kon dina</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I live a very carefree life-style, eating, going out, wearing nice clothes as I please, but I never consider that one day I will have to give up this body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>deho-geho-kalatrādi-chintā abirata</strong><br>
<strong>jāgiche hṛdoye mor buddhi kori hoto</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My poor heart is plagued by constant anxieties about the maintenance and daily turmoils created by my body, my house, my wife, my family members and my social obligations. All these anxieties are pinching me and destroying all my intelligence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāy, hāy! nāhi bhāvi—anitya e saba</strong><br>
<strong>jīvana bigote kothā rohibe vaibhava?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alas, alas! What a remorseful situation has arisen! I am absorbed in all this trouble, but I never consider that all these things are temporary and subject to perish very soon. After I’m dead and gone, where will all of my material opulences remain?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śmaśāne śorīra mama poḍiyā rohibe</strong><br>
<strong>bihaṅga-pataṅga tāy bihāra koribe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When my body will be thrown in the pit at the cremation grounds, it will simply lie there motionlessly. Then many crows, vultures, ants, and worms will come and playfully sport there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kukkur sṛgāl sob ānandita ho’ye</strong><br>
<strong>mahotsava koribe āmār deho lo’ye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the stray dogs and jackals will then become very much delighted, and in great ecstasy they will make a festival ground out of my body and will have a huge celebration and feast.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je deher ei gati, tāra anugata</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsār-vaibhava āro bondhu-jon jata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just see, this is the ultimate destination of this material body. And the most amazing thing is that all of my material opulences, house, family and friends have exactly the same destination.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ataeva māyā-moha chāḍi buddhimān</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-tattva kṛṣṇa-bhakti koruna sandhān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Therefore I ask of anyone who has any sharp intelligence: please give up all of these temporary illusions presented by Māyā, and kindly search after the means to get pure devotion to Lord Krishna, for this is the only really tangible eternal truth.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-anya-abhilasha-chadi-jniana-karma-parihari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Anya-abhilāṣa chāḍi <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anya-abhilāṣa chāḍi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna karma parihari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāya mane koribo bhajana</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-saṅge kṛṣṇa-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pūjibo devī-devā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei bhakti parama-kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Worship the Lord with body and mind, giving up the desire for material endeavour and mundane knowledge. Perform service in the association of devotees and do not worship the various demigods. These activities are the prime cause of devotion to Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahājanera jei patha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā’te ho’be anurata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrvāpara koriyā vichāra</strong><br>
<strong>sādhana-smaraṇa-līlā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ihāte nā kara helā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāya mane koriyā sūsāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The essence of devotional practice is to carefully consider and follow the path revealed by the great devotees. One should not neglect the practice of remembering the Pastimes of the Lord, for devotional service should be performed with the mind as well as the body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-saṅga sadā tyāga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍa anya gīta-rāga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karmī jñānī parihari dūre</strong><br>
<strong>kevala bhakata-saṅga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-kathā-rasa-raṅga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;līlā-kathā vraja-rasa-pure</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Always give up bad association and avoid singing mundane songs. Keep at a distance from materialistic people as well as those absorbed with mundane knowledge. Associate only with the devotees and merge in the happiness of topics of the loving Pastimes of Krishna in Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jogī nyāsī karmī jñānī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anya-deva-pūjaka dhyānī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;iha-loka dūre parihari</strong><br>
<strong>karma dharma duḥkha śoka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jebā thāke anya joga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍi’ bhaja girivara-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please give up the association of the <i>yogīs</i>, <i>sannyāsīs</i>, <i>karmīs</i>, <i>jñānīs</i>, meditators, and the worshippers of the demigods. Also abandon attachment for all varieties of fruitive activities, religious duties, distresses, lamentations and material objects. Just worship Krishna, the lifter of Govardhan Hill.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tīrtha-jātrā pariśrama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala-manera-bhrama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarva-siddhi govinda-charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dṛdha-viśvāsa hṛde dhori&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada-mātsarya parihari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā kara ananya bhajana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Travelling to the holy places is only so much hard labour to attain some mental illusion when really the lotus feet of Śrī Govinda are the perfection of one’s life. One should give up pride and envy and with firm determination in the heart, one should always perform unalloyed worship of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-bhakta saṅga kori&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-bhakta saṅga heri</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śraddhānvita śravaṇa-kīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>archana vandana dhyāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nava-bhakti mahā-jñāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei bhakti parama-kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The best process of devotional service is to hear, chant, worship, glorify and meditate with faith on the Names, Forms, Qualities and Pastimes of Krishna in the association of the devotees of Krishna through the nine forms of bhakti.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hṛsīke govinda-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pūjibo devī-devā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei to’ ananya-bhakti-kathā</strong><br>
<strong>āra jata upālambha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśeṣa sakali dambha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhite lāgaye mane vyathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Engage the senses in the service of Śrī Govinda and do not worship the various demigods. This principle is in the line of unalloyed devotion. All other activities are simply done through pride and to see them gives great pain to one’s heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dehe baise ripū-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jateka indriya-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keho kāra vādhya nāhi hoy</strong><br>
<strong>śunile nā śune kāṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jānile nā jāne prāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;daḍhāite nā pāre niścoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The six enemies; lust, anger, greed, illusion, pride and envy and the five senses of sight, sound, smell, taste, and touch reside in my body but I am unable to control them. Although I hear and understand repeatedly that one should serve Krishna with all his senses, still I cannot accept this fact with firm determination.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāma krodha moha lobha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada mātsarya dambha-saha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāne sthāne nijūkta koribo</strong><br>
<strong>ānanda kori hṛdoy&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ripu’ kori’ parājaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anāyāse govinda bhajibo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will engage lust, anger, greed, illusion, envy and pride in their proper places. In this way, I will defeat the enemies and with ecstasy in my heart, I will worship Govinda without difficulty.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāma kṛṣṇa-karmārpaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodha bhakta-dveśi-jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobha sādhu-saṅge hari-kathā</strong><br>
<strong>moha iṣṭa-lābha-bine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada kṛṣṇa-guṇa-gāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nijūkta koribo jathā tathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will engage my lust in eagerness to serve Krishna and I will use my anger against those who are envious of the devotees. I will be greedy to hear the topics of Hari in the association of the devotees. I will be illusioned if I fail to achieve my worshipable Lord and I will feel proud to chant the glories of Krishna. In this way, I will engage them in their respective duties.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anyathā svatantra kāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anarthādi jāra dhāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-pathe sadā deya bhaṅga</strong><br>
<strong>kibā bā korite pāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāma-krodha sādhakere</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jadi hoy sādhu-janāra saṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Otherwise, independent lusty desires, which are the source of all unwanted things will always disturb the path of devotional service. What harm can lusty desires and anger do to a practioner of devotional service if they associate with devotees?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>krodha bā nā kore kibā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodha-tyāga sadā dibā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobha moha ei to’ kathana</strong><br>
<strong>choya ripū sadā hīna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koribo maner adhīna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇachandra koriyā smaraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Anger spoils everything. Therefore, I will always give up anger, greed and illusion. I will control the six enemies with the help of my mind while remembering Lord Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āpani palābe saba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuniyā govinda raba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;siṁha-rabe jena kori-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>sakali vipatti jābe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mahānanda sūkha pābe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāra hoy ekānta bhajana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the enemies will run away by hearing the sound vibration of Govinda’s name just as a deer flees upon hearing the roar of a lion. One who performs unalloyed devotional service will feel great happiness and all his dangers will be vanquished.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā koriho asat-cheṣṭa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lābha pūjā pratiṣṭhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā chinta govinda-charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>sakala santāpa jābe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parānanda sukha pābe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-bhakti parama-kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Therefore my dear mind, do not endeavour for bad association, profit, adoration and distinction, but always remember the lotus feet of Govinda. Please engage in loving devotional service with great happiness and all your dangers will be destroyed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-kriya kuṭināṭi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍa anya paripāṭī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anya deve nā koriho rati</strong><br>
<strong>āpana āpana sthāne&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pirīti sabāi ṭāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-pathe paḍaye bigati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please give up all duplicity, illusory activities and the search for bodily happiness. Do not be attached to the worship of various demigods. Mundane love and affection for material relationships attracts everyone to their respective illusions and thus advancement on the path of devotional service is checked.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āpana bhajana-patha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe hobo anūrata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;iṣṭa-deva-sthāne-līlā-gāna</strong><br>
<strong>naiṣṭhika bhajana ei&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāre kohinū bhāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hanumān tāhāte pramāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Be continuously engaged on the path of devotional worship and be attached to glorifying the Pastimes of Śrī Krishna in Vṛndāvan. This process of devotional service is known as steady devotional service. The prime example of this is Hanumān.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-pita-varana-kali-pavana-gora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 1</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The first verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>cheto-darpaṇa-mārjanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava-mahā-dāvāgni-nirvāpaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śreyaḥ-kairava-chandrikā-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vitaraṇaṁ vidyā-vadhu-jīvanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prati-padaṁ pūrṇāmṛtāsvādanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sarvātma-snapanaṁ paraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name of Krishna cleanses the mirror of the heart and extinguishes the fire of misery in the forest of birth and death. As the evening lotus blooms in the moon’s cooling rays, the heart begins to blossom in the nectar of the Name.<br>
And at last the soul awakens to its real inner treasure — a life of love with Krishna. Again and again tasting nectar, the soul dives and surfaces in the ever-increasing ocean of ecstatic joy.<br>
All phases of the self of which we may conceive are fully satisfied and purified, and at last conquered by the all-auspicious influence of the Holy Name of Krishna.<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=12&p=12" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: The Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa</a></i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pīta-varaṇa kali-pāvana gorā</strong><br>
<strong>gāoyāi aichana bhāva-bibhorā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga, whose complexion is golden and who is the deliverer of the fallen souls of <i>Kali-yuga</i>, sings as follows, overwhelmed with spiritual ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chitta-darpaṇa-parimārjana-kārī</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya chitta-bihārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! It thoroughly cleanses the mirror of the heart and is the delight of the soul.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>helā-bhava-dāva-nirvāpaṇa-vṛtti</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya kleśa-nivṛtti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! It extinguishes the forest fire of material existence and removes all material tribulations.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śreyaḥ-kumuda-bidhu-jyotsnā-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya bhakti-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! It appears like the moon in the heart and distributes its cooling moonlight, making the white lotus of the soul’s good fortune blossom and bloom. Krishna-<i>kīrtan</i> is the <i>bhakti-vilāsa</i>, the beautiful pastime of devotion.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśuddha-vidyā-vadhu jīvana-rūpa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya siddha-svarūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! It reveals one’s pure identity in relationship with the Lord even up to divine consorthood. This chanting is the real perfection of life.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānanda-payo-nidhi-vardhana-kīrti</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya plābana-mūrti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! Krishna-<i>kīrtan</i> causes the ocean of ecstatic joy to overflow. It is an inundation of divine love.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pade pade pījuṣa-svāda-pradātā</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya prema-bidhātā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! Krishna-<i>kīrtan</i> gives one a taste of fully satisfying nectar at every step; ultimately it bestows ecstatic love of Godhead.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda-svātma-snapana-vidhān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya prema-nidān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Krishna! It bathes the soul of Bhakti Vinod. This chanting is a storehouse of love of Godhead.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tuhu-daya-sagara-tarayite-prani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 2</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The second verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmnām akāri bahudhā nija-sarva-śaktis</strong><br>
<strong>tatrārpitā niyamitaḥ smaraṇe na kālaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>etādṛśī tava kṛpā bhagavan mamāpi</strong><br>
<strong>durdaivam īdṛśam ihājani nānurāgaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Oh my Lord, Your Holy Name bestows auspiciousness upon all. And You have unlimited Names such as Krishna and Govinda by which You reveal Yourself. In Your many Holy Names You have kindly invested all Your transcendental potency. And in chanting these Names, there are no strict rules concerning time or place. Out of Your causless mercy, You have descended in the form of divine sound, but my great misfortune is that I have no love for Your Holy Name.”<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=13&p=10" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: Unlimited Names of God</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuhu̐ dayā-sāgara tārayite prāṇī</strong><br>
<strong>nāma aneka tuyā śikhāoli āni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! You are an ocean of mercy, and so you have brought the teachings of the glories of your unlimited Holy Names to deliver all souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala śakati dei nāme tohārā</strong><br>
<strong>grahaṇe rākholi nāhi kāla-vichārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You have conferred all Your power upon Your Name. And there are no rules governing the appropriate time or place for chanting the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-nāma-chintāmaṇi tohāri samānā</strong><br>
<strong>biśve bilāoli karuṇā-nidānā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name is a <i>chintāmaṇi</i> gem, a divine touchstone, and is nondifferent from You. Out of Your kindness for all souls, You have distributed Your Holy Name throughout the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuyā dayā aichana parama udārā</strong><br>
<strong>atiśoya manda nātha! bhāga hāmārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is Your mercy, O Lord. You are supremely kind. But, O Lord, I am most wicked and unfortunate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāhi janamalo nāme anurāga mora</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktivinoda-chitta duḥkhe bibhora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have never known any attraction for the Holy Names. And so, the heart of Bhakti Vinod is filled with sadness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-kirtane-jodi-manasa-tohara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 3</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The third verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇād api sunīchena</strong><br>
<strong>taror iva sahiṣṇunā</strong><br>
<strong>amāninā mānadena</strong><br>
<strong>kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who is humbler than a blade of grass, more forbearing than a tree who gives due honour to others without desiring it for himself is qualified to always chant the Holy Name of Krishna.<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=14&p=8" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: Humbler Than a Blade of Grass</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-kīrtane jodi mānasa tohāra</strong><br>
<strong>parama jatane tā̐hi lobho adhikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If your mind is always carefully absorbed in the chanting of the Holy Name, then you will become qualified for chanting the Holy Name of Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭṛṇādhika hīna, dīna, akiñchana chāra</strong><br>
<strong>āpane mānobi sadā chāḍi’ ahaṅkāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humbler than a blade of grass considering yourself low, poor, and meek, give up your false ego.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛkṣa-sama kṣamā-guṇa karabi sādhana</strong><br>
<strong>pratihiṁsā tyaji anye karabi pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Tolerant as a tree, you should forgive and forbear, giving up violence. Practice nurturing and protecting others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana-nirvāhe āne udvega nā dibe</strong><br>
<strong>para-upakāre nija-sukha pāsaribe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Live without causing anxiety to others; do good unto others without considering your own happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hoile-o sarva-guṇe guṇī mahāśoya</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhāśā chāḍi kara amānī hṛdoya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A great soul who has all these qualities should not become proud of his saintly qualities, but, giving up position and prestige he should always remain prideless within his heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-adhiṣṭhān sarva-jīve jāni sadā</strong><br>
<strong>karabi sammāna sobe ādare sarvadā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Always knowing that Krishna dwells within the hearts of all living beings, you should always properly respect and honour others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>doinya, dayā, anye mān, pratiṣṭhā-varjan</strong><br>
<strong>chāri guṇe guṇī hoi karaho kīrtan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humility, mercy, consideration of others, and giving up position are the four qualities needed to perform <i>kīrtan</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda kā̐di bole prabhu-pāya</strong><br>
<strong>heno adhikāra kobe dibe he āmāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, crying at the Lord’s holy feet says, “O my Lord, when will You bestow upon me the qualifications for Krishna-<i>kīrtan</i>?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-prabhu-tava-pada-juge-mora-nivedana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 4</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The fourth verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>na dhanaṁ na janaṁ na sundarīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kavitāṁ vā jagad-īśa kāmaye</strong><br>
<strong>mama janmani janmanīśvare</strong><br>
<strong>bhavatād bhaktir ahaitukī tvayi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Oh Lord, I have no desires to accumulate wealth, followers, beautiful women, or salvation. My only prayer is for Your causeless devotional service, birth after birth.”<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=15&p=8" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: Unalloyed Devotion</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prabhu tava pada-juge mora nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi māgi deho-sukha, vidyā dhana, jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! This is my humble submission at Your lotus feet. I do not ask from You sensual pleasure, learning, wealth or followers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāhi māgi svarga, āro mokṣa nāhi māgi</strong><br>
<strong>nā kori prārthanā kono vibhūtira lāgi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I do not beg for residence on the celestial planets, nor do I wish liberation from this mundane existence. Nor do I pray for the attainment of any mystic powers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-karma-guṇa-doṣe je je janma pāi</strong><br>
<strong>janme janme jeno tava nāma-guṇa gāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever birth I may obtain due to the results of my previous worldly activities, I pray that I may sing the glories of Your Holy Name birth after birth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei mātra āśā mama tomāra charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ahaitukī bhakti hṛde jāge anukṣaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This alone is my cherished hope, my aspiration, my prayer at Your lotus feet: Let causeless and uninterrupted devotion awaken within my heart and flow towards You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>biṣoye je prīti ebe āchoye āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>sei-mata prīti hauk charaṇe tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Let me love Your lotus feet as much as I now love sense gratification; transfer my affection from the objects of the senses to Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vipade sampade tāhā thākuka samabhāve</strong><br>
<strong>dine dine bṛddhi hauk nāmera prabhāve</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In danger or success, good fortune or disaster, let me remain equipoised. And let my affection for You increase day by day by the influence of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>paśu-pakṣī hoye thāki svarge bā niroye</strong><br>
<strong>tava bhakti rahu bhaktivinoda-hṛdoye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whether I live as bird or beast, in heaven or in hell, let the humble Bhakti Vinod always cherish devotion to You in his heart of hearts.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-anadi-karama-phole" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 5</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The fifth verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>ayi nanda-tanuja kiṅkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>patitaṁ māṁ viṣame bhavāmbudhau</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpayā tava pāda-paṅkaja-</strong><br>
<strong>sthita-dhūli-sadṛśaṁ vichintaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Oh son of Nanda Mahārāj, I am Your eternal servant, yet because of my own <i>karma</i>, I have fallen into this terrible ocean of birth and death. Please accept this fallen soul and consider me a particle of dust at Your holy lotus feet.”<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=16&p=8" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: King of the Land of Love</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anādi karama-phole,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍi bhavārṇava-jole,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;toribāre nā dekhi upāy,</strong><br>
<strong>e-viṣoya-holāhole,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divā-niśi hiyā jvole,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mon kobhu sukha nāhi pāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As a result of my selfish fruitive activities, which are without beginning, I have fallen into the ocean of material existence. And now I can see no means of deliverance. Day and night my heart burns from the vicious poison of these worldly affairs, and due to this my mind never finds any true happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āśā-pāśa-śata-śata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kleśa deya abirata,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pravṛti-ūrmira tāhe khelā</strong><br>
<strong>kāma-krodha-ādi choy,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bāṭapāḍe dey bhoy,</strong><br>
