<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abasāna hoilo āsi belā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am bound by hundreds and hundreds of desires that give unending distress and these desires play in the waves of ever-increasing materialistic tendency. Lust, anger, greed, pride, illusion and envy are like six highway robbers making me greatly fearful, and in this way my life is coming to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jñāna-karma—ṭhāga dui,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;more pratāriyā loi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśeṣe phele sindhu-jole</strong><br>
<strong>e heno somoye, bandhu,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi kṛṣṇa kṛpā-sindhu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā kori’ tolo more bole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two highway robbers named intellectual knowledge and fruitive activity have cheated and misled me, and finally they have thrown me into the ocean of misery. At such a time as this, dear Krishna, You are my only friend. You are an ocean of mercy. Please be kind upon me and uplift me from this condition of suffering by Your divine power.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-kiṅkore dhori’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-padma-dhūli kori’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deho bhaktivinode āśroy</strong><br>
<strong>āmi tava nitya-dās&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuliyā māyāra pāś,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baddha ho’ye āchi dayāmoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am Your eternal servant, Oh merciful Lord. But, having forgotten this since time immemorial, I have become bound up in this network of illusion. Take this fallen servant of Yours, give him a place in the dust of Your lotus feet; give your shelter to this humble Bhakti Vinod.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-aparadha-phole-mama" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 6</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The sixth verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>nayanaṁ galad-aśru-dhārayā</strong><br>
<strong>vadanaṁ gadgada-ruddhayā girā</strong><br>
<strong>pulakair nichitaṁ vapuḥ kadā</strong><br>
<strong>tava nāma-grahaṇe bhaviṣyati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Oh Lord, when will tears flow from my eyes like waves, And my voice tremble in ecstasy? When will the hairs of my body stand on end while chanting Your Holy Name?”<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=17&p=8" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: Hankering for Perfection</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aparādha-phole mama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta bhela vajra sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā nāme nā lobhe bikāra</strong><br>
<strong>hatāś hoiya hari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava nāma ucha kori</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;boḍo duḥkhe ḍāki bāro bāro</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord Hari, asa result of my offences incurred in previous lifetimes, my heart has become as hard as a thunderbolt, and feels no change upon chanting Your Holy Name. Disappointed, hopeless, and in great distress, I loudly call Your Name again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīna dayāmoy karuṇā-nidān</strong><br>
<strong>bhāva-bindu dei rākhoho parāṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord, you are compassionate towards the fallen souls and the origin of mercy. Please give me a drop of divine ecstasy and thereby save my life!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobe tuyā nāma-uchāraṇe mor</strong><br>
<strong>nayane jharabo daro daro lor</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will an incessant stream of tears flow from my eyes as I chant Your Name in divine ecstasy?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gada-gada-svara kaṇṭhe upajabo</strong><br>
<strong>mukhe bolo ādha ādha bāhirābo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my voice be choked with divine emotion, and when will words garbled due to ecstasy come from my mouth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pulake bharabo śorīra hāmāra</strong><br>
<strong>sveda-kampa-stambha hobe bāro bāro</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will the hairs of my body stand up in ecstasy? When will I become stunned with ecstasy, shiver and perspire out of the ecstasy of divine love? When will these symptoms of ecstasy overwhelm me again and again?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bibarṇa-śorīre hārāobu̐ jñāna</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-samāśraye dharobu parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my body turn colours and become pale out of ecstasy? When will I lose consciousness? And when will my very life be under the shelter of the Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>milabo hāmār kiye aiche dina</strong><br>
<strong>roye bhakativinoda mati hīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, who is devoid of good intelligence cries, “When will I meet with such a day?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaite-gaite-nama-ki-doshia-hoilo" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 7a</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The seventh verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>yugāyitaṁ nimeṣeṇa</strong><br>
<strong>chakṣuṣā prāvṛṣāyitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śūnyāyitaṁ jagat sarvaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-viraheṇa me</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Oh Govinda! Without You, the world is empty. Tears are flooding my eyes like rain, and a moment seems like forever.”<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=18&p=8" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: Forever Without You</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāite gāite nāma ki dośā hoilo</strong><br>
<strong>‘kṛṣṇa-nitya-dāsa mui’ hṛdoye sphurilo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Chanting the Name again and again, what was my condition? Within my heart I realised, “I am an eternal servant of Krishna.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jānilām māyā-pāśe e jaḍa-jagate</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-virahe duḥkha pāi nānā-mate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I realised that I was bound by Māyā’s ropes, in this dull mundane universe, and I simply experience misery in various ways due to separation from Lord Govinda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āro je saṁsāra mora nāhi lāge bhālo</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐hā jāi kṛṣṇa heri e chintā biśālo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And I don’t much like this world of birth and death. Where shall I go to see Krishna? This was my biggest worry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐dite kā̐dite mora ā̐khi-boriṣoya</strong><br>
<strong>barṣā-dhārā heno chakṣe hoilo udoya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I began weeping and weeping; my tears fell. Torrents of rain poured from my eyes just like the rains of the monsoon season.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nimeṣa hoilo mora śata-juga-sam</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-viraha āra sohite akṣam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now, a moment for me is like a hundred ages. I cannot bear this separation from Lord Govinda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śūnya dharā-tala,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudike dekhiye,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parāṇa udāsa hoya</strong><br>
<strong>ki kori, ki kori,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthira nāhi hoya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana nāhiko roya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The world is empty; as I survey the four directions, the entire surface of the world is void. My life is empty, my mind is listless; I am indifferent and apathetic towards everything. Depressed, melancholy and forlorn, my life has no meaning now. What do I do now? I cannot remain steady. I can no longer maintain my life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-vāsi-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora prāṇa rākho</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhāo śrī-rādhā-nāthe</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;minati māniyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laohe tāhāre sāthe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh residents of Vṛndāvan; save my life and show me Rādhānāth, the Lord of Śrī Rādhā. Considering this humble prayer of Bhakti Vinod, please give him Your association and take him with You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī kṛṣṇa-viraha āro sohite nā pāri</strong><br>
<strong>parāṇa chāḍite āro din dui chāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am unable to further tolerate this separation from my Lord Krishna and am ready to give up my life in two days or four.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaite-govinda-nam-upajilo-bhava-gram" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 7b</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāite ‘govinda’-nām,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upajilo bhāva-grām,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhilām yamunāra kūle</strong><br>
<strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutā-saṅge,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śyāma-naṭa-boro-raṅge,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bā̐śarī bājāya nīpo-mūle</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And while I sang the name of Govinda a host of ecstasies arose within me. I saw Sundar-śyāma the best of dancers, on Yamunā’s shores, with Śrī Rādhā, Vṛṣabhānu’s daughter, sporting in Pastimes of love playing His flute beside the water while a kadamba tree above cast shade upon the divine couple.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dekhiyā jugala-dhana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asthira hoilo mana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna hārā hoilu̐ tokhon</strong><br>
<strong>koto-kṣane nāhi jāni,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna-labha hoilo māni,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro nāhi bhelo daraśan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On seeing this, my mind was stunned. I lost my senses. Unaware of time, I fainted. When I awoke after immeasurable time, the treasured couple had gone. My vision of Vraja had broken.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sakhi-go-kemote-dhoribo-paran" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 7c</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakhi go kemote dhoribo parāṇ</strong><br>
<strong>nimeṣa hoilo jugera samān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my dear girlfriend! How will I  maintain my life? The passing of a mere moment has become like a vast age of time.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrāvaṇera dhārā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ā̐khi-bariṣoy,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūnya bhelo dharā-tala</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-virahe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa nāhi rohe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemone bā̐chibo bolo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Tears flow from my eyes like torrents of rain in the month of Śrāvaṇa, and the face of the earth has become totally void. In separation from Govinda my life cannot go on... please, tell how I will be able to live.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asthira hoiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;punaḥ nāmāśroya kori’</strong><br>
<strong>ḍāke rādhānātha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyā daraśana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa rākho, nahe mori</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod has become very restless and again takes shelter of the holy name, calling out, “Oh Lord of Rādhā! By bestowing Your audience, please save my life! By Your mercy alone I shall not perish!”<br>
Footnote to verse 1: <i>Śrīla Śrīdhar Mahārāja advised not to sing ‘sakhi go’ but ‘bandhu he’ instead.</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bondhu-gon-shiunoho-vachana-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 8a</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation">The eighth verse of the Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam is as follows:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>āśliṣya vā pāda-ratāṁ pinaṣṭu mām</strong><br>
<strong>adarśanān marma-hatāṁ karotu vā</strong><br>
<strong>yathā tathā vā vidadhātu lampaṭo</strong><br>
<strong>mat-prāṇa-nāthas tu sa eva nāparaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Krishna may embrace me in love or trample me under His feet. He may break my heart by hiding Himself from me. Let that debauchee do whatever He likes, but He will always be the only Lord of my life.”<br>
<i>Illumination in the book: <a href="https://krishnasearch.com/#/book/en-GoldenVolcanoofDivineLove?c=19&p=8" target="_blank">The Golden Volcano of Divine Love. Part II Śikṣāṣṭakam: Union in Separation</a>.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bondhu-goṇ!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śunoho vachana mora</strong><br>
<strong>bhāvete bibhora, thākiye jokhon,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhā deya chitta-chora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my friends! Listen to my words. I am filled to overflowing with devotional ecstasy when I have a divine vision of that thief who has stolen my heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bichakṣaṇa kori’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhite chāhile,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoya ā̐khi-agochara</strong><br>
<strong>punaḥ nāhi dekhi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐daye parāṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duḥkhera nā thāke ora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And when I want to see Him more clearly, He disappears from sight and becomes invisible. Again, not seeing Him, my soul weeps and my grief knows no bounds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagatera bandhu sei kobhu more loya sātha</strong><br>
<strong>yathā tathā rākhu more āmāra se prāṇanātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes Krishna, the friend of the universe, takes me as one of His own, and sometimes He ignores me, but however He keeps me, He is the only Lord of my life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>darśan-ānanda-dāne,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukha dey mora prāṇe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bole more praṇoya-vachan</strong><br>
<strong>punaḥ adarśan diyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dagdha kore mora hiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇe more māre prāṇa-dhan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When He gives His blissful audience to me, He gives happiness to my soul. He gives me great ecstasy by allowing me to see Him and by speaking affectionately to me. Again He goes away, removing Himself from my sight. And by not letting me see Him, He burns my heart with the fire of separation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāhe tā’ra sukha hoy, sei sukha mama</strong><br>
<strong>nija sukhe-duḥkhe mora sarvadāi sama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">But even though He pains my soul in this way, He is still the Lord of my life. Whatever is His happiness is also my happiness. And so, for me, my own happiness and distress is all the same.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁjoge bijoge,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe jāne prāṇeśvara</strong><br>
<strong>tā’ra sukhe sukhī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei prāṇa-nātha,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se kobhu nā hoya paro</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this way, Bhakti Vinod, in both union and separation, knows that Krishna is the only Lord of his soul. Krishna’s happiness is my happiness. He is the Lord of my life; there is no other Lord for me than Him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yoga-pithopari-sthita-ashta-sakhi-subeshtita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 8b</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yoga-pīṭhopari-sthita,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aṣṭa-sakhī-subeṣṭita,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāraṇye kadamba-kānane</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-saha vaṁśī-dhārī, biśva-jana-chitta-hārī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa mora tā̐hāra charaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the yoga-pīṭha, surrounded by the aṣṭa-sakhīs, in Vṛndāvan Forest, in a grove of kadamba trees, by the side of Śrī Rādhā, holding His flute, Śrī Krishna enchants all souls within the universe. I fall at His feet and surrender my life to Him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakhī-ājñāmata kori do̐hāra sevana</strong><br>
<strong>pālya-dāsī sadā bhāvi do̐hāra charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Following the orders of a sakhī, I engage in service to the holy pair, Śrī Rādhā and Krishna, always considering myself a dependent maidservant at Their lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobhu kṛpā kori,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mama hasta dhori’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhura vachana bole</strong><br>
<strong>tāmbula loiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāya dui jone,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mālā loya kutūhole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes being kind, They take me by the hand and speak sweet words to me out of affection. Taking the betel nuts I have prepared, the two of Them eat and accept a garland from me with great delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adarśana hoya kokhona ki chole</strong><br>
<strong>nā dekhiyā do̐he hiyā mora jvole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Again, They disappear from my vision by some trick. Not seeing Rādhā and Krishna, my heart burns in agony.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jekhāne sekhāne,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākuka du’ jane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi to charaṇa-dāsī</strong><br>
<strong>milane ānanda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virahe jātanā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakala sammāna vāsi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Here or there, wherever They stay, I am the maidservant of Their lotus feet. Through the happiness of union and the agony of separation, I am Their maidservant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa prāṇa mor jīvane maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>more rākhi’ māri’ sukhe thākuka du’ jone</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Rādhā and Krishna are my life and soul in life and in death; whether They protect me or take my life—may they always be happy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āna nāhi jāne,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;poḍi’ nija-sakhī-pāy</strong><br>
<strong>rādhikāra goṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā satata,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jugala-charaṇa chāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod knows nothing else. Falling at the feet of the sakhī, Bhakti Vinod stays among the intimate confidants of Śrī Rādhā. The lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā and Krishna is Bhakti Vinod’s eternal desire.<br>
Footnote to Song 8b: <i>Śrīla Śrīdhar Mahārāja advised not to sing this song because it’s “too high.”</i></p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Songs to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-radhika-charana-padma-sakala-shreyera" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 1</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-charaṇa-padma,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakala śreyera sadma,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jatane je nāhi ārādhilo</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-padāṅkita dhāma,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana jār nāma,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā je nā āśroya korilo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā are the abode of all auspiciousness, the supreme goal of life. One who in his life has failed to take shelter of that holy <i>dhāma</i> named Vṛndāvan which is decorated by the lotus-like Rādhā,…</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-bhāva-gambhīra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta jebā mahādhīra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;goṇ-soṅga nā koilo jīvane</strong><br>
<strong>kemone se śyāmānanda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasa-sindhu-snānānanda,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobhibe bujhoho ek-mane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">…who in his life has failed to associate with the intimate of Śrī Rādhā who are deeply filled with Rādhā’s mood of devotion and who are greatly intelligent—how will such a person bathe in the rasa-sindhu, the ocean of ecstatic joy which is Śyāmasundar? You must singlemindedly understand this point.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā ujjvala-rasera āchārya</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-mādhava-śuddha-prema vichārya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Rādhā is the exemplary teacher of the mellows of conjugal love. Pure ecstatic love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava is what is to be considered and discussed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je dhorilo rādhā-pada parama jatane</strong><br>
<strong>se pāilo kṛṣṇa-pada amūlya-ratane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who holds tightly to the lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā with great care will certainly attain the priceless jewels of the lotus feet of Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-pada vinā kobhu kṛṣṇa nāhi mile</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra dāsīr kṛṣṇa sarva-vede bole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Without taking shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā, one will never meet Krishna. All the Vedas say that Krishna belongs to the maidservants of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>choḍata dhona-jon,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kalatra-suta-mita,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;choḍata karama geyān</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-pada-paṅkaja,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhurata sevana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakativinoda paramāṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Having given up wealth, followers, wife, children, family, and friends, material activities and intellectual knowledge, one should become absorbed in the divine sweetness of service to the lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā as one of Her maidservants in <i>madhura-rasa</i>. This is Bhakti Vinod’s solemn declaration.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-virajar-pare-shiuddha-paravyoma-dham" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 2</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>virajār pāre śuddha-paravyoma-dhām</strong><br>
<strong>tad upari śrī-gokula-vṛndāraṇya nām</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beyond the Virajā River lies the pure spiritual sky, and above that Vaikuṇṭha realm lies the divine abode known as Śrī Goloka Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāvana chintāmaṇi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chid-ānanda-ratna-khani,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chinmoy apūrva-daraśan</strong><br>
<strong>ta̐hi mājhe chamatkār,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa vanaspati sār,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nīla-maṇi tamāla jemon</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The land of Vṛndāvan is made of spiritual gems and is therefore likened to a mine of fully cognizant and blissful jewels. This transcendentally conscious realm is certainly a wonderful and extraordinary sight. Within that abode is the most astonishing presence of Lord Krishna, who is compared to a <i>tamāl</i> tree, the king of trees possessing the hue of a dark sapphire.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tāhe eka svarna-mayī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;latā sarva-dhāma-jayī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uthiyāche parama-pāvanī</strong><br>
<strong>hlādinī-śaktir sār,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘mahābhāva’ nām jār,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tribhuvana-mohana-mohinī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Entwined upon that blackish tree a beautiful golden creeper has arisen, who is the conqueror of all realms, being the supreme purifier. Her name is Mahābhāva, being the essence of the supreme pleasure-giving hlādinī potency. She is the enchantress of Śrī Krishna, who is Himself the enchanter of the three worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-nāme parichita,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuṣiyā govinda-chita,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virājaye parama ānande</strong><br>
<strong>sei latā-patra-phul,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lalitādi sakhī-kul,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sobe mili’ vṛkṣe dṛḍha bāndhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Known by the name of Rādhā, She remains shining there in great ecstasy, always engaged in satisfying Govinda’s heart. The leaves and flowers of that creeper form the assembly of Her girlfriends headed by Lalitā. Together She and all Her friends entwine that blackish tree in a tight embrace.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>latāra paraśe praphulla tamāl</strong><br>
<strong>latā chāḍi’ nāhi rohe kono kāl</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the touch of this creeper, the Tamāl tree blooms; without the embrace of the creeper, He can no longer exist.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tamāla chaḍiyā latā nāhi bā̐che</strong><br>
<strong>se latā milan sadā-kāla jāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The creeper never desires to leave the company of the tamāl tree; the creeper perpetually craves Their union.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda milana do̐hār</strong><br>
<strong>nā chāhe kokhona vinā kichu ār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Other than the meeting of these two, Bhakti Vinod desires but nothing else.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-bhajane-jodi-moti-nahi-bhela" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song 8</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-bhajane jodi moti nāhi bhelā</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-bhajana tava akāraṇe gelā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If one does not develop within his heart the desire for Rādhā-bhajan, then his Krishna-bhajan goes for nothing.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātapa-rohita sūray nāhi jāni</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-birahita mādhav nāhi māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Without sunlight the sun cannot be seen, similarly I don’t pay any mind to Krishna without Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kevala mādhav pūjaye so ajñānī</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā anādara karai abhimānī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who worships Krishna alone is ignorant. Who does not adore Śrī Rādhā is an egotist.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kobohi nāhi karabi tā̐kor saṅga</strong><br>
<strong>chitte ichāsi jadi vraja-rasa-raṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Never keep company with those who envy Rādhā if within your heart you at all aspire to the loving Pastimes of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-dāsī jodi hoy abhimān</strong><br>
<strong>śīghrai milai tava gokula kān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who consider themselves maidservants of Śrī Rādhā will quickly meet Gokul-Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>brahmā, śiva, nārada, śruti, nārāyaṇī</strong><br>
<strong>rādhikā-pada-raja pūjaye māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Brahmā, Shiva, Nārada, the śrutis, and Nārāyaṇī (Lakṣmī) worship with regard the holy feet of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>umā, ramā, satyā, śachī, chandrā, rukmiṇī</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-avatār sobe, —āmnāya-vāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Umā, Ramā, Satyā, Śachī, Chandrāvālī, and Rukminī are all personal expansions of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī — this is the self-evident verdict of the Vedas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>heno rādhā-paricharjyā jā̐kara dhana</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda tā̐ra māgaye charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, whose only wealth is the service of Śrī Rādhā, humbly prays to be a serving maid at Her lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radhakundatata-kunjakutira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Rādhā-kuṇḍataṭa-kuñja-kuṭīra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhākuṇḍataṭa-kuñjakuṭīra</strong><br>
<strong>govardhana-parvata jāmuna-tīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The grove-dwelling on the banks of Rādha-kuṇḍa, Govardhan mountain, the banks of River Yamunā;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kusuma-sarovara, mānasa-gaṅgā</strong><br>
<strong>kalinda-nandinī vipula-taraṅgā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kusuma-sarovar lake, Mānasa-gaṅgā river, The rolling waves of Yamunā, daughter of the sun;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaṁśīvaṭa, gokula, dhīrasamīra</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvana-taru latikā-vānīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Place of the Rāsa dance, and Gokula, the gentle breezes, The trees, vines, and Vetasa palms of Vraja;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>khaga-mṛgakula, malaya-bātāsa</strong><br>
<strong>mayura, bhramara, muralī-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The birds and animals, the sweet air of spring, The peacocks, bumblebees, the flute’s sweet song;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>veṇu, śṛṇga, padachihna, meghamālā</strong><br>
<strong>vasanta, śaśāṅka, śaṅkha karatālā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Flute, horn, His footsteps, range of clouds in the sky, Season of spring, the moon, the conch, hand-cymbals;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jugala vilāse anukūla jāni</strong><br>
<strong>līlā-vilāsa-uddīpaka māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I know all these things are nourishing the Pastimes of the Divine Couple, I can feel they arouse the holy Pastimes of the Lord;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e soba choḍata kā̐hā nāhi jāu̐</strong><br>
<strong>e soba choḍata parāṇa hārāu̐</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I can never leave all these things so dear to me, Without them I know I’ll surely die;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktivinoda kohe, śuno kāna</strong><br>
<strong>tuyā uddīpaka hāmārā parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hear me, O Kāna, says Śrī Bhakti Vinod: You are the only light of my life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radhe-jaya-jaya-madhava-dayite" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhikā-stava <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhe jaya jaya mādhava-dayite</strong><br>
<strong>gokula-taruṇī-maṇḍala-mahite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Rādhā! O beloved of Mādhava! O You who are worshiped by all the young girls of Gokula! All glories unto You! All glories unto You!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dāmodara-rati-vardhana-veśe</strong><br>
<strong>hari-niṣkuṭa-vṛndā-vipineśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You who dress Yourself in such a way as to increase Lord Dāmodar’s love and attachment for You! O Queen of Vṛndāvan, which is the pleasure grove of Lord Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānūdadhi-nava-śaśi-lekhe</strong><br>
<strong>lalitā-sakhi guṇa-ramita-viśākhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O new moon who has arisen from the ocean of King Vṛṣabhānu! O friend of Lalitā! O You who make Viśākhā loyal to You due to Your wonderful qualities of friendliness, kindness, and faithfulness to Krishna!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karuṇāṁ kuru moyi karuṇā-bhorite</strong><br>
<strong>sanaka-sanātana-varṇita-charite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O You who are filled with compassion! O You whose divine characteristics are described by the great sages Sanaka and Sanātan! O Rādhā, please be merciful to me!</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Songs to Śrī Krishna</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-he-deva-bhavantam-vande" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>He deva bhavantaṁ vande <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he deva bhavantaṁ vande</strong><br>
<strong>man-mānasa-madhukara marpaya nija-pada-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paṅkaja-makarande</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord, I offer my respectful obeisances to You. May you place the bumblebee of my mind in the honey of Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadapi samādhiṣu vidhir api paśyati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na tava nakhā gramarīchim</strong><br>
<strong>idam ichāmi niśamya tavāchyuta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tad api kṛpādbhuta vīchim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although Brahmā in his <i>samādhi</i> cannot obtain a glimpse of even a ray of the tips of the nails of Your feet, still Oh Achyuta! Having heard of the waves of Your astonishing mercy, I yearn to see You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktir udañchati yad api mādhava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na tvayi mama tila-mātrī</strong><br>
<strong>parameśvaratā tad api tavādhika-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;durghaṭa-ghaṭana-vidhātrī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Mādhava, although I have not even a sesame seed of devotion for You, Your supreme power can make even the impossible become possible.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ayam avilola tayādya sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kalitādbhuta-rasa-bhāram</strong><br>
<strong>nivasatu nityam ihāmṛta-nindini</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vindan madhurima-sāram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh eternal Lord, may the bumblebee of my mind, finding there the most wonderful sweetness, eternally stay in the honey of Your lotus feet, which rebuke the sweetest nectar.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-madhava-kunja-bihari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>(jaya) rādhā-mādhava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) kuñja-bihārī</strong><br>
<strong>(jaya) gopī-jana-vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) giri-vara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>(jaya) yaśodā-nandana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) vraja-jana-rañjana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(jaya) jāmuna-tīra-vana-chārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the lover of Rādhā. He displays many amorous Pastimes in the groves of Vṛndāvan, He is the lover of the cowherd maidens of Vraja, the holder of the great hill named Govardhan, the beloved son of Mother Yaśodā, the delighter of the inhabitants of Vraja, and He wanders in the forests along the banks of the River Yamunā.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-mayura-mukuta-pitambara-dhari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mayura mukuṭa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mayura-mukuṭa pītāmbara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>muralīdhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Krishna holder of the flute, Muralī and lifter of Govardhan Hill! Oh Krishna who wears a crown adorned with a peacock feather dressed in golden coloured clothes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhā-mādhava kuñja-bihārī</strong><br>
<strong>muralīdhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Krishna holder of the flute, Muralī and lifter of Govardhan Hill! Oh Krishna the Lord of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī who plays in the groves of Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(jaya) yaśodā-nandana kṛṣṇa murāri</strong><br>
<strong>muralīdhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Krishna holder of the flute, Muralī and lifter of Govardhan Hill! All glories to you Krishna, son of Mother Yaśodā and killer of the Mura demon.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(jaya) gopī-jana-ballabha vaṁśī-bihārī</strong><br>
<strong>muralīdhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Krishna holder of the flute, Muralī and lifter of Govardhan Hill! All glories to you Krishna, dearmost lover of all the cowherd girls of Vṛndāvan and player of the flute Vaṁśī.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-radha-krishna-jugala-milan" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Jugala-ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya rādhā-kṛṣṇa jugala-milan</strong><br>
<strong>ārati karaye lalitādi sakhī-goṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the meeting of the transcendental couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā Krishna. The gopīs headed by Lalitā devī perform the ceremony of their worship.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madana-mohana rūpa tri-bhaṅga-sundara</strong><br>
<strong>pītāmbara śikhi-pucha-chūḍā-manohara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the attractor of Cupid, and His beautiful form is bending in three places with yellow garments and a charming peacock-feather crown.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalita-mādhava-bāme vṛṣabhānu-kanyā</strong><br>
<strong>nīla-basanā gaurī rūpe guṇe dhanyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dressed in blue garments, Her complexion fair, and Her virtues glorious, King Vṛṣabhānu’s daughter, Śrīmatī Rādhikā is on the left of the charming Lord Mādhava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nānā-bidha alaṅkāra kore jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>hari-mano-bimohana vadana ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Her various ornaments sparkle and Her effulgent face charms the heart of Lord Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśākhādi sakhī-goṇ nānā rāge gāya</strong><br>
<strong>priya-narma-sakhī jata chāmāra ḍhulāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Viśākhā and the other girls sing songs in various tunes and moods as Krishna’s intimate playful girlfriends fan the divine couple with whisks.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhā-mādhava-pada-sarasija-āśe</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda sakhī-pade sukhe bhāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Yearning to attain the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Krishna, Bhakti Vinod floats in happiness at the gopīs’ feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-krishna-prana-mora-jugala-kishora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sakhi-vṛnde Vijñaptiḥ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Prayer to the Gopīs</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa prāṇa mora jugala-kiśora</strong><br>
<strong>jīvane maraṇe gati āro nāhi mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The youthful Divine Couple Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Krishna are my life and soul. In life or death I have no other refuge but Them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kālindīra kūle keli-kadambera bon</strong><br>
<strong>ratana-bedira upara bosābo du’jon</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I shall install the Divine Couple on a jewelled throne in a pleasant forest of kadamba trees on the shore of the Yamunā river.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śyāma-gaurī-aṅge dibo chandanera gandha</strong><br>
<strong>chāmara ḍhulābo kobe heri mukha-chandra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I place aromatic sandalwood paste on the limbs of Lord Śyāmasundar and Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī? When will I fan Them with a <i>chāmara</i> wisk? When will I see Their moonlike faces?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāṅthiyā mālātir mālā dibo doṅhara gole</strong><br>
<strong>adhare tuliyā dibo karpūra-tāmbule</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I string garlands of malati flowers and place them on the necks of the Divine Couple? When will I place betel nuts mixed with camphor in Their lotus mouths?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalitā visākhā-ādi jata sakhī-vṛnda</strong><br>
<strong>ājñāya koribo sevā charaṇāravinda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, following the orders of Lalitā, Viśākhā and the other <i>gopīs</i>, will I serve the lotus feet of the Divine Couple?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-prabhur dāser anudāsa</strong><br>
<strong>sevā abhilāṣa kore narottama-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Narottam Dās, the servant of the servant of Śrī Krishna Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, longs for this service to the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-radhe-jaya-krishna-jaya-vrindavan" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Vraja-dhāma-mahimāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rādhe, jaya kṛṣṇa, jaya vṛndāvan</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-govinda, gopīnātha, madana-mohan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Rādha and Krishna and the divine forest of Vṛndāvan. All glories to the three presiding Deities of Vṛndāvan–Śrī Govinda, Gopīnāth, and Madana-Mohan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śyāma-kuṇḍa, rādhā-kuṇḍa, giri-govardhan</strong><br>
<strong>kālindī yamunā jaya, jaya mahāvan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śyāma-kuṇḍa, Rādhā-kuṇḍa, Govardhan Hill, and the Yamunā River (Kālindī). All glories to the great forest known as Mahāvan, where Krishna and Balarām displayed all of Their childhood Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>keśī-ghāṭa, vaṁśī-vaṭa, dvādaśa-kānan</strong><br>
<strong>jā̐hā sob līlā koilo śrī-nanda-nandan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Keśī-ghāṭa, where Krishna killed the Keśī demon. All glories to the Vaṁśī-vaṭa tree, where Krishna attracted all the gopīs to come by playing His flute. Glories to all of the twelve forests of Vraja. At these places the son of Nanda, Śrī Krishna, performed all of His Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-nanda-yaśodā jaya, jaya gopa-gaṇ</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdāmādi jaya, jaya dhenu-vatsa-gaṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Krishna’s divine father and mother, Nanda and Yaśodā. All glories to the cowherd boys, headed by Śrīdāmā, the older brother of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and Anaṅga Mañjarī. All glories to the cows and calves of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya vṛṣabhānu, jaya kīrtidā sundarī</strong><br>
<strong>jaya paurṇamāsī, jaya ābhīra-nāgarī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Rādhā’s divine father and mother, Vṛṣabhānu and the beautiful Kīrtidā. All glories to Paurṇamāsī, the mother of Sāndīpani Muni, grandmother of Madhumaṅgal and Nāndīmukhī, and beloved disciple of Devarṣi Nārada. All glories to the young cowherd maidens of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya gopeśvara vṛndāvana-mājh</strong><br>
<strong>jaya jaya kṛṣṇa-sakhā baṭu dvija-rāj</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to Gopeśvara Shiva, who resides in Vṛndāvan in order to protect the holy <i>dhām</i>. All glories, all glories to Krishna’s funny <i>brāhmaṇ</i> friend, Madhumaṅgal.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rāma-ghāṭa, jaya rohiṇī-nandan</strong><br>
<strong>jaya jaya vṛndāvana-vāsī jata jon</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Rāma-ghāṭa, where Lord Balarām performed His rāsa dance. All glories to Lord Balarām, the son of Rohiṇī. All glories, all glories to all of the residents of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya dvija-patnī, jaya nāga-konyā-goṇ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktite jā̐hārā pāilo govinda-charaṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the wives of the proud Vedic <i>brāhmaṇs</i>. All glories to the wives of the Kālīya serpent. Through pure devotion they all obtained the lotus feet of Lord Govinda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rāsa-maṇḍala jaya, jaya rādhā-śyām</strong><br>
<strong>jaya jaya rāsa-līlā sarva-manoram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the place where the rāsa dance of Śrī Krishna was performed. All glories to Rādhā and Śyāma. All glories, all glories to the divine rāsa dance, which is the most beautiful of all of Lord Krishna’s Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jayojvala-rasa sarva-rasa-sār</strong><br>
<strong>parakīyā-bhāve jāhā vrajete prachār</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the mellow of conjugal love, which is the most excellent of all rasas and is propagated in Vraja by Śrī Krishna in the form of the divine <i>parakīyā-bhāva</i> [paramour love].</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-jāhnavā-pāda-padma koriyā smaraṇ</strong><br>
<strong>dīna kṛṣṇadāsa kohe nāma-saṅkīrtan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remembering the lotus feet of Lord Nityānanda’s consort, Śrī Jāhnavā Devī, this very fallen and lowly Krishnadās sings the saṅkīrtan of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-janama-saphala-tara-krishna-darashiana-jara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Rūpa-Kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janama saphala tā’ra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-daraśana jā’ra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāgye hoiyāche eka-bāra</strong><br>
<strong>bikaśiyā hrin-nayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kori’ kṛṣṇa daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chāḍe jīva chittera bikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His birth is successful whose good fortune dawns so as to have the vision of Lord Krishna just once. When the soul gives up all delusions of the mind, then only will they see the vision of Krishna blooming within the eyes of their heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-keli chatur vanamālī</strong><br>
<strong>tribhaṅga-bhaṅgimārūpa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-dharī aparūpa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasamoya nidhi, guṇa-śālī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One sees Krishna there fully decorated with garlands of forest flowers as the most expert connoisseur of all the amorous love-sports in Vṛndāvan. His transcendental form bent in three places, playing wonderfully on His flute, He is the reservoir of all relishable mellows and is the abode of all virtuous qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varṇa nava jaladhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śire śikhi picha vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;alakā tilaka śobhā pāya</strong><br>
<strong>paridhāne pīta-vāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vadane madhūra hāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heno rūpa jagat mātāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By such a beautiful form He is maddening the entire universe. His complexion is like that of a fresh new rain cloud, His head is decorated with a big peacock feather, and the sandalwood tilak on His forehead is most becoming. Wearing brilliant yellow-coloured garments, He stands with His face decorated by a wide, sweet smile.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>indranīla jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-rūpakhāni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heriyā kadamba-mule</strong><br>
<strong>mon uchāṭana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na chole charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra gelāma bhūle</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding Him standing at the base of a kadamba tree, I can see that Krishna’s beauty is conquering the lustre of an entire mine of sapphires. Seeing this, my mind has become so restless that my feet will move no longer, and I’ve completely forgotten about my family and home life in this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>(sakhī he)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sudhāmoya se rūpa-mādhurī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhile nayana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoya achetana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jhore premamoya vāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O sakhī! Oh dear girlfriend! Seeing that sweet form abounding in nectar, I have fallen unconscious as a fountain of tears gushing with ecstatic love cascades from my eyes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kibā chūḍā śire&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kibā vaṁśī kore</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kibā se tribhaṅga-ṭhāma</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇa-kamale,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amiyā uchole,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāte nūpura dāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What a wonderful crown upon His head! What a wonderful flute He is holding in His hand! What a wonderfully beautiful form as He stands in His three-fold bending posture! The nectar of His lotus feet is overflowing with the tinkling sound coming from the clusters of ankle bells which are decorating them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sadā āśā kori&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhṛṅga-rūpa dhori</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa kamale sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>anāyāse pāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro nā bhajibo āna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Accepting the form of a honeybee, I always hope for a residence near His lotus feet. And I will get it very easily because I always sing the glories of Krishna, adoring no-one else.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shiuno-he-rasika-jon-krishna-guna-aganon" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Guṇa-Kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuno, he rasika jon&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa guṇa agaṇon</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ananta kohite nāhi pāre</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa jagatera guru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa vāñchākalpataru</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāviko se bhava-pārābāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Listen to this, oh wisest relishers of mellows! The transcendental qualities of Śrī Krishna are innumerable; indeed it is not possible to describe such unlimited divine attributes. Krishna is the spiritual master of the entire universe, He is like a wish-fulfilling desire-tree, and He is the helmsman of the boat on the ocean of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hṛdoya pīḍita jā’ra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa chikitsaka tā’ra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava roga nāśite chatura</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa bahirmukha jone&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premāmṛta vitaraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krame loya nija antaḥpura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is just like a doctor for those whose hearts are ailing, as He is very expert at destroying the diseased condition of material existence. By distributing the nectar of ecstatic love to all the inimical souls, He gradually takes them back into His own confidential realm.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karma-bandha jñāna-bandha, āveśe mānava andha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre kṛṣṇa karuṇā sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>pādapadma madhu diyā, andha-bhāva ghuchāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe korena anuchara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is an ocean of compassion for those who are bound up in fruitive reactions, as well as for those bound up in philosophical speculation, and for those blinded by other human perplexities. Giving the honey of His lotus feet, and thus relieving the darkness of such worldliness, He makes one an attendant of His own feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidhi-mārga rata jone&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svādhīnatā ratna dāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāgamārge karāna praveśa</strong><br>
<strong>rāga-vaśavarti ho’ye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pārakīya-bhāvāśroye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobhe jīva kṛṣṇa-premāveśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna eventually bestows the jewel of independence unto those persons who are attached to the path of rules and regulations, thereby allowing them entrance into the path of spontaneous loving service. Becoming influenced by such spontaneity, remaining under the shelter of the mellows of unwedded love (<i>pārakīya-bhāva</i>), the soul finally attains all the symptoms of ecstatic love for Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>premāmṛta-vāri-dhārā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā pānarata tā̐’rā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa tā̐hādera bandhu pati</strong><br>
<strong>sei saba vraja-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukalyāṇa-niketana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīnahīna vinodera gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the dearmost friend and husband of those who are always attached to drinking the fountain of ecstatic tears of love. Thus, the goal of this most fallen and lowly Bhakti Vinod is to be with all such residents of Vraja within the abode of supreme auspiciousness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-yashoda-nandana-krishna-gopala-govinda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Jaya Yaśodā-Nandana Kṛṣṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya yaśodā-nandana kṛṣṇa gopāla govinda</strong><br>
<strong>jaya madana-mohana hari ananta mukunda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Krishna, the son of Mother Yaśodā, the cowherd boy and giver of pleasure to the cows! All glories to the conquerer of Cupid, Lord Hari, who takes away all inauspiciousness, who is unlimited, and the awarder of liberation!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya achyuta mādhava rām vṛndāvana-chandra</strong><br>
<strong>jaya muralī-vadana śyāma gopī-janānanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the infallible Lord, husband of the goddess of fortune, the supreme enjoyer, and the moon of Vṛndāvan! All glories to Krishna, who always holds a flute to His mouth, who is the colour of a dark blue raincloud, and is the bliss of the gopīs!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shrita-kamala-kuchha-mandala-dhrita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrita Kamalā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Jayadeva Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrita-kamalā-kuchha-maṇḍala dhṛta-kuṇḍala he</strong><br>
<strong>kalita-lalita-vana-māla jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I Glories, glories to Lord Hari, the Supreme Lord of Godhead, who is bedecked with jeweled earrings and a garland of forest flowers and whose feet are marked with a lotus!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dina-maṇi-maṇḍala-maṇḍana bhava-khaṇḍana he</strong><br>
<strong>muni-jana-mānasa-haṁsa jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I The Lord’s face shines like the whorl of the sun. He removes the miseries of His devotees and is the resting place of the minds of the swan-like sages. Glories! Glories to Lord Śrī Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāliya-viṣa-dhara-gañjana jana-rañjana he</strong><br>
<strong>yadukula-nalina-dineśa jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I O Supreme Personality of Godhead who destroyed the demoniac Kāliya serpent! O Lord, You are the beloved of all living entities and the sun in the galaxy of the Yadu dynasty. Glories! Glories to Lord Śrī Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhu-mura-naraka-vināśana garuḍāsana he</strong><br>
<strong>sura-kula-keli-nidāna jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I O Lord, destroyer of the demons Madhu, Mura and Naraka! Seated on Garuḍa, You are the source of joy for the demigods. All glories to Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>amala-kamala-dala-lochana bhava-mochana he</strong><br>
<strong>tribhuvana-bhuvana-nidhāna jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I O Lord Your eyes are like lotus petals, and You destroy the bondage of the material world. You are the maintainer of the three worlds. Glories to Lord Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janaka-sutā-kṛta-bhūṣaṇa jita-dūṣaṇa he</strong><br>
<strong>samara-śamita-daśa-kaṇṭha jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, as the gem of the sons of Janaka, You were victorious over all the <i>asuras</i>, and You smashed the greatest asura, the ten-headed Ravana. Glories to Lord Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>abhinava-jala-dhara-sundara dhṛta-mandara he</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-mukha-chandra-chakora jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Supreme Personality of Godhead who held the Govardhan Hill! Your complexion is like a fresh monsoon cloud, and Śrī Rādhārāṇī is like a <i>chakora</i> bird who is nourished by drinking the light of Your moonlike face. Glories! Glories to Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava charaṇaṁ praṇatā vayam iti bhāvaya he</strong><br>
<strong>kuru kuśalaṁ praṇateṣu jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, I offer my humble obeisances at Your lotus feet. Please bless me by Your limitless mercy. Glories! Glories to Lord Śrī Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-jayadeva-kaver idaṁ kurute mudam he</strong><br>
<strong>maṅgalam ujjvala-gītaṁ jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The poet Śrī Jayadeva offers this song of devotion and shining good fortune to Thee. All glories! All glories to Lord Śrī Hari!</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Songs on Special Days</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-shrila-maharaja-charanaravinda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āchārya-charaṇa-vandana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīpād Bhakti Ānanda Sāgar Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya śrīla mahārāja charaṇāravinda</strong><br>
<strong>śrī bhakti sundara deva-gosvāmī govinda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Glory to the lotus feet of our Śrī Gurudeva,<br />Śrī Bhakti Sundara Deva-Goswāmī Govinda</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagatera sumaṅgala koribāra āśe</strong><br>
<strong>tumi avatīrṇa hoilā snigdha guru-veśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wishing Grace Divine upon the whole of the creation,<br />From Goloka came thee down, most Gracious Incarnation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>praphulla nalina jena hāsimākhā mukha</strong><br>
<strong>dekhi vṛddha-bāla-juvā pāya mahā-sukha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hundred-petalled lotus flower—Lotus Face ablooming,<br />Young and old behold thee, all their hearts’ joy overflowing.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>svadeśe-videśe harināmera prachāre</strong><br>
<strong>abhinava gaura-preme bhāsāle sabāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Both home and abroad Lord’s Hallowed Name thou art apreaching,<br />Flood of Love for Gaura entire planet inundating.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kore lekhanī jena ananta phoyārā</strong><br>
<strong>sumadhura divyavāṇī-vigalita-dhārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Benedictine fountain from thy pen in hand awriting,<br />Sweetest songs of Love Divine a flow is never ending.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>su-siddhānta-dhārādhara satya sugambhīra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-sevā-niṣṭha mahādhīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Agent of the Waves of Perfect Truth—fathomless ocean,<br />Serve Śrī Guru and Vaiṣṇava! thy heart’s dedication.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sādhu-priya saralatā tomāra bhūṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>amānī mānada sadā miṣṭha-sambhāṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Truthful heart adored by saints—thy natural compassion,<br />Always humble, giving honour, in sweet conversation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guru-mahārāja jatirāja-rājeśvara</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Guru Mahārāj of Nyāsī Kings the Great Grandfather,<br />Śrī-Bhakti-Rakṣaka Deva-Goswāmī Śrīdhara</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>parama sontuṣṭa tava viśrambha-sevāya</strong><br>
<strong>nija-hāte vyāsāsane vasā’la tomāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Satisfied supremely with thy Service dedicated,<br />By his own hand did he place thee on his chair exalted.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sukhe manonīta koilā uttarādhikārī</strong><br>
<strong>ehena apūrva sneha kothāo nā heri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Joyfully as his successor he hath thee achosen,<br />Such benign affection ne’er we find in all creation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-sarasvatī-śrīdhar-dhārā-srotasvinī</strong><br>
<strong>tava hṛde vahe sadā bhuvana pāvanī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Rūpa-Saraswatī-Śrīdhar River flowing freely,<br />From thy heart throughout the world makes everything so holy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śīva-guru-saṅkarṣaṇa karuṇā-nilaya</strong><br>
<strong>nityānanda-rūpe tava hṛde vilasaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Supreme God of Good our Guide, compassion’s Divine Treasure,<br />Ever plays within thy heart as Nityānanda’s Pleasure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sujana sudhīra-gaṇa tava guṇa-gāya</strong><br>
<strong>durbhāgā vañchita aparādhe more hāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Faithfully the true devotees sing thy Supreme Glories,<br />Lost, deluded souls dishonour thee, alas! and perish.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guru-gaura-kṛṣṇa-sevā-saubhāgya-prakāśe</strong><br>
<strong>sānande adhama bhāse saṅkīrtana rāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By thy Grace shall we now serve our Lord and Golden Master,<br />This fallen soul exults in thy Sweet Chanting Dance forever</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-krishna-hoite-chatur-mukha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-paramparā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Verses 1-9 composed by Śrīla Saraswati Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa hoite chatur-mukha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hon kṛṣṇa-sevonmukha,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;brahmā hoite nāradera mati</strong><br>
<strong>nārada hoite vyāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhva kohe vyāsa-dāsa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrṇaprajña padmanābha gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the beginning of creation the science of devotional service was received by the four-headed Brahmā from the Supreme Lord Śrī Krishna. Devarṣi Nārada’s understanding of this divine science was obtained from Brahmā. The great sage Krishna Dvaipāyana Vyāsa, who was empowered to compile the Vedic literatures, became a disciple of Devarṣi Nārada. Śrīpād Madhva Āchārya, the founder of the Śuddha-dvaita school of Vedānta philosophy, who visited Vyāsadeva at Badarīkāśram in the thirteenth century to learn from him Vedānta philosophy, calls himself a servant of Krishna Dvaipāyana Vyāsa. Pūrṇaprajña Tīrtha [Madhva] is the guru and sole refuge of Padmanābha Tīrtha.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nṛhari mādhava-baṁśe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;akṣobhya paramahaṁse,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śiṣya boli aṅgīkāra kore</strong><br>
<strong>akśobhyera śiṣya jaya-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tīrtha nāme parichaya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra dāsye jñānasindhu tore</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two other principal disciples of Madhva are Nṛhari Tīrtha and Mādhava Tīrtha. Mādhava Tīrtha accepted the great paramahaṁsa Akṣobhya Tīrtha as a disciple. The principal disciple of Akṣobhya Tīrtha was known as Jayatīrtha. Jayatīrtha’s service was for his disciple Jñānasindhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐hā hoite dayānidhi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra dāsa vidyānidhi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rājendra hoilo tā̐hā hoite</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra kiṅkora jaya-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharma nāme parichoya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paramparā jāno bhālo mate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dayānidhi received the science of devotional service from Jñānasindhu, and the servant of Dayānidhi was Vidyānidhi [Vidyādhirāja Tīrtha]. Rājendra Tīrtha became a disciple of Vidyādhirāja Tīrtha. Rājendra Tīrtha’s servant was known as Jayadharma or Vijayadhvaja Tīrtha. In this way you should properly understand this disciplic succession.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayadharma-dāsye khyāti,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī puruṣottama-yati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐’ha’te brahmaṇya-tīrtha sūri</strong><br>
<strong>vyāsatīrtha tā̐ra dāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lakṣmīpati vyāsa-dāsa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ha hoite mādhavendra purī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great sannyāsī Śrī Puruṣottama Tīrtha received his knowledge in the service of his guru, Vijayadhvaja Tīrtha [Jayadharma]. The principal disciple of Puruṣottama Tīrtha was Subrahmaṇya Tīrtha. His servant was the great Vyāsatīrtha [Vyāsa Rāya]. Vyāsatīrtha’s servant was Lakṣmīpati Tīrtha, whose disciple was Mādhavendra Purī Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhavendra purī-vara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śiṣya-vara śrī-īśvara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nityānanda, śrī-advaita vibhu</strong><br>
<strong>īśvara-purīke dhonya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;korilena śrī-chaitanya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagad-guru gaura mahāprabhu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The chief disciple of Mādhavendra Purī was Īśvara Purī, and two of his other disciples were the renowned incarnations of Godhead Śrī Nityānanda and Śrī Advaita Āchārya. Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, the spiritual preceptor of all the worlds, made Īśvara Purī greatly fortunate by accepting him as His spiritual master.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprabhu śrī-chaitanya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa nahe anya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūpānuga jonera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>viśvambhara priyaṅkara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī svarūpa-dāmodara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;srī gosvāmī rūpa-sanātana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mahāprabhu Śrī Chaitanya is nondifferent from Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Krishna and is the very life of those Vaiṣṇavas who follow Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī. Śrī Swarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī, Rūpa Goswāmī and Sanātan Goswāmī were the givers of great happiness to Viśvambhara [Śrī Chaitanya].</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpapriya mahājana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīva-raghunātha hon,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra priya kavi kṛṣṇadāsa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇadāsa-priyavara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama sevāpara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jā̐ra pada viśvanātha-āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great souls Jīva Goswāmī and Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī became very dear to Rūpa Goswāmī. Jīva Goswāmī was a disciple of Rūpa Goswāmī. Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, a disciple of Advaita Āchārya’s disciple Jadunandan Āchārya, was accepted by Rūpa and Sanātan as their third brother. Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī’s beloved student was Krishnadās Kavirāj Goswāmī. Kṛṣṇādās Kavirāj was an intimate friend of Lokanāth Goswāmī. They lived together in Vṛndāvan and always discussed the topics of Krishna with one another. Lokanāth Goswāmī, a disciple of Gadādhara Paṇḍit, had only one disciple, whose name was Narottam Dās. Narottam Dās was always engaged in the service of his guru, and he also engaged himself in the service of his guru’s intimate friends. Thus he became very dear to Krishnadās Kavirāj Goswāmī. To serve the feet of Narottam Dās Ṭhākur was the only desire of Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur, who was the fourth <i>āchārya</i> in disciplic succession from Narottam Dās.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśvanātha-bhakta-sāth,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baladeva jagannāth,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra priya śrī-bhaktivinoda</strong><br>
<strong>mahā-bhāgavata-vara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaurakiśora-vara,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-bhajanete jā̐ra moda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Viśvanātha Chakravartī Ṭhākura was the śikṣā-guru [instructing spiritual master] of Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa, to whom he taught the precepts of Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam. Jagannāth Dās Bābājī was a very prominent Āchārya after Śrī Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa and was the beloved śikṣā-guru of Śrī Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur. Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s intimate friend and associate was the eminent mahābhāgavat Śrī Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī, whose sole joy was found in hari-bhajana.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tadanuga-mahājon&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-kīrtana-dhon</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jebā dilo puri jaga kāma</strong><br>
<strong>śrī vārṣabhānavībarā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā sevya sevāparā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hāra dayita-dāsa nāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The followers of Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī fill up the world with the wealth of Śrī Krishna-kīrtan. He who is always serving Krishna the dearmost lover of Rādhārāṇī, is Śrī Vārṣabhānavī Dayita Dās (Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tadabhīnna deho-divya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svarūpa-rūpa-raghu-jivya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā sevya jā̐ra pādapadma</strong><br>
<strong>susiddhānta mūrti-dhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī śrīdhara guruvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rupānuga-sādhu śreya sadma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The nondifferent personality and embodiment of bhakti-siddhānta as well as Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur; and who is living within the conception of Śrīla Swarūp Dāmodar, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī and Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī; and whose lotus feet are always the worshipable happy shelter of the followers of Rūpa Goswāmī, he is Śrīla Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐ra priya mano’ bhīṣṭa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāpane sadāsacheṣṭa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti sundara śrī govinda nāma</strong><br>
<strong>ei paramparā dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabe gaura-nijajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐dera uchiṣṭe mora kāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He who is a very dear disciple of that Śrīla Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, and who is establishing his divine conception, and who is fulfilling his desire continuously, he is Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī. This is our grand preceptorial line and all of them are very dear to Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. My sustenance is their remnants alone.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhayabhanjana-jayashiamsana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
