<h2>Śrī Dayita Dās Praṇati Pañchakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Homage unto Śrīla Saraswati Ṭhākur</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhayabhañjana jayaśaṁsana-karuṇāyata-nayanam</strong><br>
<strong>kanakotpala janakojjvala-rasasāgara-chayanam</strong><br>
<strong>mukharīkṛta-dharaṇītala-harikīrtana-rasanam</strong><br>
<strong>kṣitipāvana-bhavatāraṇa-pihitāruṇa-vasanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He (of divine form) came forth from the birthplace of the golden lotus - the ocean of the mellow of divine consorthood. His large, merciful eyes dispel (the suffering souls’) fear and proclaim (the surrendered souls’) victory. His tongue (constantly) vibrates the whole Earth planet with Śrī Krishna-<i>saṅkīrtan</i>, his beauty resplendent in the robes of the sun’s radiance (saffron) that purifies the universe and dispels the suffering of material existence. On his Holy Day of Advent, I (again and again) bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śaraṇāgata-bhajanavrata-chirapālana-charaṇam</strong><br>
<strong>sukṛtālaya-saralāśaya-sujanākhila-varaṇam</strong><br>
<strong>harisādhana-kṛtabādhana janaśāsana-kalanam</strong><br>
<strong>sacharāchara-karuṇākara-nikhilāśiva-dalanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The devotees surrendered in pure devotion are eternally protected at his lotus feet. He is worshippable by the pure souls endowed with sincerity and good fortune, and he accepts (even) those who obstruct the service of Śrī Hari, just to rectify them. As the very fountainhead of mercy upon all mobile and immobile beings, he crushes the inauspiciousness of the whole universe. On his Holy Day of Advent, I (again and again) bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atilaukika-gatitaulika-ratikautuka-vapuṣam</strong><br>
<strong>atidaivata-mativaiṣṇava-yati-vaibhava-puruṣam</strong><br>
<strong>sasanātana-raghurūpaka-paramāṇugacharitam</strong><br>
<strong>suvichāraka iva jīvaka iti sādhubhiruditam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like a miracle, his body moves with a joyful elegance and charm beyond the world’s understanding, fulfilling the artist’s aspiration. (Or, dancing in Pastimes transcendental to the world, his artistic form incites divine love’s hankering). His intellect surpasses that of (even) the demigods, and he is nobility incarnate as the commander in-chief of the Vaiṣṇava <i>sannyāsīs</i> (<i>tridaṇḍi-yatis</i>). The <i>sādhus</i> of profound intellect describe the nature of his personality as meticulously in the line of Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Rūpa, and Śrī Raghunāth, and they speak of him as being on the same plane as Śrī Jīvapāda (being superbly replete in perfect theistic conclusions). On his Holy Day of Advent, I (again and again) bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarasītaṭa-sukhadoṭaja-nikaṭapriyabhajanam</strong><br>
<strong>lalitāmukha-lalanākula-paramādarayajanam</strong><br>
<strong>vrajakānana-bahumānana-kamalapriyanayanam</strong><br>
<strong>guṇamañjari-garimā-guṇa-harivāsanavayanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On the bank of Śrī Rādhā Kuṇḍa at Svānanda Sukhada Kuñja, he is devoted to the service of his Beloved, and (furthermore) he is greatly endeared to the divine damsels of Vraja headed by Lalitā. He is most favourite to Kamala-mañjarī who is pre-eminent in Vṛndāvan, and with the glorious qualities of Guṇamañjarī he builds the residence of Śrī Hari. On his Holy Day of Advent, I (again and again) bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vimalotsavam amalotkala-puruṣottama-jananam</strong><br>
<strong>patitoddhṛti-karuṇāstṛti-kṛtanūtana-pulinam</strong><br>
<strong>mathurāpura-puruṣottama-samagaurapuraṭanam</strong><br>
<strong>harikāmaka-haridhāmaka-harināmaka-raṭanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is immaculate joy incarnate, or, he is the graciousness or the joy of Vimalā Devī. He manifested the Pastimes of his Advent at Puruṣottama Kṣetra in the holy land of Orissa, and he revealed his Pastimes of delivering the fallen souls and extending his mercy upon them (by awarding them the gift of divine love) at the ‘new isles,’ or Nabadwīp. Circumambulating Gaura Dhām in the same way as traditionally done at Vraja Dhām and Puruṣottama Dhām, he continuously propagates - the loving desire of Vraja, the divine abode of Vaikuṇṭha, and the holy name of Krishna. On his Day of Holy Advent, I (again and again) make my obeisance unto that dear associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his holy lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayare-jayare-jaya-paramahamsa-mahashiaya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āchārya vandana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Gopāl Govinda Mahanta</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayare jayare jaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paramahaṁsa mahāśaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī bhaktisiddhānta sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>gosvāmī ṭhākura jaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama karuṇāmoya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīnahīna agatira gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the topmost personality, Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Goswāmī Ṭhākur, who is an ocean of mercy and the only resort for the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nilāchole hoiyā udaya</strong><br>
<strong>śrī gauḍamaṇḍale āsi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema bhakti parakāśi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jivera nāśilā bhava-bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He made his advent in Śrī Nilāchala, Śrī Puri Dhām. He came to the holy <i>dhām</i> of Mahāprabhu, manifesting the line of pure devotion and destroying the fear of material life of the conditioned souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mahimā gāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heno sādhya mora nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tobe pāri jodi deho śakti</strong><br>
<strong>viśvahite avirata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchāra-prachāre rata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśuddha śrī rūpānuga bhakti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have no capacity to sing your glories. Only if you empower me can I do so. You are travelling unceasingly all over India, practising and preaching in a dedicated way, the pure and genuine line of devotion in the current of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīpāṭ khetari dhāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṭhākura śrī narottama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāte tā̐hāra guṇa dekhi</strong><br>
<strong>śāstrera siddhānta-sāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuni lāge chamatkāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kutārkika dite nāre phā̐ki</strong></p>
<p class="translation">We see your qualities are like Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur who in the festival at Khetari Dhām delivered all the wonderful conclusions of the Scriptures which defeated the unfair argument and deception of the scholars there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha bhakti-mata jata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upadharma-kavalita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heriyā lokera mone trāsa</strong><br>
<strong>hāni’ susiddhānta-vāṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upadharma khāna khāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sajjanera vāḍāle ullāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By presenting the line of pure devotion he devoured the path of unscriptural practice, and seeing this everybody was afraid. He struck this unscriptural path leaving it in pieces, giving increasing joy to all honest and noble men.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>smārtamata jaladhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuddha bhakti robi-kara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchādila bhāviyā antare</strong><br>
<strong>śāstra sindhu manthanete&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;susiddhānta jhañjhāvāte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uḍāilā dig digantare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Pure devotion is like the sun risen in the heart, while the path of logic is like clouds covering that sun. By churning the ocean of the Scriptures, you spread perfect conclusions in all directions like a hurricane to disperse the clouds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthāne sthāne koto moṭh&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāpiyācha niṣkapoṭ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema sevā śikhāite jīve</strong><br>
<strong>moṭhera vaiṣṇava goṇ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kore sadā vitaroṇ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari guṇa-kathāmṛta bhave</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You founded many temples in many places without reservation, teaching the fallen souls service in divine love. In these temples the Vaiṣṇavas constantly distribute talks on the nectarean qualities of the Supreme Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha-bhakti-mandākinī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vimala pravāha āni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śitala korilā taptaprāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>deśe deśe niṣkiñchan&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prerilā vaiṣṇava goṇ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vistārite hariguṇa gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The current of pure devotion descends like the flow of the Ganges, cooling the fire of suffering of material life. Similarly, you sent the Vaiṣṇavas from place to place, widely distributing and chanting the qualities of Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūrve yathā gaurahari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māyāvāda cheda kori</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇava korilā kāśivāsī</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava darśana-sukṣma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vichāre tumi he dakṣa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;temati toṣilā vārāṇasī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just as in the past, Lord Gaurahari severed the <i>māyāvāda</i> conception,  converting  the  residents  of Vārāṇasī into Vaiṣṇavas, similarly by expert analysis of the Vaiṣṇava philosophy you have enraptured all in Vārāṇasī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>daiva-varṇāśrama-dharma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari bhakti jāra marma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāstra jukte korilā-niśchoy</strong><br>
<strong>jñana-joga-karma choy&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mūlya tāra kichu noy</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhaktira virodhī jodi hoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You showed that certainly devotion to Lord Hari at the core of daiva-varṇāśrama-dharma, is the directive of the revealed Scriptures, while knowledge, yoga and material activities, if averse to such devotion are of absolutely no value.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī gauḍamaṇḍala bhūmi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta saṅge parikrami</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukīrti sthāpilā mahāśoy</strong><br>
<strong>abhinna vraja maṇḍala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gauḍabhūmi premojvala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prachāra hoilo viśvamoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You founded parikramā of Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍal, Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām in the association of the devotees, showing your glories as a great devotee. You showed the whole world the nectarean blissful nature of Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍal and how it is non-different from Śrī Vraja Maṇḍal, Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuliyāte pāṣaṇḍīrā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;atyāchāra koilo jā’rā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā sabāra doṣa kṣomā kori</strong><br>
<strong>jagate koile ghoṣaṇā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘taroriva sahiṣṇunā’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hon ‘kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You forgave all the atheists of Kuliyā village, despite their blasphemous behaviour. Loudly you proclaimed to one and all, one who is more tolerant than a tree can chant the glories of Lord Hari endlessly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī viśva-vaiṣṇava-rāja&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabhā-madhye ‘pātrarāja’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upādhi-bhūṣaṇe vibhūṣita</strong><br>
<strong>viśvera maṅgala lāgi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoiyācho sarva’ tyāgī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvavāsī jon-hite rata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the grand assembly of Vaiṣṇava kings you are adorned with the title ‘Pātrarāj’, as the head of the assembly. For the benefit of the whole world, you have renounced everything and devoted yourself to the good of all.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koritecho upakāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāte para upakāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobhe jīva śrī kṛṣṇa-sevāya</strong><br>
<strong>dūre jāya bhava-roga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khaṇḍe jāhe karma bhoga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari pāda padma jā’te pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You untiringly perform benevolent works just to help the souls of this world to attain the service of Lord Śrī Krishna. Then the disease of material suffering would leave them, their bondage to fruitive works would break and they would attain the lotus feet of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīva moha-nidrā gata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāgā’te vaikuṇṭha dūta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘gauḍīya’ pāṭhāo ghore ghore</strong><br>
<strong>uṭhore uṭhore bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro to samoya nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘kṛṣṇa bhaja’ bole uchaiḥsvare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing the souls of this world sleeping in illusion, you are sending the ‘Gauḍīya’ magazine from door to door just like a messenger from the transcendental plane. It calls aloud, “Awake, arise, O brother, there is no more time to waste, Serve and worship your Lord, Śrī Krishna.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mukhāravinda-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vigalita makaranda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;siñchita achyuta-guṇa-gāthā</strong><br>
<strong>śunile juḍāya prāṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tamo moha antardhāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dūre jāya hṛdoyera vyathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hearing the nectarean verses of the glorious qualities of the infallible Lord Krishna, issuing from your lotus mouth soothes our souls, dissipates our ignorance and illusion, and the pain in our hearts goes far away.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāni āmi mahāśoy&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaśovāñchā nāhi hoy</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bindu mātra tomāra antare</strong><br>
<strong>tava guṇa vīṇādhārī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora kaṇṭha-vīṇā dhori’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśete bolāya āmāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O great soul! I know that there is no trace of desire for fame in your heart. Your qualities, like an expert musician, take hold of the <i>vīṇā</i> of my voice and make me speak, devoid of any power over myself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera guṇa-gāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;korile jīvera trāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuniyāchi sādhu guru mukhe</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa bhakti-samudoy&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama saphala hoy</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e bhava-sāgara tore sukhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have heard from the great saints and gurus mouths that upon hearing the qualities of the Vaiṣṇava saints, the conditioned souls attain liberation. And beyond this, devotion to Krishna develops, makes the soul’s birth perfect and the soul crosses this material ocean happily with ease.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>te-kāraṇe prayāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yathā vāmanera āśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaganera chā̐da dhori bāre</strong><br>
<strong>adoṣa-daraśī tumi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama patita āmi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija guṇe kṣomivā āmāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">For this reason I endeavour thus, just like a dwarf aspiring to reach the moon. I am lowly and fallen, but your nature is not to consider any offence, so I beg you to pardon my flaws.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="20">
<h5>20</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī gaurāṅga-pāriṣada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṭhākura bhaktivinoda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīnahīna patitera bandhu</strong><br>
<strong>kali-tamaḥ vināśite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānilena avanīte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;toma’ akalaṅka pūrṇa indu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur, the associate of Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu and friend of the lowly fallen souls brought you here, and you came like a spotless full moon to dispel the ignorance of this age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="21">
<h5>21</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kara kṛpā vitaraṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premasudhā anukṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mātiyā uṭhuka jīva goṇ</strong><br>
<strong>harināma-saṅkīrtane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāchuka jagata-jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇava-dāsera nivedan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">So please keep incessantly showering your mercy, the nectar of ecstatic love of Godhead, and let all the souls of this world be uplifted by that. May all the people of the world dance in the congregational chanting of the Lord’s Holy Name—this is the prayer of this servant of the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-pralaya-payodhi-jale-dhritavan-asi-vedam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Daśāvatār-stotram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Jayadeva Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pralaya-payodhi-jale dhṛtavān asi vedaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vihita-vahitra-charitram akhedam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-mīna-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a fish! All glories to You! You easily acted as a boat in the form of a giant fish just to give protection to the Vedas, which had become immersed in the turbulent sea of devastation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṣitir iha vipulatare tiṣṭhati tava pṛṣṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharaṇi-dharaṇa-kina-chakra-gariṣṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-kūrma-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a tortoise! All glories to You! In this incarnation as a divine tortoise the great Mandara Mountain rests upon Your gigantic back as a pivot for churning the ocean of milk. From holding up the huge mountain a large scarlike depression is put in Your back, which has become most glorious.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vasati daśana-śikhare dharaṇī tava lagnā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śaśini kalaṅka-kaleva nimagnā</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-śūkara-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a boar! All glories to You! The earth, which had become immersed in the Garbhodaka Ocean at the bottom of the universe, sits fixed upon the tip of Your tusk like a spot upon the moon.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava kara-kamala-vare nakham adbhuta-śṛṅgaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dalita-hiraṇyakaśipu-tanu-bhṛṅgam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-narahari-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of half-man, half-lion! All glories to You! Just as one can easily crush a wasp between one’s fingernails, so in the same way the body of the wasplike demon Hiraṇyakaśipu has been ripped apart by the wonderful pointed nails on Your beautiful lotus hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chalayasi vikramaṇe balim adbhuta-vāmana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pada-nakha-nīra-janita-jana-pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-vāmana-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a dwarf-<i>brāhmaṇ</i>! All glories to You! Oh wonderful dwarf, by Your massive steps You deceive King Bali, and by the Ganges water that has emanated from the nails of Your lotus feet, You deliver all living beings within this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṣatriya-rudhira-maye jagad-apagata-pāpaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;snapayasi payasi śamita-bhava-tāpam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-bhṛgupati-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of Bhṛgupati [Paraśurāma]! All glories to You! At Kurukṣetra You bathe the earth in the rivers of blood from the bodies of the demoniac kṣatriyas that You have slain. The sins of the world are washed away by You, and because of You people are relieved from the blazing fire of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vitarasi dikṣu raṇe dik-pati-kamanīyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;daśa-mukha-mauli-baliṁ ramaṇīyam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-rāma-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of Rāmachandra! All glories to You! In the battle of Laṅkā You destroy the ten-headed demon Rāvaṇa and distribute his heads as a delightful offering to the presiding deities of the ten directions, headed by Indra. This action was long desired by all of them, who were much harassed by this monster.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vahasi vapuṣi viśade vasanaṁ jaladābhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hala-hati-bhīti-milita yamunābham</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-haladhara-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of Balarām, the wielder of the plow! All glories to You! On Your brilliant white body You wear garments the colour of a fresh blue rain cloud. These garments are coloured like the beautiful dark hue of the River Yamunā, who feels great fear due to the striking of Your plowshare.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nindasi yajña-vidher ahaha śruti-jātaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadaya-hṛdoya darśita-paśu-ghātam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-buddha-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of Buddha! All glories to You! Oh Buddha of compassionate heart, you decry the slaughtering of poor animals performed according to the rules of Vedic sacrifice.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mlecha nivaha-nidhane kalayasi karavālaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhūmaketum iva kim api karālam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-kalki-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of Kalki! All glories to You! You appear like a comet and carry a terrifying sword for bringing about the annihilation of the wicked barbarian men at the end of the <i>Kali-yuga</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-jayadeva-kaver idam uditam udāraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śṛṇu sukha-daṁ śubha-daṁ bhava-sāram</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-daśa-vidha-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed these ten different forms of incarnation! All glories to You! Oh readers, please hear this hymn of the poet Jayadeva, which is most excellent, an awarder of happiness, a bestower of auspiciousness, and is the best thing in this dark world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vedān udharate jaganti vahate bhū-golam udbibhrate</strong><br>
<strong>daityaṁ dārayate baliṁ chalayate</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṣatra-kṣayaṁ kurvate</strong><br>
<strong>paulastyaṁ jayate halaṁ kalayate kāruṇyam ātanvate</strong><br>
<strong>mlechān mūrchayate daśākṛti-kṛte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇāya tubhyaṁ namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord Krishna, I offer my obeisances unto You, who appear in the forms of these ten incarnations. In the form of Matsya You rescue the Vedas, and as Kūrma You bear the Mandara Mountain on Your back. As Varāha You lift the earth with Your tusk, and in the form of Narasiṁha You tear open the chest of the demon Hiraṇyakaśipu. In the form of Vāmana You trick the demoniac king Bali by asking him for only three steps of land, and then You take away the whole universe from him by expanding Your steps. As Paraśurāma You slay all of the wicked kṣatriyas, and as Rāmachandra You conquer the rākṣasa king Rāvaṇa. In the form of Balarām You carry a plough with which You subdue the wicked and draw toward You the River Yamunā. As Lord Buddha You show compassion toward all the living beings suffering in this world, and at the end of the <i>Kali-yuga</i> You appear as Kalki to bewilder the mlechas [degraded low-class men].</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namas-te-narasimhaya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Nṛsiṁha Praṇām <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>namas te narasiṁhāya</strong><br>
<strong>prahlādāhlāda-dāyine</strong><br>
<strong>hiraṇyakaśipor vakṣaḥ-</strong><br>
<strong>śilā-ṭaṅka-nakhālaye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisances to Lord Narasiṁha, who gives joy to Prahlād Mahārāj and whose nails are like chisels on the stonelike chest of the demon Hiraṇyakaśipu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>ito nṛsiṁhaḥ parato nṛsiṁho</strong><br>
<strong>yato yato yāmi tato nṛsiṁhaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>bahir nṛsiṁho hṛdaye nṛsiṁho</strong><br>
<strong>nṛsiṁham ādiṁ śaraṇaṁ prapadye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nṛsiṁha is here and also there. Wherever I go Lord Nṛsiṁha is there. He is in the heart and is outside as well. I surrender to Lord Nṛsiṁha, the origin of all things and the supreme refuge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse-subtitle">Śrī Daśāvatār-stotram</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava kara-kamala-vare nakham adbhuta-śṛṅgaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dalita-hiraṇyakaśipu-tanu-bhṛṅgam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-narahari-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Keśava! Oh Lord of the universe! Oh Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of half-man, half-lion! All glories to You! Just as one can easily crush a wasp between one’s fingernails, so in the same way the body of the wasplike demon Hiraṇyakaśipu has been ripped apart by the wonderful pointed nails on Your beautiful lotus hands.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namamishvaram-sachchidananda-rupam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Dāmodarāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrī Satyavrat Muni</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namāmīśvaraṁ sachchidānanda rūpaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>lasat-kuṇḍalaṁ gokule bhrājamānam</strong><br>
<strong>yaśodā-bhiyolūkhalād dhāvamānaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>parāmṛṣṭam atyaṁ tato drutya gopyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To the Supreme Lord, whose form is the embodiment of eternal existence, knowledge, and bliss, whose shark-shaped earrings are swinging to and fro, who is beautifully shining in the divine realm of Gokul, who [due to the offence of breaking the pot of yoghurt that his mother was churning into butter and then stealing the butter that was kept hanging from a swing] is quickly running from the wooden grinding mortar in fear of Mother Yaśodā, but who has been caught from behind by her who ran after Him with greater speed—to that Supreme Lord, Śrī Dāmodar, I offer my humble obeisances.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rudantaṁ muhur netra-yugmaṁ mṛjantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>karāmbhoja-yugmena sātaṅka-netram</strong><br>
<strong>muhuḥ śvāsa-kampa-trirekhāṅka-kaṇṭha-</strong><br>
<strong>sthita-graiva dāmodaraṁ bhakti-baddham</strong></p>
<p class="translation">[Seeing the whipping stick in His mother’s hand,] He is crying and rubbing His eyes again and again with His two lotus hands. His eyes are filled with fear, and the necklace of pearls around His neck, which is marked with three lines like a conchshell, is shaking because of His quick breathing due to crying. To this Supreme Lord, Śrī Dāmodar, whose belly is bound not with ropes but with His mother’s pure love, I offer my humble obeisances.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>itīdṛk sva-līlābhir ānanda-kuṇḍe</strong><br>
<strong>sva-ghoṣaṁ nimajjantam ākhyāpayantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tadīyeṣita-jñeṣu bhaktair jitatvaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>punaḥ prematas taṁ śatāvṛtti vande</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By such childhood Pastimes as this He is drowning the inhabitants of Gokul in pools of ecstasy, and is revealing to those devotees who are absorbed in knowledge of His supreme majesty and opulence that He is only conquered by devotees whose pure love is imbued with intimacy and is free from all conceptions of awe and reverence. With great love I again offer my obeisances to Lord Dāmodar hundreds and hundreds of times.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varaṁ deva mokṣaṁ na mokṣāvadhiṁ vā</strong><br>
<strong>na chānyaṁ vṛṇe ’haṁ vareśād apīha</strong><br>
<strong>idan te vapur nātha gopāla-bālaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sadā me manasy āvirāstāṁ kim anyaiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord, although You are able to give all kinds of benedictions, I do not pray to You for the boon of impersonal liberation, nor the highest liberation of eternal life in Vaikuṇṭha, nor any other boon [which may be obtained by executing the nine processes of bhakti]. Oh Lord, I simply wish that this form of Yours as Bāla Gopāl in Vṛndāvan may ever be manifest in my heart, for what is the use to me of any other boon besides this?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>idan te mukhāmbhojam avyakta-nīlair</strong><br>
<strong>vṛtaṁ kuntalaiḥ snigdha-raktaiś cha gopyā</strong><br>
<strong>muhuś chumbitaṁ bimba-raktādharaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>manasy āvirāstām alaṁ lakṣa-lābhaiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh Lord, Your lotus face, which is encircled by locks of soft black hair tinged with red, is kissed again and again by Mother Yaśoda, and Your lips are reddish like the bimba fruit. May this beautiful vision of Your lotus face be ever manifest in my heart. Thousands and thousands of other benedictions are of no use to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo deva dāmodarānanta viṣṇo</strong><br>
<strong>prasīda prabho duḥkha-jālābdhi-magnaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-dṛṣṭi-vṛṣṭyāti-dīnaṁ batānu-</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhāṇeśa mām ajñam edhy akṣi-dṛśyaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Supreme Godhead, I offer my obeisances unto You. O Dāmodar! O Ananta! O Viṣṇu! O Master! O my Lord, be pleased upon me. By showering Your glance of mercy upon me, deliver this poor ignorant fool who is immersed in an ocean of worldly sorrows, and become visible to my eyes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuverātmajau baddha-mūrtyaiva yadvat</strong><br>
<strong>tvayā mochitau bhakti-bhājau kṛtau cha</strong><br>
<strong>tathā prema-bhaktiṁ svakāṁ me prayacha</strong><br>
<strong>na mokṣe graho me ’sti dāmodareha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Dāmodar, just as the two sons of Kuvera — Maṇigrīva and Nalakīśvara — were delivered from the curse of Nārada and made into great devotees by You in Your form as a baby tied with rope to a wooden grinding mortar, in the same way, please give to me Your own prema-bhakti. I only long for this and have no desire for any kind of liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namas te ’stu dāmne sphurad-dīpti-dhāmne</strong><br>
<strong>tvadīyodarāyātha viśvasya dhāmne</strong><br>
<strong>namo rādhikāyai tvadīya-priyāyai</strong><br>
<strong>namo ’nanta-līlāya devāya tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Dāmodar, I first of all offer my obeisances to the brilliantly effulgent rope which binds Your belly. I then offer my obeisances to Your belly, which is the abode of the entire universe. I humbly bow down to Your most beloved Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, and I offer all obeisances to You, the Supreme Lord, who displays unlimited Pastimes.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nadiya-nagare-nitai-neche-neche-gay-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīman Mahāprabhur śata-nāma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-nagare nitāi neche neche gāy re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(Refrain:) In the towns and villages of Nadia, Lord Nityānanda ecstatically dances and sings these Names of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagannātha-suta mahāprabhu viśvambhara</strong><br>
<strong>māyāpura-śaśi navadvīpa-sudhākara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya is the beloved son of Jagannāth Miśra; He is Mahāprabhu, the great master, protector, and maintainer of the whole world. He is the shining moon of Māyāpur, dissipating the dark ignorance of the world, and the source of all nectar for the Land of Nabadwīp.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śachī-suta gaurahari nimāi-sundara</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-bhāva-kānti-āchādito naṭavara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the beloved son of Śachī Mātā and is Lord Hari with a golden complexion. As He was born under a neem tree He was called Nimāi-<i>sundar</i> (beautiful Nimāi). He is covered by the sentiment and lustre of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, and He is the best of all dancers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmānanda chapala bālaka mātṛ-bhakta</strong><br>
<strong>brahmāṇḍa-badana tarkī kautukānurakta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya becomes ecstatic upon hearing the Holy Name of Hari. As a boy He was swift and agile, devoted to His mother, a great logician, and fond of joking. Millions of worlds are contained in His mouth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidyārthi-uḍupa chaura-dvayera mohana</strong><br>
<strong>tairthika-sarvasva grāmya-bālikā-krīḍana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He bewildered two thieves who tried to steal His jewels when He was a small child, and He teased and joked with the village girls of Nadia. He is the moon among scholars and pre-eminent among all philosophers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lakṣmī-prati boro-dātā udhata bālaka</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-śachīra pati-putra-śoka-nibāraka</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya is the giver of blessings to Lakṣmī Prīya (His first wife). He was a mischievous child and is the Lord, son and preventer of all kinds of grief of Śachī Mātā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lakṣmī-pati pūrva-deśa-sarva-kleśa-hara</strong><br>
<strong>digvijayi-darpa-hārī viṣṇu-priyeśvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the Lord and husband of Lakṣmī Priyā. By His saṅkīrtan movement, He removed the distresses of East Bengal. He removed the pride of the conquering paṇḍit Keśava Kasmiri. He is the Lord of Viṣṇu Priyā (His second wife).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ārya-dharma-pāla pitṛ-gayā piṇḍa-dātā</strong><br>
<strong>purī-śiṣya madhvāchārya-sampradāya-pātā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He was the protector and preserver of sanātana-dharma and the giver of <i>piṇḍa</i> at Gayā after the disappearance of His father, Jagannāth Miśra. He became the disciple of Īśvara Purī and is the protector of the Madhva Āchārya Sampradāya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāmonmatta kṛṣṇa-tattva-adhyāpaka</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-saṅkīrtana-juga-dharma-pravartaka</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya became intoxicated by and mad for the Holy Name of Krishna, and began to profess the science of Krishna. Thus He inaugurated the religion of the age, Harinām Saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>advaita-bāndhava śrīnivāsa-gṛha-dhon</strong><br>
<strong>nityānanda-prāṇa gadādharera jīvan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He was the friend of Advaita Āchārya, the treasure of Śrīvās Ṭhākur’s home, the life and soul of Nityānanda Prabhu, and the very source of life to Gadādhara Paṇḍit.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>antadvīpa-śaśadhara sīmanta-bijoy</strong><br>
<strong>godruma-bihārī madhyadvīpa-līlāśroy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu is the moon of Antardwīp and the triumph of Sīmantadwīp. He is wandering on the island of Godruma and is the shelter of Pastimes in Madhyadwīp.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koladvīpa-pati ṛtudvīpa-maheśvara</strong><br>
<strong>jahnu-modadruma-rudradvīpera īsvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya is the Lord of Koladwīp, Ṛtudwīp, Jahnudwīp, Modadrumadwīp, and Rudradwīp.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>navakhaṇḍa-raṅganātha jāhnavī-jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>jagāi-mādhāi-ādi durbṛtta tāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is thus the Lord of Nabadwīp which serves as the stage for His wonderful Pastimes. He is the life of the River Ganges and He delivers all sorts of rogues and rascals beginning with Jagāi and Mādhāi.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nagara-kīrtana-siṁha kājī-udhāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha-nāma-prachāraka bhaktārti-haraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is the lion of the village kīrtan. He delivered the Chānd Kazi. He is the preacher of the pure Holy Name, and the remover of the distresses of His devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nārāyaṇī-kṛpā-sindhu jīvera niyantā</strong><br>
<strong>adhama-paḍūyā-daṇḍī bhakta-doṣa-hantā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the ocean of mercy for Nārāyaṇī; the controller of all souls; the chastiser of the fallen student who criticised His chanting of “Gopī, gopī!”, and the destroyer of the sins of His devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-chandra bhāratī-tāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>paribrāja-śiromaṇi utkala-pāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He was initiated into sannyās with the Name of Śrī Krishna Chaitanya, and He is beautiful like the moon. He delivered Keśava Bhārati; is the crest-jewel of all wandering sannyāsīs, and the saviour of Orissa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ambu-liṅga-bhuvaneśa-kapoteśa-pati</strong><br>
<strong>kṣīra-chora-gopāla-darśana-sukhī jatī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya is the master of Lord Śiva, who is known as Ambu-liṅga, Bhuvaneśvara, and Kapoteśvara at three different Śaiva tīrthas visited by Śrī Chaitanya on His way to Jagannātha Purī. As a sannyāsī He rejoiced at the sight of Kṣīra-chora Gopīnāth and Sākṣī Gopāl.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nirdaṇḍi-sannyāsī sārvabhauma-kṛpāmoy</strong><br>
<strong>svānanda-āsvādānānandī sarva-sukhāśroy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He became a sannyāsī without a daṇḍa (staff) due to Lord Nityānanda’s breaking it and throwing it in the Bhargi River. He is full of mercy for Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭāchārya. He is ecstatic by tasting the bliss of Himself in the form of Krishna, and He is the resting place of all happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>puraṭa-sundara vāsudeva-trāṇa-kartā</strong><br>
<strong>rāmānanda-sakhā bhaṭṭa-kula-kleśa-hartā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga is beautiful like molten gold and He delivered the leper Vāsudeva. He is the friend of Rāmānanda Rāy, and the remover of all miseries from the family of Vyeṅkaṭa Bhaṭṭa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bauddha-jaina-māyāvādi-kutarka-khaṇḍana</strong><br>
<strong>dakṣiṇa-pāvana bhakti-grantha-udhāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He refuted the atheistic arguments of the Buddhists, Jains, and Māyāvādīs. He is the saviour of South India, and He has brought to light the two devotional literatures, Krishna-karṇāmṛta and Brahma-saṁhitā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="20">
<h5>20</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ālāla-darśanānandī rathāgra-nartaka</strong><br>
<strong>gajapati-trāṇa devānanda-udhāraka</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya became ecstatic at the sight of Ālānātha. He danced in front of the Jagannāth cart during Ratha Yātrā, He delivered Gajapati Prataparudra, and He was the saviour of Devānanda Paṇḍit.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="21">
<h5>21</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuliyā-prakāśe duṣṭa paḍuyāra trāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-sanātana-bandhu sarva-jīva-prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By His appearance at Kuliyā He delivered the ill-behaved student community. He is the friend of Rūpa and Sanātan, and the life of all souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="22">
<h5>22</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāvanānanda-mūrti balabhadra-saṅgī</strong><br>
<strong>javana-udhārī bhaṭṭa-vallabhera raṅgī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaurāṅga is the personification of bliss in the transcendental realm of Vṛndāvan. He is the companion and friend of Balabhadra Bhaṭṭāchārya. On His return from Vṛndāvan He delivered many Mohammedans. He is very fond of Vallabhāchārya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="23">
<h5>23</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāśīvāsi-sannyāsī-udhārī prema-dātā</strong><br>
<strong>markaṭa-vairāgī-daṇḍī ā-chaṇḍāla-trātā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He delivered the Māyāvādī sannyāsīs of Kāśī, and He is the bestower of love of God. To set a strict example for sannyāsīs He chastised the great devotee Choṭo Haridās’s begging rice from Mādhavī Devī. Śrī Chaitanya is the saviour of everyone down to the lowest class of men.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="24">
<h5>24</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktera-gaurava-kārī bhakta-prāṇa-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>haridāsa-raghunāth-svarūpa-jīvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya is the glorifier of His devotees and is the wealth of their lives. He is the life of such devotees as Haridās Ṭhākur, Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, and Swarūp Dāmodar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="25">
<h5>25</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadiyā-nagare nitāi neche neche gāy re</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda tā̐’ra poḍe rāṅgā-pāy re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the towns and villages of Nadia, Lord Nityānanda dances ecstatically, singing these divine Names, and Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod falls down at His reddish lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nagare-nagare-gora-gay" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Kṛṣṇera Viṁsottara-śata-nāma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>nagare nagare gorā gāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">From village to village Lord Gaura sings these names of Śrī Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaśomatī-stanya-pāyī śrī-nanda-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>indra-nīla-maṇi vraja-janera jīvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the baby who feeds on the breast of Mother Yaśodā. He is the son of Mahārāj Nanda, dark blue like an indra-nīla gem, and the life of the residents of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī gokula niśācharī-pūtanā ghātana</strong><br>
<strong>duṣṭa-tṛṇāvarta-hantā śakaṭa-bhañjana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna destroyed the witch of Gokul, Pūtanā; He broke the cart in which the demon Śakaṭāsura was hiding; and He destroyed the wicked demon, Tṛṇāvarta.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>navanīta-chora dadhi-haraṇa-kuśala</strong><br>
<strong>jamala-arjuna-bhañjī govinda gopāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is an expert thief of butter and yoghurt, He broke the two Jamala Arjuna trees, and He is a cowherd boy who is always giving pleasure to the cows, land and senses.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dāmodara vṛndāvana-go-vatsa-rākhāla</strong><br>
<strong>vatsāsurāntaka hari nija-jana-pāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is so naughty that His mother bound Him around the waist with ropes. Thus He is known as Dāmodar. He is the keeper of Vṛndāvan’s cows and calves, the destroyer of the demon Vatsāsura, He is the remover of all evils and is the protector of His devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>baka-śatru agha-hantā brahma-vimohana</strong><br>
<strong>dhenuka-nāśana kṛṣṇa kālīya-damana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the enemy of the demon Bakāsura, the slayer of Aghāsura, the bewilderer of Lord Brahmā, the destroyer of Dhenukāsura and the subduer of the Kālīya serpent. He is all-attractive and is the colour of a fresh rain cloud.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pītāmbara śikhi-picha-dhārī veṇu-dhara</strong><br>
<strong>bhāṇḍīra-kānana-līla dāvānala-hara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Krishna dresses in yellow silk cloth and wears peacock feathers on His head. He holds a flute, performs Pastimes in the Bhāṇḍīra forest, and He once swallowed a forest fire to save the inhabitants of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>naṭavara-guhāchara śarata-bihārī</strong><br>
<strong>ballabhī-ballabha deva gopī-vastra-hārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna, best of dancers, sometimes wanders in the caves of Govardhān Hill. He enjoys various amorous Pastimes in the autumn season, He is the lover of the young cowherd maidens, the stealer of their garments, and the Supreme Lord of all.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yajña-patnī-goṇ-prati karuṇāra sindhu</strong><br>
<strong>govardhana-dhṛk mādhava vraja-vāsi-bandhu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the ocean of mercy of the wives of the sacrificial <i>brāhmaṇs</i>, He is the holder of Govardhān Hill, the husband of the goddess of fortune, Lakṣmī, and the dearmost friend of the inhabitants of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>indra-darpa-hārī nanda-rakṣitā mukunda</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gopī-ballabha rāsa-krīḍa pūrṇānanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He broke Indra’s pride and protected His father, Nanda Mahārāj. He is the giver of liberation, the lover of the cowherd maidens of Vraja, the enjoyer of the Rāsa Dance and the reservoir of pleasure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhā-vallabha rādhā-mādhava sundara</strong><br>
<strong>lalitā-viśākhā-ādi sakhī-prāṇeśvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the lover of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and the very springtime of Her life. He is the Lord of the lives of all the gopīkās of Vṛndāvan, headed by Lalitā and Viśākhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nava-jaladhara-kānti madana-mohana</strong><br>
<strong>vana-mālī smera-mukha gopī-prāṇadhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna possesses the loveliness of a fresh rain cloud in the Autumn sky. He is the bewilderer of Cupid, always garlanded with fresh forest flowers. His face is like a full-blown lotus flower of sweet smiles and laughter. He is the wealth of the lives of all the young maidens in Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tri-bhaṅgī muralī-dhara jāmuna-nāgara</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kuṇḍa-raṅga-netā rasera sāgara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He possesses a beautiful three-fold bending form and holds a flute known as Muralī. He is the lover of the Yamunā River, the director of all the amorous love sports at Rādhā Kuṇḍa, and He is the ocean of devotional mellows.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chandrāvalī-prāṇanātha kautukābhilāṣī</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-māna-sulampaṭa milana-prayāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the Lord of the life of Chandrāvalī, and He is always desirous of joking and sporting. He is very anxious to taste the rasa of his beloved Rādhikā’s feigned sulking and always endeavours for Their meeting.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mānasa-gaṅgāra dānī prasūna-taskara</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-saha haṭha-kārī vraja-vaneśvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the giver of the Mānasa Gaṅgā Lake to the inhabitants of Vṛndāvan. This thief of flower blossoms acts very outrageously with the maidens of Vṛndāvan. not caring for social restrictions, for He is the Lord of the Vraja Maṇḍal forests.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gokula-sampada gopa-duḥkha-nibāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>durmada-damana bhakta-santāpa-haraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the wealth of Gokul and protects the cowherd men and boys from all miseries. He curbs all foolish pride and removes all distress from His devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sudarśana-mochana śrī-saṅkhachūḍāntaka</strong><br>
<strong>rāmānuja śyāma-chā̐da muralī-bādaka</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna gives the gopīs side-long glances and is the destroyer of Śaṅkhachūḍa. He is the younger brother of Lord Baladeva, He is the beautiful dark moon of Vṛndāvan and the player of the flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopī-gīta-śrotā madhusūdana murāri</strong><br>
<strong>ariṣṭa-ghātaka rādhā-kuṇḍādi-bihārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He hears the songs of the gopīs and is the slayer of the Madhu demon. This enemy of the demon Mura and killer of Ariṣṭāsura enjoys amorous sports and Pastimes at Rādhā Kuṇḍa and other places in Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vyomāntaka padma-netra keśī-nisūdana</strong><br>
<strong>raṅga-krīḍa kaṁsa-hantā mallu-praharaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the destroyer of the demon Vyomāsura. He is lotus-eyed, the killer of the Keśī demon, frolicsome, the slayer of King Kaṁsa and the conquerer of Kaṁsa’s wrestlers, Chāṇūra and Muṣṭika.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vasudeva-suta vṛṣṇi-baṁśa-kīrti-dhvaja</strong><br>
<strong>dīnanātha mathureśa devakī-garbha-ja</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the beloved son of Vasudeva and the emblem of fame for the Vṛṣṇi dynasty. The Lord of the fallen souls, Śrī Krishna, is the Lord of Mathurā and He appears to have taken birth from the womb of Devakī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="20">
<h5>20</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kubjā-kṛpāmoya viṣṇu śauri nārāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dvārakeśa narakaghna śrī-jadunandana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is full of mercy for the hunchbacked Kubjā. He is the maintainer of the entire creation, the son of Vasudeva, the refuge of all souls, the Lord of Dvārakā, the slayer of Narakāsura and the beloved descendent of the Jadu dynasty.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="21">
<h5>21</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rukmiṇī-kānta satyā-pati sura-pāla</strong><br>
<strong>pāṇḍava-bāndhava śiśupālādira kāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the lover of Rukmiṇī, the husband of Satyā, the protector of the godly, the beloved friend and relative of the five Pāṇḍava brothers and the cause of death for Śiśupāla and other demoniac kings.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="22">
<h5>22</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagadīśa janārdana keśavārta-trāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-avatāra-bīja viśvera nidāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Krishna is the Lord of the universe, the maintainer of all living beings and He possesses beautiful hair. He is the deliverer from all misery and the origin of the universe and all incarnations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="23">
<h5>23</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyeśvara yogeśvara brāhma-tejā-dhāra</strong><br>
<strong>sarvātmāra ātmā prabhu prakṛtira pāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the Lord of Māyā; the master of mysticism; the proprietor of the spiritual powers of the brāhmanas; the Lord, master and soul of all souls, and He is the opposite shore of the ocean of material nature.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="24">
<h5>24</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-pāvana jagannātha sarveśvara</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvana-chandra sarva-rasera ākara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Krishna is the purifier of the fallen souls, the Lord of the universe, the Lord of all beings, the moon of Vṛndāvan and the origin of all rasas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="25">
<h5>25</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nagare nagare gorā gāy</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda tachu pāy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya sings these names of Krishna from village to village, and Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod falls at His lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-hari-vasare-hari-kirtana-vidhana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Hari-vāsara-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī hari-vāsare hari-kīrtana-vidhāna</strong><br>
<strong>nṛtya ārambhilā prabhu jagatera prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Once, on the auspicious day on Ekādaśī, in the house of Śrīvās Paṇḍit, Śrīman Mahāprabhu inaugurated congregational nāma saṅkīrtan with great enthusiasm, accompanied by his devotees and associates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>puṇyavanta śrīvāsa-aṅgane śubhārambha</strong><br>
<strong>uṭhilo kīrtana-dhvani gopāla govinda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Surging in divine ecstasy, Śrīman Mahāprabhu, the life and soul of the universe, began to dance beautifully. The devotees surrounded Him being deeply touched and inspired by such an ecstatic moment and they also began to dance and sing the Holy Names like Gopāl, Govinda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mṛdaṅga mandirā bāje śaṅkha karatāla</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana soṅge sob hoilo miśāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The combined sound vibrations of mṛdaṅga, temple bells, karatāla and śaṅkha in harmony with the congregational singing and dancing took everyone to the plane of transcendental delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>brahmāṇḍe uṭhilo dhvani pūriyā ākāśa</strong><br>
<strong>chaudikera amaṅgala jāya sob nāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The holy vibration of saṅkīrtan filled the sky and spread through the ether all over the cosmic universal existence. Thus the atmosphere in all directions became purified and filled with auspiciousness by that divine sound.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chaturdike śrī hari-maṅgala saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>modhye nāche jagannātha miśrera nandana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In all four directions the auspicious chanting of the Lord’s name spread and in the centre of that sound vibration the son of Jagannāth Miśra, Śrīman Mahāprabhu danced.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6-8">
<h5>6-8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sabāra aṅgete śobhe śrī chandana mālā</strong><br>
<strong>ānande nāchoye sobe hoiye vibholā</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ni jānande nāche mahāprabhu viśvambhara</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇera tāli śuni ati manohara</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>bhāvāveśe mālā nāhi rohoye galāya</strong><br>
<strong>chiṇḍiyā poḍoye giyā bhakatera gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everyone was decorated with fragrant sandalwood paste and beautiful garlands. The most wonderful rhythmic sound arose from the dancing footsteps of Śrīman Mahāprabhu which captivated the minds of the devotees. As He continued dancing in His own self-born ecstasy, the swinging flower garlands around His neck began to fall, decorating the earth. The devotees beheld before their eyes, this rare and precious heart captivating, ecstatic dance of the supreme tattva, Śrī Gaurāṅga who is the most worshipable object of even the great personalities such as Lord Shiva, Śuka, Nārada and others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jā̐ra nāmānande śiva vasana nā jāne</strong><br>
<strong>jā̐ra rase nāche śiva se nāche āpane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being totally overwhelmed and lost in the transcendental bliss of taking Mahāprabhu’s Holy Name, Lord Shiva sometimes does not care for his formal appearance or obligation to retain his clothing, which sometimes falls down while he dances in the ecstasy of loving devotion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jā̐ra nāme vālmīki hoilo tapodhana</strong><br>
<strong>jā̐ra nāme ajāmila pāilo mochana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By chanting and meditating upon His Holy Name Śrī Valmīki became a great powerful rishi and Ajāmila attained pure liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jā̐ra nāme śravane saṁsāra-bandha ghuche</strong><br>
<strong>heno prabhu avatari kali-juge nāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As one hears and takes His Holy Name with devotion, embracing its holy potency within the core of their heart, their existence becomes totally purified and they attain deliverance. Such is the Lord, the source of all incarnations, in His dancing form in this age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jā̐ra nāma loi śuka nārada beḍāya</strong><br>
<strong>sahasra-vadana-prabhu jāra guṇa gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The pure devotees Śrī Śukadeva and Devarṣi Nārada are always blissfully engaged in relishing the nectar of His Holy Name and distributing it to suitable jīva souls, wherever they travel. Even Anantadeva describes His unlimited glories with thousands of mouths.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-mahā-prāyaśchitta je prabhura nāma</strong><br>
<strong>se prabhu nāchaye dekhe jata bhāgyavān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Of all penances, the chanting of the Lord’s name is supreme and whoever sees the dancing of the Lord in his form as Śrī Chaitanya is a supremely fortunate soul.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī kṛṣṇa-chaitanya nityānanda-chā̐da jāna</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvana dāsa tuchu pada-juge gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two brothers, Śrī Krishna Chaitanya Mahāprabhu and Nityānanda Prabhu are my beloved masters and my life and soul. Thus Vṛndāvan dās sings this song of Their glorification and devotionally offers it at Their lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shiuddha-bhakata-charana-renu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śuddha-bhakata <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha-bhakata-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-reṇu,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajana-anukūla</strong><br>
<strong>bhakata-sevā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-siddhi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-latikāra mūla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dust from the lotus feet of pure devotees is conducive to devotional service, while service to the Vaiṣṇavas is itself the supreme perfection and the root of the tender creeper of divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhava-tithi,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-jananī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jatane pālana kori</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-basati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;basati boli’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama ādare bori</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I observe with great care the holy days like Ekādaśī and Janmāṣṭamī, for they are the mother of devotion. As my dwelling place I choose with the greatest reverence and love the transcendental abode of Śrī Krishna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura āmāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;je-saba sthāne,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koralo bhramaṇa raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>se-saba sthāna,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heribo āmi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praṇayi-bhakata-saṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All those places where my Lord Gaurasundar travelled for Pastimes I will visit in the company of loving devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mṛdaṅga-bādya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śunite mon,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abasara sadā jāche</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-bihita,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kīrtana śuni’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande hṛdoya nāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind always begs for the opportunity to hear the music of the mṛdaṅga. Upon hearing the kind of kīrtan ordained by Lord Gaurachandra, my heart dances in ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jugala-mūrti,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhiyā mora,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-ānanda hoy</strong><br>
<strong>prasāda-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;korite hoya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakala prapañcha jay</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By beholding the Deity forms of the divine couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Krishna, I feel the greatest joy. By honouring the Lord’s prasādam I conquer over all worldly illusions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je-dina gṛhe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajana dekhi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gṛhete goloka bhāya</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇa-sīdhu,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhiyā gaṅgā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukha nā sīmā pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Goloka Vṛndāvan appears in my home whenever I see the worship and service of Lord Hari going on there. Upon seeing the Ganges, which is a river of nectar emanating from the lotus feet of the Lord, my happiness knows no bounds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tulasī dekhi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;juḍāya prāṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhava-toṣaṇī jāni’</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-priya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāka-sevane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana sārthaka māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sight of the holy Tulasī tree soothes my soul, for I know she gives pleasure to Lord Krishna. By honouring śāk [a green leafy vegetable preparation], a dear favourite of Lord Chaitanya, I consider life worthwhile.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakativinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-bhajane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anukūla pāya jāhā</strong><br>
<strong>prati-divase,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-sukhe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svīkāra karaye tāhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever Bhakti Vinod obtains that is conducive for the service of Śrī Krishna, he accepts every day with the greatest of joy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-chaudda-shiata-sata-shiake-masa-je-phalgun" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="89">
<h2>Śrī Gaura Purṇīmā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verses 89-124 from Śrī Chaitanya Charitāmṛta</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>89</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chaudda-śata sāta-śake māsa je phālgun</strong><br>
<strong>paurṇamāsīra sondhyā-kāle hoile śubha-kṣaṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Thus in the year 1407 of the Śaka Era (A.D. 1486), in the month of Phālguna [March-April], on the evening of the full moon, the desired auspicious moment appeared.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="90">
<h5>90</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>siṁha-rāśi, siṁha-lagna, ucha graha-goṇ</strong><br>
<strong>ṣaḍa-varga, aṣṭa-varga, sarva sulakṣaṇ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to the Jyotir-veda, or Vedic astronomy, when the figure of the lion appears both in the zodiac and the time of birth [<i>lagna</i>], this indicates a very high conjunction of planets, an area under the influence of <i>ṣaḍa-varga</i> and <i>aṣṭa-varga</i>, which are all-auspicious moments.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="91">
<h5>91</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>a-kalaṅka gaurachandra dilā daraśan</strong><br>
<strong>sa-kalaṅka chandre āro kon prayojan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the spotless moon of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu became visible, what would be the need for a moon full of black marks on its body?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="92">
<h5>92</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eto jāni’ rāhu koilo chandrera grahaṇ</strong><br>
<strong>‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘hari’ nāme bhāse tri-bhuvan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Considering this, Rāhu, the black planet, covered the full moon, and immediately vibrations of “Krishna! Krishna! Hari!” inundated the three worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="93">
<h5>93</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya dhvani hoilo sakala bhuvan</strong><br>
<strong>chamatkāra hoiyā lok bhāve mone mon</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All people thus chanted the Holy Names during the lunar eclipse, and their minds were struck with wonder.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="94">
<h5>94</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagat bhoriyā lok bole——‘hari’</strong><br>
<strong>‘hari’ sei-kṣaṇe gaurakṛṣṇa bhūme avatari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the whole world was thus chanting the Holy Names “Hari! Hari!” Krishna in the form of Gaurahari advented Himself on the earth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="95">
<h5>95</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prasanna ho-ilo sob jagatera mon</strong><br>
<strong>‘hari’ boli’ hinduke hāsya karaye javan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The whole world was pleased. While the Hindus chanted the Holy Name of the Lord, the non-Hindus, especially the Muslims, jokingly imitated the words.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="96">
<h5>96</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘hari’ boli’ nārīgoṇ dei hulāhuli</strong><br>
<strong>svarge vādya-nṛtya kore deva kutūhalī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the ladies vibrated the Holy Name and chanted “huli huli huli!!!”, while in the heavenly planets dancing and music were going on and the demigods were very curious.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="97">
<h5>97</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prasanna hoilo doś dik, prasanna nadījol</strong><br>
<strong>sthāvara-jaṅgama hoilo ānande vihval</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this atmosphere, all the ten directions became jubilant, as did the waves of the rivers. Moreover, all beings, moving and nonmoving, were overwhelmed with transcendental bliss.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="98">
<h5>98</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-udayagiri,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrṇachandra gaurahari,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā kori’ ho-ilo udoy</strong><br>
<strong>pāpa-tamaḥ hoilo nāśa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tri-jagatera ullāsa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagabhari’ hari-dhvani hoy</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By His causeless mercy the full moon, Gaurahari, rose in the district of Nadia, which is compared to Udayagiri, where the sun first becomes visible. His rising in the sky dissipated the darkness of sinful life, and thus the three worlds became joyful and chanted the Holy Name of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="99">
<h5>99</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei-kāle nijāloya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uṭhiyā advaita rāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nṛtya kore ānandita-mone</strong><br>
<strong>haridāse loyā saṅge,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;huṅkāra-kīrtana-raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kene nāche, keho nāhi jāne</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At that time Śrī Advaita Āchārya, in His own house at Shantipur, was dancing in a happy mood. Taking Haridās Ṭhākur with Him, they danced and loudly chanted the Holy Names of the Lord. But why they were dancing, no one could understand.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="100">
<h5>100</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dekhi’ uparāga hāsi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śīghra gaṅgā-ghāṭe āsi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande korilo gaṅgā-snāna</strong><br>
<strong>pāyā uparāga-chole,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpanāra mano-bole,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;brāhmaṇere dilo nānā dāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing the lunar eclipse and laughing, both Advaita Āchārya and Haridās Ṭhākur immediately went to the bank of the Ganges to bathe in that sacred river in great jubilation. Taking advantage of the occasion of the lunar eclipse, Advaita Āchārya, in His mind offered various kinds of charity to the <i>brāhmaṇs</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="101">
<h5>101</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagata ānandamoy,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhi’ mone sa-vismoy</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṭhāreṭhore kohe haridāsa</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra aichana raṅga,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora mon parasanna,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhi—kichu kārjye āche bhāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When he saw that the whole world was jubilant, Haridās Ṭhākur, his mind astonished, directly and indirectly expressed himself to Advaita Āchārya: “Your dancing and distributing charity are very pleasing to me. I can understand that there is some special purpose in these actions.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="102">
<h5>102</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āchāryaratna, śrīvāsa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hoilo mone sukhollāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāi’ snāna koilo gaṅgā-jole</strong><br>
<strong>ānande vihvala mon,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kore hari-saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā dāna koilo mano-bole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Āchāryaratna and Śrīvās Ṭhākur were overwhelmed with joy, and immediately they also went to the bank of the Ganges to bathe there. Their minds full of happiness, they chanted the Holy Names of Hari and in their minds also meditated and offered charity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="103">
<h5>103</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei mata bhakta-tati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jā̐ra jei deśe sthiti,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā̐ tāhā̐ pāyā mano-bole</strong><br>
<strong>nāche, kore saṅkīrtana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande vihvala mana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dāna kore grahaṇera chole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this way all the devotees, wherever they were situated, in every city and every land, danced, performed sankīrtan [the congregational chanting of the Lord’s Names], and in their minds offered all benedictions to others, being overwhelmed with joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="104">
<h5>104</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>brāhmaṇa-sajjana-nārī, nānā-dravye thālī bhori’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āilā sobe jautuka lo-iyā</strong><br>
<strong>jena kā̐chā-soṇā-dyuti,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhi’ bālakera mūrti,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśīrvāda kore sukha pāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Many respectful <i>brāhmaṇ</i> gentlemen and ladies, carrying plates filled with various gifts, came with their presentations. Seeing the newborn child, whose form resembled shining gold, all of them with happiness offered their blessings.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="105">
<h5>105</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sāvitrī, gaurī, sarasvatī, śachī, rambhā, arundhatī,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āro jata deva-nārīgoṇ</strong><br>
<strong>nānā-dravye pātra bhori’, brāhmaṇīra veśa dhori’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsi’ sobe koren daraśan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sāvitrī, Gaurī, Śachī, Rambhā, Arundhatī and all the celestial ladies also came there dressed as the wives of <i>brāhmaṇs</i>, and brought with them many varieties of gifts for the child.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="106">
<h5>106</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>antarīkṣe deva-goṇ, gandharva, siddha, chāraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;stuti-nṛtya kore vādya-gīta</strong><br>
<strong>nartaka, vādaka, bhāṭa,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navadvīpe jāra nāṭa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sobe āsi’ nāche pāyā prīta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the inhabitants of the heavenly planets, including the Gandharvas, Siddhas, the residents of Chāraṇaloka, offered their prayers, sang and danced, accompanied by the sound of musical instruments and the beating of drums. Also, in the holy town of Navadwīp, dancers, musicians, and poets gathered together, and began to celebrate the Lord’s appearance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="107">
<h5>107</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kebā āse kebā jāya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kebā nāche kebā gāya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sambhālite nāre kāra bolo</strong><br>
<strong>khaṇḍileka duḥkha-śoka, pramoda-pūrita loka,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;miśra hoilā ānande vihvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No one could understand who was coming and who was going, who was dancing and who was singing. Nor could they understand each other’s language.
