He widely made available the fifth end of life which had never been given before in this world, the divine love for Krishna found in the Lord’s original divine abode of Goloka Vṛndāvan. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patitādhama-dīna-dayādra-hṛdaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>hṛdayāśrita-yāchaka-veśa-kṛtam</strong><br>
<strong>kṛta-veṣa-yati-śrita-nīla-giriṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the great benefactor whose heart is melted with compassion for the fallen and out of that feeling in His heart He accepted the dress of a beggar (the renounced order of life, <i>sannyās</i>). After accepting this dress He took shelter in Nīlāchala (Jagannāth Purī). Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti-kīrtita-pūruṣa-rukṣma-ruchiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>ruchi-rāga-niṣevaṇa-dāna-param</strong><br>
<strong>paramārtha-purāṇa-vigīta-hariṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the resplendent golden Personality gloried by the Scriptures and saints; He is the greatest distributor of spontaneous loving divine service (pure transcendental attachment and service to the Lord free from all mundanity). He is the Lord whose glories are sung throughout the Scriptures describing transcendental knowledge (<i>Purāṇas</i>), (or, He is the Lord who takes away all apparent discord found within the <i>Purāṇas’</i> revelation of transcendental knowledge.) Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bahu-nartana-kīrtana-matta-karam</strong><br>
<strong>karatāla-mṛdaṅga-vibhaṅga-param!</strong><br>
<strong>paramādara-pāmara-śāntipurīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He performed greatly maddened dancing and chanting amidst the wonderful sounds of the <i>karatāla</i> and <i>mṛdaṅga</i> as He was shown great affection and honour by the fallen souls. He is the grand abode of divine peace of relief for one and all. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Compositions of Śrīla Govinda Mahārāj</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-svarupa-raya-rupa-jiva-bhava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Praṇām Mantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>to Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-svarūpa-rāya-rūpa jīva-bhāva-sambharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>varṇa-dharma-nirviśeṣa-sarvaloka-nistaram</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-sarasvatī-priyañ cha bhakti-sundarāśrayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdharaṁ namāmi bhakti-rakṣakaṁ jagad-gurum</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>sindhu-chandra-parvatendu-śāka-janma-līlanam</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha-dīpta-rāga-bhakti-gauravānuśīlanam</strong><br>
<strong>bindu-chandra-ratna-soma-śāka-lochanāntaram</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdharaṁ namāmi bhakti-rakṣakaṁ jagad-gurum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my humble obeisances unto the Guru of the entire world, Oṁ Viṣṇupād Śrī Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who carries the conception of Śrī Swarūp Damodār, Śrī Rāmānanda Rāya, Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī and Śrī Jīva Goswāmī; who delivers everyone irrespective of caste or creed; who is the most beloved of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Goswāmī Prabhupād; and who is the abode of Transcendental Loving Service [the only shelter of Śrī Bhakti Sundar Govinda].<br>
I offer my humble obeisances unto the Guru of the entire world, Oṁ Viṣṇupād Śrī Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who revealed the Pastimes of his Holy Advent in the world in the year 1817 Saka era [1895 A.D.]; who constantly practices the wealth of Pure Loving Devotion in Divine Consorthood; and who disappeared from our eyes in the year 1910 Saka era [1988 A.D.].</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>kanaka suruchi rāngaṁ sundaraṁ saumya-mūrtiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vibudha-kula-vareṇyaṁ śrī-guruṁ siddhi-pūrtim</strong><br>
<strong>taruṇa-tapana-vāsaṁ bhaktidañ chid-vilāsaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja bhaja tu mano re śrīdharaṁ śambidhānam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my dear mind! Please engage yourself eternally in the worship of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who has manifested his form as Śrī Gurudeva; who is the giver of all perfection; whose charming golden form is beautifully reposed with sweetness and tranquility, dressed in radiant saffron robes, he is rich with perfect devotional conceptions, highly learned and pre-eminent among the followers of Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-naumi-shri-gurupadabjam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Praṇati Daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Ten Prayers in glorification of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>naumi śrī-gurupādābjaṁ, yatirājeśvareśvaram</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhaktirakṣakam śrīla-śrīdhara-svāminaṁ sadā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I eternally offer my humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of my Most Worshipful Divine Master, the Supreme Emperor of the Sannyāsī Kings—Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Swāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2-4">
<h5>2-4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sudīrghonnata-dīptāngaṁ, supībya-vapuṣaṁ param</strong><br>
<strong>tridaṇḍa-tulasīmālā-, gopīchandana-bhūṣitam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>achintya-pratibhā-snigdhaṁ, divyajñāna-prabhākaram</strong><br>
<strong>vedādi-sarvaśāstrānāṁ, sāmañjasya-vidhāyakam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>gauḍīyāchārya-ratnānām, ujjvalaṁ ratna-kaustubham</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-chaitanya-mahāpremon-, mattālīnāṁ śiromaṇim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He possesses a lofty, dignified, divinely effulgent, incomparable holy form, so charming to behold. Adorned with Gopīchandan and a necklace of Tulasī beads, he holds the triple staff of the Vaiṣṇava ascetics.<br>
Although he possesses the qualification of inconceivable potency, he is full of the utmost affection. All the ten directions are illuminated by the effulgence of his supramundane unalloyed perception. He is the dispenser of the genuine proper harmonious adjustment of all the scriptures—the Vedas, the Vedānta, the Upaniṣads, the Purāṇas, etc. headed by the Śrīmad-Bhāgavata-Purāṇa (<i>Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam</i>) which holds equal importance with the Vedas.<br>
In the necklace of the jewels of Āchāryas in the Śrī Gauḍīya Sampradāya he shines resplendently like the brilliant Kaustubha gem, and he is radiant in his glory as the Crown-jewel of the bumblebee-devotees who are mad in the Supreme Lord Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu’s love supreme. I eternally offer my obeisances unto him, my Most Worshipful Divine Master.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5-6">
<h5>5-6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāyatry-artha-vinirjyāsaṁ, gītā-gūḍhārtha-gauravam</strong><br>
<strong>stotraratnādi-samṛddhaṁ, prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>apūrvagrantha-sambhāraṁ, bhaktānāṁ hṛd-rasāyanam</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpayā yena dattaṁ taṁ, naumi kāruṇya-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By his grace, he brought to full bloom the deepest underlying purport of Gāyatrī, the Mother of the Vedas; by his grace he opened the hidden treasure-house of the glorious internal purport of Śrīmad Bhagavad-gītā—and he distributed these gifts to one and all, even the lowest of the low. He revealed the supreme holy book (<i>Śrī Prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam</i>, which is a treasure of many types of precious gems of holy stanzas (the wealth of holy stanzas compiled from Stotra-ratna by Śrī Yamunāchārya and many other valuable sources) sung by the Lord and His devotees; he revealed an unprecedented collection of holy books (<i>Search for Śrī Krishna—Reality the Beautiful</i>, <i>Śrī Guru and His Grace</i>, <i>The Golden Volcano of Divine Love</i>, etc.) that are the very life-nectar for the devotee’s hearts and spiritual senses - he gave these gifts to the world. I eternally offer my obeisances unto him, my Most Worshipful Divine Master, the beautiful Deity of mercy incarnate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṅkīrtana-mahārāsarasabdheś chandramānibham</strong><br>
<strong>saṁbhāti vitaran viśve gaura-kṛṣṇaṁ gaṇaiḥ saha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He revels in his full-blown splendour as along with and through his intimate servitors he distributes in the entire universe the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Gaura-Krishna, the moon risen from the nectar ocean of the Grand Dance of the congregational chanting of Śrī Krishna’s holy name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8-10">
<h5>8-10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhāmani śrī-navadvīpe, guptagovardhane śubhe</strong><br>
<strong>viśvaviśruta-chaitanya-, sārasvata-maṭhottamam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>sthāpayitvā gurūn gaura-, rādhā-govindavigrahān</strong><br>
<strong>prakāśayati chātmānaṁ, sevā-saṁsiddhi-vigrahaḥ</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>gaura-śrīrūpa-siddhānta-, divya-dhārādharaṁ gurum</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣakaṁ devaṁ, śrīdharaṁ praṇamāmy aham</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām, which is nondifferent from the holy abode of Śrī Vṛndāvan, His Divine Grace established the world-renowned Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh at Śrī Koladwīp, the selfsame ‘Hidden Govardhan Hill’ (Gupta-Govardhan), which is the sacred place where all offenses are absolved (Aparādha-bhañjana-pāṭ). There, he revealed the beautiful devotional service of the Worshipful Deities Śrī Śrī Guru-Gaurāṅga-Gāndharvā Govindasundar, divinely manifesting his very self as the Deity of service in perfection. Eternally do I offer my obeisances unto the holy lotus feet of my Divine Master Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj who carries the divine current of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī coming in the line of Śrī Rūpa, the object of Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu’s divine affection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>śraddhayā yaḥ paṭhen nityaṁ, praṇati-daśakam mudā</strong><br>
<strong>viśate rāgamārgeṣu, tasya bhakta-prasādataḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who with pure faith happily sings this Praṇatidaśakam every day, gains the qualification to serve the Supreme Lord on the path of spontaneous love (Rāgamarga), by the grace of the associate-servitors of that Śrīla Gurudeva.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-samudra-sambhava-gabhi-surabhi-apana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa Māhātmya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Glories of Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa</h3>
<h3>Śrī Girirāj-khaṇḍam – Śrī Garga-saṁhitā</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>atha govardhane ramye surabhi gauḥ samudrajā</strong><br>
<strong>snāpayā māsa gopeśaṁ dugdha dhārābhi rātnanaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>surabhe dugdha dhārābhi govinda snānato nṛpa</strong><br>
<strong>jāto govinda kuṇḍohadrau mahāpāpaharaḥ paraḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kadā chittasmin dugdhasya svādutvaṁ prati padyate</strong><br>
<strong>tatra snātvā naraḥ sākṣād govinda padamāpnuyāt</strong><br>
<strong>govinda kuṇḍe viśade yaḥ snāti kṛṣṇa-mānasaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>prāpnoti kṛṣṇa-sārūpyaṁ maithilendra na saṁśayaḥ</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse-subtitle">Bengali verse illumination<br>
by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>samudra-sambhavā gābhī surabhī āpana</strong><br>
<strong>dugdhe abhiṣeka koilo vrajendra-nandana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Surabhī, the divine cow who appeared from churning the milk ocean, lovingly bathed Vrajendra Nandan Krishna with her own milk.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei dugdhe pūrṇa kuṇḍa ‘śrī govinda’ nāma</strong><br>
<strong>ramya giri govardhane śobhe abhirāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The pond filled with that milk, “Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa,” graces beautiful Govardhan with exquisite beauty.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuṇḍavāri mahāpāpahārī se chinmoya</strong><br>
<strong>snāne pāne bhavabhaya tritāpa nāśaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The transcendental water of this Kuṇḍa can banish even the greatest sins. The threefold miseries—<i>ādhyātmic</i>, <i>ādhidaivic</i> and <i>ādhibhautic</i>—and all fears of this material world are destroyed by bathing in or drinking this water.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bahu-bhāgye keho sei dugdhāsvāda pāya</strong><br>
<strong>chidānanda-deho labhi kṛṣṇa-loke jāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes, by great fortune, someone will get to taste the water of Govinda Kuṇḍa as that milk, attain a transcendental form, and go to Krishna-<i>loka</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī kṛṣṇa-mānase jebā kore hethā snāna</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-govardhane rādhā-kṛṣṇa-sevā pāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Anyone who bathes in Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa, desiring to serve Krishna, attains divine service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Krishna in Gupta Govardhan (hidden Govardhan).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-devadidevam-ahibhushanam-indukashiam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Gaṅgadhara Sadāśiva Praṇām-Mantram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>devādidevam-ahibhūṣaṇam-indukāśaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>panchānanaṁ paśupatiṁ varadaṁ prasannam</strong><br>
<strong>gaṅgādharaṁ praṇatapālakamāśutoṣaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande sadāśiva-haripriya-chandramaulim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The chief of the Demigods, whose body is decorated by snakes, whose body shines like the moon, who has five heads, who carries his weapon known as Paśupat, who gives boons, who is of satisfied nature, who holds the Gaṅgā on his head, who maintains those who give obeisance to him, who is easily pleased — I offer my obeisances to Sadā-śiva who is dear to Hari, and whose crown is the moon.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhagyadhishia-tvadiyo-vimala-sukhamayah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-Praśastiḥ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāgyādhīśa! tvadīyo vimala-sukhamayaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;samprakāśastu-nityo</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍaṁ rāḍhaṁ tathedaṁ tribhūvanam-akhilaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhanya-dhanyan chakāra</strong><br>
<strong>khaṇḍe kāle dṛśāṁ no gagaṇa-rasamitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrayitvā budhānā–</strong><br>
<strong>mānandaṁ bardhayan vai sva-parijana-gaṇair-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhāmani tvaṁ vibhāsi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Fortune-maker! Divine Master! Your unalloyed blissful appearance is eternal. The people of Gauḍa, Rāḍha and all the universe speak of your virtuous and auspicious appearance. Time appears to stand still at your appearance, as the sky is filled with your wonderful qualities. Your manifestation gives joy to all the saintly persons and that joy increases unlimitedly due to the illumination of the Holy Dhām by you, along with your divine associates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>devādyāste ‘khila guṇa-gaṇān-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;naiva gātuṁ samarthāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kvāhaṁ jīvo ’tiśaya-patito</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;manda-bhāgyo ’ti kṣudraḥ</strong><br>
<strong>bho ārādhya! stavana-viṣaye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kintu dīnādhamasya</strong><br>
<strong>pratyāśā tat-sukaruṇatayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vīrachandrābhidastvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even gods and goddesses are unable to sing your praiseworthy qualities and attributes. Where am I!? A very fallen soul, an unfortunate and tiny living entity. O adorable, venerable Gurudeva! Your blessings and mercy can raise me to sing with expectation, as you are non-different from Birachandra Prabhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dṛṣṭvā viśvasya jīvān khalu hari-vimukhān</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaura-devo dayāyā</strong><br>
<strong>rūpaṁ gauḍe bhavastaṁ parama-karuṇayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāhinod- dīna bandho!</strong><br>
<strong>etaj jñātvā prakāśāt sudina-samudayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smāramāśāḥ saharṣā</strong><br>
<strong>jayante chaiva māyā-nigaḍa-nikara-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁmochane ’smakamaddhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To observe the people of the universe that are averse to Śrī Hari, the Lord, Śrī Gaurahari mercifully sent you to Śrī Rūpa’s own Gauḍa <i>deśa</i> as their protector and guardian. By the remembrance of your auspicious appearance day we can cut the bondage of Māyā, the nescience of material ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadvad-bhānuḥ kiraṇa-nikarair-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāsayan viśvam etan</strong><br>
<strong>nāśaṁ kṛtvā nikhila-tamasāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tejasā saṁvibhāti</strong><br>
<strong>kṛtvā nāśaṁ prakṛti-tamasāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;satya-sūryaṁ prakāśya</strong><br>
<strong>divya-jñānair-hari-guṇa-gaṇais</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tvan cha tadvad-vibhāsi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the sun eradicates all the darkness of the universe by spreading its rays and shining forth, similarly you are shining and eradicating the darkness of Māyā as the true sun of divine knowledge, full of the all-powerful qualities of Śrī Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>duḥkhaiḥ pūrṇaṁ vibudha-hṛdayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāla-dharmāch cha dṛṣṭvā</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvādān kalijakumatān</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duṣkṛtān śāsitun cha</strong><br>
<strong>deśe deśe bhramasi vitaran</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaura-vāṇīn cha nāma</strong><br>
<strong>dhṛtvā deva! tribhuvana-jayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vajra-kalpaṁ tridaṇḍam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my Lord! Taking the thunder-like mendicant’s (<i>sannyasī</i>’s) triple staff (<i>tridaṇḍa</i>) and conquering the universe, you travelled from place to place distributing the teachings of Gaurāṅga ( Gaura-vāṇī ) to please the saddened hearts of the intelligentsia and to destroy the impersonalist conceptions and demoniac ideas that have grown by the influence of the age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varṣāyāṁ vai sajala-jalado</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vādayan mandrabheriṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yadyad viśve bhramati bahudhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāridhārān cha varṣan</strong><br>
<strong>tadvad-bhūmau bhramasi sagaṇair-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghoṣayan gaura-gāthā</strong><br>
<strong>nityaṁ divyāmṛta-sukaruṇāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tvaṁ hi deva! pravarṣan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the rainy season, the clouds filled with water move through the sky with a thunderous sound and shower their rains. Similarly you shower your merciful divine rains as you travel throughout this universe. Your travelling throughout the earth, along with your associates, is like the rumble of thunder as you distribute the glories of Lord Gaurāṅga. Oh my Lord, in this way you eternally shower supremely merciful nectar over all.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-chaitanya-vilāsa-dhāmani</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navadvīpāśrame sundare</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-vidhos-tathā vraja yunoḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevā-sudhā-sampadam</strong><br>
<strong>tadvan gāṅga-taṭe dayāmaya-vibho!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadhūn samāhlā-dayan</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpānuga-sampradāya-vibhavānud</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāsayan bhāsase</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At Nabadwīp, the manifestation place of Śrī Chaitanya, on the banks of the Ganges River, you built a beautiful hermitage filled with the wealth of the conception of Śrī Gaurachandra, the worship of the youthful son of Vraja, Śrī Govindasundar. Oh merciful Lord! Here you adore the saintly devotees and give them joy by perfectly explaining the supreme teachings of Śrī Rūpa, appearing personally to give this merciful current of divinity as his full representative.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chārvākānta-kṛtāntako ’khila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guruḥ pāṣaṇḍa-śailāśanir</strong><br>
<strong>bauddha-dhvānta-matānta-dāyaka</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mahāmārtaṇḍa-chūḍāmaṇiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvāda-mahāvivarta-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gahaṇāj-jīvān samuddhā-vayan</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurendu-jaya-dhvajo vijayate</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svāmin bhavān nityaśaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the divine master and protector of all by destroying the philosophy of Charvak Rishi; you are a stone-like weapon to the miscreants of this world; you are like the bright sun to the darkness of Buddhism and you protect the souls of this world from the horrible hole of impersonalist thought (<i>māyāvāda</i>); and you fly the victory flag of Śrī Gaurachandra or you are the victory flag for Śrī Gaurendu (the poet).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-sarasvatī-dhunidhara!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-bhakti-saṁrak-ṣaka!</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-siddhānta-sarasvatī priyavara!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nyāsīśvara! śrī-guro!</strong><br>
<strong>devādyeha! bhavat-śubhodaya-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dine saṁprārthaye ’haṁ vibho!</strong><br>
<strong>pādābje khalu nitya-bhṛtya iti</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;me kāruṇyam-ātan-vatām</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Oh my Lord! Oh divine guardian of the current of the teachings of Śrī Gaurāṅga coming through Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur; who is the protector of pure devotion; who is the dearmost of Śrī Saraswatī Ṭhākur; you are the senior-most <i>sannyasī</i>; you are the great divine master. Oh my Lord! Today being your advent day this eternal servant of your lotus feet prays to always bathe in the shower of your mercy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nava-nalina-ganjitam-kusuma-dala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Praṇati-Pañchakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>nava-nalina-gañjitaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kusuma-dala-maṇḍitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-yugam-abhaya-varadānam</strong><br>
<strong>kanaka-ghana-ninditaṁ vapura-mara-vanditaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;naṭana-gati-gaurava-nidānam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhaje śrīdhara-svāminam-aniśam</strong><br>
<strong>śarada-vidhu-nindi-mukha- padma-mati-śobhanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-tilaka-charchita-subhālam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>gamana-jita-vāraṇaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śamana-bhaya-tāraṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prakṛti-ruchi-nindi-kṛta-veśam</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prakṛti-ruchi-nambhrama-suveśam</strong><br>
<strong>phulla-kara-pallavaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-jana-vallabhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuṅga-pṛthulaṁ hṛdaya-deśam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>gaura-puraṭa-saurabhaṁ kṛṣṇa-puraṭa-gauravaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vibudha-kula-padma-naliśam</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-kula-daṇḍanaṁ bhaṇḍa-mata-khaṇḍanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-kula-nandana-yatīśam</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>tribhuvana-nivāsine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇam-upadeśinaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asura-kula-dāmbhika-kṛtāntam</strong><br>
<strong>naumi budha-nāyakaṁ parama-gati-dāyakaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mama śaraṇa-maraṇa-gaṇa-chintyam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1-2">
<h5>1-2</h5>
<p class="translation">His two feet resemble fresh petals of a lotus flower and distribute fearless blessings; his divine glowing form puts to shame the glow of gold, and is glorified for its beautiful dancing by all the demigods; his lotus face is so charming it puts to shame the charm of the autumnal moon; his broad forehead is decorated with <i>gopī-chandan tilak</i>; I adore that Śrīdhara-deva Swāmī always.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3-4">
<h5>3-4</h5>
<p class="translation">He can put to an end our wandering in this material world; he can protect us from fear and death; his beautiful form dressed in saffron robes puts to shame the attraction and taste for mundane beauty; his hands like fresh blossoms distribute bliss; he is mutually beloved of the devotees; his heart is broad and strong; he preaches the glories of Śrī Govardhan; he spreads the sweet aroma of the abode of Śrī Krishna; he punishes the deceivers and refutes their faulty conceptions thereby giving pleasure to the devotees and revealing himself as a pre-eminent <i>sannyāsi</i> preacher. I always adore that Śrīdhara-deva Swāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="translation">He is a death-sentence to arrogant, haughty demons; he is the saviour of everyone in all the three worlds for he distributes the teachings of Śrī Krishna; he is pre-eminent amongst the learned, the most supreme merciful shelter, I adore that Śrīdhara-deva until my death.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-maha-bhaya-shiankula-e-bhava-tarangini" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Māyā Duratyayā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahā-bhaya śaṅkula&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e bhava taraṅginī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama-maraṇa mālā-dale</strong><br>
<strong>niśi-diśi gatāgati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara ta̐hi jīva-kula</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mohita mahāmāyā bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Filled with tremendous trepidation (<i>mahā-bhaya</i>), the <i>jīva</i>-souls are carried away by the waves of the ocean of material existence along the chain of birth and death, perpetually coming and going in all directions throughout the darkness of the confines of the illusory environment, captivated by the power of Mahāmāyā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karabi ki mana abhāgiyā—</strong><br>
<strong>bhuvana-mohana-māyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane re utarabi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhibi kemane kānāiyā?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Mind! What can you do about my great misfortune? Mahāmāyā bewilders the whole universe, how will you ever overcome her? How will you attain Kānāi-lāl — the youthful Krishna of Goloka Vṛndāvan?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-rūpevitāri’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tri-guṇa-jāle phā̐da rachi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ta̐hi māhe gaṇikā pratiṣṭhā—</strong><br>
<strong>kanaka varāṅganā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dāniyā āmiṣa sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sa̐pitechhe kāla-kare dhṛṣṭā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In your own form you are trying to cross over the net of the three modes of material nature but in doing so they have bound you in a noose. You have treated Māyā like a prostitute. She has been feeding you <i>kanak</i>, <i>kāminī</i>, and <i>pratiṣṭha</i> just like meat and you have become immersed in the darkness of arrogance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mana majhu vāta avadhāna</strong><br>
<strong>soi dayālu prabhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa saroje mana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhana-tanu, sabahu̐ dāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my mind, your attention is like the wind. Your real wealth will be found at the lotus feet of the all-merciful Lord, so give yourself completely there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐kara māyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tachhu pade paraṇami</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;leha śaraṇa hoi dīna</strong><br>
<strong>svapaneha nā bhāviha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ka charaṇa vinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharama yukati samīchīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Offer yourself to the lotus feet of the Lord who is the owner and controller of Māyā, taking shelter there in all humility. Do not even dream that without His lotus feet you can properly adjust with the environment or practice spiritual life (<i>dhārma</i>).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ami-guru-dasa-nahi-anya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Svarūpodbodhana Awakening the True Self <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>Originally published in Sri Gaudiya Darsan:</h4>
<h4>Volume 1, Issue 4, Tuesday, 15 November 1955</h4>
<h4>[When he was 27 years old.]</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi guru-dāsa—nahi anya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am a servant of Guru—nothing else.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi kariba bhramana chaudda-bhuvana</strong><br>
<strong>svarūpe sabāya kari’ udbodhana</strong><br>
<strong>hāte la’ye yāba premera niśāna</strong><br>
<strong>dharāba svarūpa chihna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will travel throughout the fourteen worlds awakening everyone to their true self. I will go with the banner of divine love in hand and cause everyone to recognise the sign of the true self.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi chhāḍāba sakale sarva-dharma</strong><br>
<strong>kariba chūrṇa jñāna o karma</strong><br>
<strong>rachiba viśāla bhakati-harmya</strong><br>
<strong>guru-dāsa nahi anya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will induce everyone to abandon all religions. I will demolish the pursuits of liberation and worldly enjoyment. I will construct a grand palace of devotion. I am a servant of Guru—nothing else.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi bhāṅgiba chhanda lāgāba dhanda</strong><br>
<strong>vāchālera mukha kariba banda</strong><br>
<strong>mṛtyure dhari dāniba śanda</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi kapaṭatā dainya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will break the customs, bemuse everyone, shut the mouths of the garrulous, hold back death, and distribute good fortune to all. I will do so humbly, without deceit.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei mahābhāratera mahān parva</strong><br>
<strong>yāhāra prakāśe hayechhe kharva</strong><br>
<strong>dekhāba tā̐hāra asīma garva</strong><br>
<strong>kaitave kari khinna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will show the unlimited glory of He whose manifestation brought the great portion of the Mahābhārata to an end, and efface all duplicity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi bājāba jagate vijaya-ḍaṅkā</strong><br>
<strong>ghuchāba sakala dvandva-śaṅkā</strong><br>
<strong>bahāba viśve bhakati-gaṅgā</strong><br>
<strong>tuṣi’ hari labhi puṇya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will sound the kettle-drums of victory throughout the world, dispel all quarrel and fear, and cause the Ganges of pure devotion to flow throughout the universe. Satisfying the Lord in this way, I will attain good fortune.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi āryānārya mlechchha sabāya</strong><br>
<strong>lāgāiba ba’le kṛṣṇa-sevāya</strong><br>
<strong>chaḍāiba sabe golokera nā’ya</strong><br>
<strong>pṛthvī kariba śūṇya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will forcibly engage all—the noble, the ignoble, and the barbarous—in Kṛṣṇa’s service. I will board them all in the boat to Goloka and empty the earth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi chālāba sakale guru-pada bale</strong><br>
<strong>vādāma tuliba hari-bola bale</strong><br>
<strong>mahāmāyā-vinī chhalanā chhalile</strong><br>
<strong>kariba chinna-bhinna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will guide them all by the power of my Guru’s feet. I will hoist the boat’s sails by the power of chanting ‘Hari’. If Mahāmāyā presents any illusion, I shall cut it asunder.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi chinmaya-dhāme chālāiba tarī</strong><br>
<strong>chatur-bhuja habe yata nara-nārī</strong><br>
<strong>sthāvara jaṅgama sabe labhi hari</strong><br>
<strong>habe deva deva mānya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will drive the boat to the spiritual world, and all the men and women will become four-armed. All moving and inert beings will attain Hari and become worshippable to the demigods.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sabe eka parichaye dibe parichaya</strong><br>
<strong>labhibe svarūpa amṛta-maya</strong><br>
<strong>pūjibe śrī-hari dibe jaya jaya</strong><br>
<strong>nehāri’ ha-iba dhanya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everyone will identify with the same identity, realise their immortal self, worship Śrī Hari, and call out, ‘Jay! Jay!’ Seeing this, I will be satisfied.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>āmi guru-dāsa nahi anya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am a servant of Guru—nothing else.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="first-lines" id="first-lines">
<h1>Index</h1>
<h2 class="first-line-group">A</h2>

<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-anadi-karama-phole">anādi karama-phole<span class="page-num"> — 224</span></a></p>

<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-nite-yasmin-nishiante-nayana-jala-bharaih">nīte yasmin niśānte nayana-jala-bharaiḥ<span class="page-num"> — 311</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">O</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-ohe-harinam-tava-mahima-apara">ohe harinām, tava mahimā apāra<span class="page-num"> — 166</span></a></p>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-vaishnava-thakura-dayara-sagara">(ohe) vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, dayāra sāgara<span class="page-num"> — 84</span></a></p>
<h2 class="first-line-group">P</h2>
<p class="first-line" data-translated="1"><a href="#song-parama-karuna-pahu-dui-jana">parama karuṇa, pahu̐ dūi jana<span class="page-num"> — 132</span></a></p>
